export const chronopediaEventsTable = pgTable("chronopedia_events", {

  slotId: integer("slot_id")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => chronopediaSlotsTable.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),

    .notNull()
    .references(() => citizensTable.id),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  description: text("description").notNull(),
  status: text("status").notNull().default("approved"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
});

// --- FILE: lib/db/src/schema/chronopediaSlots.ts ---
import { pgTable, serial, text, doublePrecision, timestamp, uniqueIndex } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

export const chronopediaSlotsTable = pgTable(
  "chronopedia_slots",
  {
    id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
    timeMarker: text("time_marker").notNull(),
    placeId: text("place_id").notNull(),
    geoLat: doublePrecision("geo_lat").notNull(),
    geoLng: doublePrecision("geo_lng").notNull(),
    locationName: text("location_name").notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex("chronopedia_unique_time_space").on(t.timeMarker, t.placeId),
  ]
);

export type ChronopediaSlot = typeof chronopediaSlotsTable.$inferSelect;

// --- FILE: lib/db/src/schema/citizens.ts ---
import { pgTable, text, serial, integer, timestamp, real } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
// balance_minutes, total_given, total_received are `real` to support fractional Darmins (tax 2% = 0.02 Д on 1 Д)
import { createInsertSchema } from "drizzle-zod";
import { z } from "zod/v4";

export const citizensTable = pgTable("citizens", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  phone: text("phone").notNull().unique(),
  telegramId: text("telegram_id").unique(),
  walletAddress: text("wallet_address").unique(),
  walletNonce: text("wallet_nonce"),
  walletVerified: integer("wallet_verified").notNull().default(0),
  balanceMinutes: real("balance_minutes").notNull().default(1), // 1 родовая минута
  totalGiven: real("total_given").notNull().default(0),
  totalReceived: real("total_received").notNull().default(0),
  status: text("status").notNull().default("active"),
  meritScore: real("merit_score").notNull().default(0),
  rank: text("rank").notNull().default("Гражданин"),
  lastActivity: timestamp("last_activity", { withTimezone: true }),
  tosAcceptedAt: timestamp("tos_accepted_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  ageVerified: integer("age_verified").notNull().default(0),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),

  // ── ICAO Doc 9303 Passport fields ──────────────────────────────────────────
  citizenNumber: text("citizen_number").unique(), // 000000001 … 999999999
  surname: text("surname"),
  givenNames: text("given_names"),
  dateOfBirth: text("date_of_birth"), // YYYY-MM-DD
  photoUrl: text("photo_url"),
  idCardUrl: text("id_card_url"), // filled when issued
  // 'pending' → 'issued' | 'rejected'. NULL = legacy citizen (treated as issued)
  registrationStatus: text("registration_status"),
  availableAt: timestamp("available_at", { withTimezone: true }), // NOW + 1h

  // ── Registration contact & compliance fields ───────────────────────────────
  email: text("email"),                          // contact email (optional)
  seedThought: text("seed_thought"),             // kept for future use, not collected at registration
  privacyAgreementAccepted: integer("privacy_agreement_accepted").notNull().default(0),
  privacyAgreementDate: timestamp("privacy_agreement_date", { withTimezone: true }),
  signatureBase64: text("signature_base64"),

  // ── Government role ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  govRole: text("gov_role").notNull().default("citizen"),
});

export const insertCitizenSchema = createInsertSchema(citizensTable).omit({
  id: true,
  balanceMinutes: true,
  totalGiven: true,
  totalReceived: true,
  meritScore: true,
  rank: true,
  lastActivity: true,
  createdAt: true,
  walletAddress: true,
});
export type InsertCitizen = z.infer<typeof insertCitizenSchema>;
export type Citizen = typeof citizensTable.$inferSelect;

// --- FILE: lib/db/src/schema/deals.ts ---
import { pgTable, text, serial, integer, timestamp } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

// TiMETR Escrow — task-based citizen-to-citizen time transfer
// Flow: Sender creates deal → minutes locked → Receiver accepts + submits proof →
//       Sender approves → minutes released to Receiver (or declined → returned to Sender)
export const dealsTable = pgTable("deals", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  senderId: integer("sender_id").notNull(),
  receiverId: integer("receiver_id"),            // null = open marketplace deal
  amount: integer("amount").notNull(),           // TiMETR minutes locked in escrow
  description: text("description").notNull(),
  // Statuses: open → accepted → proof_submitted → completed | declined | disputed
  //           open → cancelled (sender cancels before accepted)
  status: text("status").notNull().default("open"),
  proofUrl: text("proof_url"),                   // receiver-supplied link to evidence
  proofText: text("proof_text"),                 // receiver-supplied text description
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
});

export type Deal = typeof dealsTable.$inferSelect;
export type InsertDeal = typeof dealsTable.$inferInsert;

// --- FILE: lib/db/src/schema/tvCapsules.ts ---
import { pgTable, serial, integer, text, boolean, timestamp } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { createInsertSchema } from "drizzle-zod";
import { z } from "zod/v4";

// ─── Three independent capsule dimensions ────────────────────────────────────
// A. broadcastPolicy : echo | noise | private
//    echo    = Retro-капсула врывается в LIVE (доп. оплата)
//    noise   = Бесплатно, рандомный пул пустых минут
//    private = Только по прямой ссылке, никогда в эфире
// B. contentState    : filled | empty
//    filled  = Seed + Manifesto → рендер
//    empty   = Silent cyber-minimalist timer overlay (доказательство владения)
// C. hasSovereignLink: bool
//    true    = Уникальный постоянный URL timenation.tv/moment/YYYY-MM-DD-HHmm
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const tvCapsulesTable = pgTable("tv_capsules", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  slotId: integer("slot_id").notNull().unique(),
  citizenId: integer("citizen_id").notNull(),
  seedText: text("seed_text"), // ≤500 chars, nullable for empty capsules
  manifestoText: text("manifesto_text"), // LLM-expanded or raw
  mode: text("mode").notNull().default("a"), // a=AI-Packaging | b=Conscious Citizen
  renderPath: text("render_path").notNull().default("light"), // light | heavy
  broadcastPolicy: text("broadcast_policy").notNull().default("noise"), // echo | noise | private
  contentState: text("content_state").notNull().default("filled"), // filled | empty
  hasSovereignLink: boolean("has_sovereign_link").notNull().default(false),
  sovereignUrl: text("sovereign_url").unique(), // timenation.tv/moment/YYYY-MM-DD-HHmm
  // Shariah / anti-Gharar: theme chosen BEFORE render to make service deterministic.
  // Values: space | cyberpunk | abstract | nature | minimal  (null = not yet chosen)
  visualStyle: text("visual_style"),
  mediaUrl: text("media_url"), // local rendered MP4 path
  // ── Catcast.tv publishing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  catcastVideoId: text("catcast_video_id"), // Catcast internal video ID
  catcastUrl: text("catcast_url"), // public Catcast video URL (video_url in meaning_containers)
  // ── Compliance & Moderation ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  isReported: boolean("is_reported").notNull().default(false), // DMCA / takedown flag
  reportStatus: text("report_status").notNull().default("active"), // active | suspended | removed
  reportReason: text("report_reason"), // reason from reporter
  status: text("status").notNull().default("draft"), // draft | pending_render | rendered | scheduled | broadcast
  renderProgress: text("render_progress"), // generating_prompt | generating_tts | fetching_video | encoding | done
  isRetro: boolean("is_retro").notNull().default(false), // slot_time < NOW
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
});

export const insertTvCapsuleSchema = createInsertSchema(tvCapsulesTable).omit({
  id: true,
  createdAt: true,
  updatedAt: true,
});
export type InsertTvCapsule = z.infer<typeof insertTvCapsuleSchema>;
export type TvCapsule = typeof tvCapsulesTable.$inferSelect;

// --- FILE: lib/db/src/schema/tvSlots.ts ---
import { pgTable, serial, integer, text, real, boolean, timestamp, uniqueIndex } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { createInsertSchema } from "drizzle-zod";
import { z } from "zod/v4";

// ─── Slot types by minute position ───────────────────────────────────────────
// apex    : minuteIndex % 60 === 0          (:00)  → 24 slots/day
// half    : minuteIndex % 30 === 0, ≠ :00   (:30)  → 24 slots/day
// quarter : minuteIndex % 15 === 0, ≠ :00/:30  (:15, :45)  → 48 slots/day
// citizen : all others                      → 1344 slots/day
// Total: 1440 slots/day
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const tvSlotsTable = pgTable(
  "tv_slots",
  {
    id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
    date: text("date").notNull(), // YYYY-MM-DD
    minuteIndex: integer("minute_index").notNull(), // 0–1439
    slotType: text("slot_type").notNull(), // citizen | quarter | half | apex
    status: text("status").notNull().default("free"), // free | booked | broadcast
    ownerId: integer("owner_id"), // FK citizens.id
    paymentType: text("payment_type"), // fiat | timeltr | null
    priceEur: real("price_eur").notNull(),
    timeltrBurnCost: integer("timeltr_burn_cost").notNull(),
    fingerprintHash: text("fingerprint_hash"), // anti-consecutive guard
    stripeSessionId: text("stripe_session_id"),
    cryptoPaymentUuid: text("crypto_payment_uuid"),   // Cryptomus payment UUID
    paymentStatus: text("payment_status").default("pending"), // pending | completed | refunded
    billingDescriptor: text("billing_descriptor"),    // exact label used in checkout
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [uniqueIndex("tv_slots_date_minute_idx").on(t.date, t.minuteIndex)]
);

export const insertTvSlotSchema = createInsertSchema(tvSlotsTable).omit({
  id: true,
  createdAt: true,
});
export type InsertTvSlot = z.infer<typeof insertTvSlotSchema>;
export type TvSlot = typeof tvSlotsTable.$inferSelect;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/auctions.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { auctionsTable, auctionLogsTable, citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const router: IRouter = Router();

const CreateAuctionBody = z.object({
  sellerId: z.number().int().positive(),
  amountDarmins: z.number().int().positive(),
  startingPrice: z.number().positive(),
  durationHours: z.number().int().min(1).max(168).default(24),
});

const BidBody = z.object({
  buyerId: z.number().int().positive(),
  bid: z.number().positive(),
});

// GET /api/auctions — активные лоты
router.get("/auctions", async (_req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const auctions = await db
    .select({
      id: auctionsTable.id,
      sellerId: auctionsTable.sellerId,
      sellerName: citizensTable.name,
      amountDarmins: auctionsTable.amountDarmins,
      startingPrice: auctionsTable.startingPrice,
      currentBid: auctionsTable.currentBid,
      buyerId: auctionsTable.buyerId,
      status: auctionsTable.status,
      endsAt: auctionsTable.endsAt,
      createdAt: auctionsTable.createdAt,
    })
    .from(auctionsTable)
    .leftJoin(citizensTable, eq(auctionsTable.sellerId, citizensTable.id))
    .where(eq(auctionsTable.status, "active"));

  res.json(auctions);
});

// POST /api/auctions — создать лот
router.post("/auctions", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CreateAuctionBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { sellerId, amountDarmins, startingPrice, durationHours } = parsed.data;

  const [seller] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, sellerId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!seller) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Seller not found" }); return; }

  // Родовая минута (1 Д) не может быть выставлена
  const transferable = seller.balanceMinutes - 1;
  if (amountDarmins > transferable) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: `Недостаточно Дарминов. Доступно: ${transferable}` });
    return;
  }

  // Блокируем Дармины продавца на время аукциона
  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: seller.balanceMinutes - amountDarmins })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, sellerId));

  const endsAt = new Date(Date.now() + durationHours * 60 * 60 * 1000);

  const [auction] = await db
    .insert(auctionsTable)
    .values({ sellerId, amountDarmins, startingPrice, status: "active", endsAt })
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ sellerId, amountDarmins, auctionId: auction!.id }, "Auction created");
  res.status(201).json(auction);
});

// POST /api/auctions/:id/bid — сделать ставку
router.post("/auctions/:id/bid", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid auction ID" }); return; }

  const parsed = BidBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { buyerId, bid } = parsed.data;

  const [auction] = await db
    .select()
    .from(auctionsTable)
    .where(and(eq(auctionsTable.id, id), eq(auctionsTable.status, "active")))
    .limit(1);

  if (!auction) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Активный аукцион не найден" }); return; }
  if (new Date() > auction.endsAt) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Аукцион истёк" }); return; }
  if (auction.sellerId === buyerId) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Нельзя делать ставку на собственный лот" }); return; }

  const minBid = auction.currentBid ?? auction.startingPrice;
  if (bid <= minBid) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: `Ставка должна быть выше ${minBid}` });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(auctionsTable)
    .set({ currentBid: bid, buyerId })
    .where(eq(auctionsTable.id, id));

  res.json({ ok: true, currentBid: bid, buyerId });
});

// POST /api/auctions/:id/finalize — завершить аукцион и зачислить Дармины победителю
router.post("/auctions/:id/finalize", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid ID" }); return; }

  const [auction] = await db
    .select()
    .from(auctionsTable)
    .where(and(eq(auctionsTable.id, id), eq(auctionsTable.status, "active")))
    .limit(1);

  if (!auction) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Активный аукцион не найден" }); return; }
  if (new Date() < auction.endsAt) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Аукцион ещё не завершён" }); return; }

  const finalPrice = auction.currentBid ?? auction.startingPrice;
  // 2% комиссия. Точная дробь, без округления.
  const tax = finalPrice * 0.02;

  await db.update(auctionsTable).set({ status: "completed" }).where(eq(auctionsTable.id, id));

  if (auction.buyerId) {
    // Зачислить Дармины победителю
    const [buyer] = await db.select().from(citizensTable).where(eq(citizensTable.id, auction.buyerId)).limit(1);
    if (buyer) {
      await db.update(citizensTable)
        .set({ balanceMinutes: buyer.balanceMinutes + auction.amountDarmins })
        .where(eq(citizensTable.id, auction.buyerId));
    }

    // Зачислить продавцу (finalPrice - tax) — условно через Казначейство (Дармины)
    const [seller] = await db.select().from(citizensTable).where(eq(citizensTable.id, auction.sellerId)).limit(1);
    if (seller) {
      const sellerReceives = Math.floor(finalPrice - tax);
      await db.update(citizensTable)
        .set({ balanceMinutes: seller.balanceMinutes + sellerReceives })
        .where(eq(citizensTable.id, auction.sellerId));
    }

    await db.insert(auctionLogsTable).values({
      auctionId: id,
      buyerId: auction.buyerId,
      sellerId: auction.sellerId,
      finalPrice,
      tax2pct: tax,
    });

    req.log.info({ auctionId: id, finalPrice, tax }, "Auction finalized");
    res.json({ ok: true, finalPrice, tax, winner: auction.buyerId });
  } else {
    // Нет победителя — вернуть Дармины продавцу
    const [seller] = await db.select().from(citizensTable).where(eq(citizensTable.id, auction.sellerId)).limit(1);
    if (seller) {
      await db.update(citizensTable)
        .set({ balanceMinutes: seller.balanceMinutes + auction.amountDarmins })
        .where(eq(citizensTable.id, auction.sellerId));
    }
    res.json({ ok: true, finalPrice: 0, winner: null });
  }
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/chronopedia.ts ---
import { Router } from "express";
import { z } from "zod/v4";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import {
  chronopediaSlotsTable,
  chronopediaEventsTable,
  citizensTable,
} from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, ne, desc, and, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

// ── Validation schemas ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CreateSlotBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  geoLat: z.number().min(-90).max(90),
  geoLng: z.number().min(-180).max(180),
  locationName: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  placeId: z.string().min(1).max(300).optional(),
  timeMarker: z.string().min(1).max(100),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
});

const AddEventBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
});

const EditEventBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200).optional(),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(2000).optional(),
});

// Generate synthetic placeId from coordinates (±1km grid)
function syntheticPlaceId(lat: number, lng: number): string {
  const rLat = Math.round(lat * 100) / 100;
  const rLng = Math.round(lng * 100) / 100;
  return `syn_${rLat}_${rLng}`;
}

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/slots?citizenId=X ────────────────────────────────────
// All slots with event counts and hasMyEvent flag.
router.get("/chronopedia/slots", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = parseInt(String(req.query["citizenId"] ?? ""), 10) || null;

  const rows = await db
    .select({
      id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id,
      timeMarker: chronopediaSlotsTable.timeMarker,
      placeId: chronopediaSlotsTable.placeId,
      geoLat: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLat,
      geoLng: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLng,
      locationName: chronopediaSlotsTable.locationName,
      createdAt: chronopediaSlotsTable.createdAt,
      eventCount: sql<number>`count(case when ${chronopediaEventsTable.status} != 'deleted' then 1 end)::int`,
      hasMyEvent: citizenId
        ? sql<boolean>`bool_or(${chronopediaEventsTable.authorId} = ${citizenId} and ${chronopediaEventsTable.status} != 'deleted')`
        : sql<boolean>`false`,
    })
    .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
    .leftJoin(
      chronopediaEventsTable,
      eq(chronopediaEventsTable.slotId, chronopediaSlotsTable.id)
    )
    .groupBy(chronopediaSlotsTable.id)
    .orderBy(desc(chronopediaSlotsTable.createdAt))
    .limit(500);

  res.json({ slots: rows });
});

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/feed?citizenId=X&limit=50 ───────────────────────────
// Recent events across all slots (for feed view).
router.get("/chronopedia/feed", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const limit = Math.min(parseInt(String(req.query["limit"] ?? "50"), 10) || 50, 100);

  const events = await db
    .select({
      id: chronopediaEventsTable.id,
      slotId: chronopediaEventsTable.slotId,
      authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId,
      authorName: citizensTable.name,
      authorRank: citizensTable.rank,
      title: chronopediaEventsTable.title,
      description: chronopediaEventsTable.description,
      status: chronopediaEventsTable.status,
      createdAt: chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt,
      locationName: chronopediaSlotsTable.locationName,
      timeMarker: chronopediaSlotsTable.timeMarker,
      geoLat: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLat,
      geoLng: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLng,
    })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .innerJoin(
      chronopediaSlotsTable,
      eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.id, chronopediaEventsTable.slotId)
    )
    .innerJoin(citizensTable, eq(citizensTable.id, chronopediaEventsTable.authorId))
    .where(ne(chronopediaEventsTable.status, "deleted"))
    .orderBy(desc(chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt))
    .limit(limit);

  res.json({ events });
});

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/slots/:id ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Slot detail with all non-deleted events + author info.
router.get("/chronopedia/slots/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slot ID" }); return; }

  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }

  const events = await db
    .select({
      id: chronopediaEventsTable.id,
      authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId,
      authorName: citizensTable.name,
      authorRank: citizensTable.rank,
      title: chronopediaEventsTable.title,
      description: chronopediaEventsTable.description,
      status: chronopediaEventsTable.status,
      createdAt: chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt,
      updatedAt: chronopediaEventsTable.updatedAt,
    })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .innerJoin(citizensTable, eq(citizensTable.id, chronopediaEventsTable.authorId))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(chronopediaEventsTable.slotId, slotId),
        ne(chronopediaEventsTable.status, "deleted")
      )
    )
    .orderBy(desc(chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt));

  res.json({ slot, events });
});

// ── POST /api/chronopedia/slots ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Create a new space-time slot + first opinion.
// If slot with same timeMarker+placeId already exists → adds event to existing slot.
router.post("/chronopedia/slots", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CreateSlotBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, geoLat, geoLng, locationName, timeMarker, title, description } = parsed.data;
  const placeId = parsed.data.placeId ?? syntheticPlaceId(geoLat, geoLng);

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  let slotId: number;
  let created = false;

  try {
    const [inserted] = await db
      .insert(chronopediaSlotsTable)
      .values({ timeMarker, placeId, geoLat, geoLng, locationName })
      .returning({ id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id });
    slotId = inserted!.id;
    created = true;
  } catch {
    // Unique constraint violation — slot already exists, find it
    const [existing] = await db
      .select({ id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id })
      .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.timeMarker, timeMarker),
          eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.placeId, placeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1);

    if (!existing) { res.status(409).json({ error: "Конфликт при создании слота" }); return; }
    slotId = existing.id;
  }

  const [event] = await db
    .insert(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .values({ slotId, authorId: citizenId, title, description, status: "approved" })
    .returning({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id });

  req.log.info({ citizenId, slotId, created }, "Chronopedia slot/event created");
  res.status(201).json({ ok: true, slotId, eventId: event!.id, created });
});

// ── POST /api/chronopedia/slots/:id/events ────────────────────────────────────
// Add an opinion to an existing slot. Free — no Дармин cost.
router.post("/chronopedia/slots/:id/events", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slot ID" }); return; }

  const parsed = AddEventBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, title, description } = parsed.data;

  const [slot] = await db
    .select({ id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id })
    .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  const [event] = await db
    .insert(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .values({ slotId, authorId: citizenId, title, description, status: "approved" })
    .returning({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id });

  req.log.info({ citizenId, slotId }, "Chronopedia event added");
  res.status(201).json({ ok: true, eventId: event!.id });
});

// ── PATCH /api/chronopedia/events/:id ────────────────────────────────────────
// Edit own event (title and/or description).
router.patch("/chronopedia/events/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const eventId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(eventId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid event ID" }); return; }

  const parsed = EditEventBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, title, description } = parsed.data;

  const [event] = await db
    .select({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id, authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!event) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Запись не найдена" }); return; }
  if (event.authorId !== citizenId) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только автор может редактировать запись" });
    return;
  }

  const updates: Record<string, unknown> = { updatedAt: new Date() };
  if (title !== undefined) updates["title"] = title;
  if (description !== undefined) updates["description"] = description;

  await db
    .update(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .set(updates)
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId));

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// ── DELETE /api/chronopedia/events/:id ───────────────────────────────────────
// Soft-delete own event (sets status = 'deleted').
router.delete("/chronopedia/events/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const eventId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(eventId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid event ID" }); return; }

  const citizenId = z.number().int().positive().safeParse(req.body?.citizenId);
  if (!citizenId.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [event] = await db
    .select({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id, authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!event) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Запись не найдена" }); return; }
  if (event.authorId !== citizenId.data) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только автор может удалить запись" });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .set({ status: "deleted", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId));

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/places/search?q=TEXT ────────────────────────────────
// Proxy to Google Places Text Search API (keeps key server-side).
router.get("/chronopedia/places/search", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const q = String(req.query["q"] ?? "").trim();
  if (!q) { res.json({ places: [] }); return; }

  const apiKey = process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
  if (!apiKey) { res.json({ places: [] }); return; }

  try {
    const url = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/textsearch/json?query=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&key=${apiKey}&language=ru`;
    const upstream = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
    if (!upstream.ok) { res.json({ places: [] }); return; }

    const data = await upstream.json() as {
      results?: {
        place_id: string;
        name: string;
        formatted_address: string;
        geometry: { location: { lat: number; lng: number } };
      }[];
    };

    const places = (data.results ?? []).slice(0, 5).map((r) => ({
      placeId: r.place_id,
      name: r.name,
      address: r.formatted_address,
      lat: r.geometry.location.lat,
      lng: r.geometry.location.lng,
    }));

    res.json({ places });
  } catch {
    res.json({ places: [] });
  }
});
