// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function formatCitizenRow(c: typeof citizensTable.$inferSelect) {
  return {
    ...c,
    // Decrypt PII fields before returning — encrypted values stored as v1:... in DB
    email:      tryDecrypt(c.email)      ?? null,
    surname:    tryDecrypt(c.surname)    ?? null,
    givenNames: tryDecrypt(c.givenNames) ?? null,
    // Never expose the raw vault path as a web URL
    photoUrl: c.photoUrl ? "vault:protected" : null,
    transferableBalance: c.balanceMinutes - 1,
    lastActivity: c.lastActivity?.toISOString() ?? null,
    createdAt: c.createdAt.toISOString(),
    availableAt: c.availableAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
  };
}

async function generateNextCitizenNumber(): Promise<string> {
  const result = await db
    .select({ max: sql<string>`MAX(citizen_number)` })
    .from(citizensTable);
  const current = result[0]?.max ? parseInt(result[0].max, 10) : 0;
  return String(current + 1).padStart(9, "0");
}

// ── Passport SVG helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function p2(n: number): string { return String(Math.floor(n)).padStart(2, "0"); }

// ICAO Doc 9303 transliteration table (Russian Cyrillic → Latin)
const CYR_TO_LAT: Record<string, string> = {
  А:"A", Б:"B", В:"V", Г:"G", Д:"D", Е:"E", Ё:"E", Ж:"ZH", З:"Z",
  И:"I", Й:"I", К:"K", Л:"L", М:"M", Н:"N", О:"O", П:"P", Р:"R",
  С:"S", Т:"T", У:"U", Ф:"F", Х:"KH", Ц:"TS", Ч:"CH", Ш:"SH",
  Щ:"SHCH", Ъ:"IE", Ы:"Y", Ь:"", Э:"E", Ю:"IU", Я:"IA",
  а:"A", б:"B", в:"V", г:"G", д:"D", е:"E", ё:"E", ж:"ZH", з:"Z",
  и:"I", й:"I", к:"K", л:"L", м:"M", н:"N", о:"O", п:"P", р:"R",
  с:"S", т:"T", у:"U", ф:"F", х:"KH", ц:"TS", ч:"CH", ш:"SH",
  щ:"SHCH", ъ:"IE", ы:"Y", ь:"", э:"E", ю:"IU", я:"IA",
};

function translit(s: string): string {
  return s
    .split("")
    .map(ch => CYR_TO_LAT[ch] ?? (/[A-Za-z0-9\s<]/.test(ch) ? ch.toUpperCase() : ""))
    .join("")
    .toUpperCase();
}

function starPath(cx: number, cy: number, r: number, fill: string): string {
  const pts: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const a = (i * Math.PI) / 5 - Math.PI / 2;
    const radius = i % 2 === 0 ? r : r * 0.42;
    pts.push(`${(cx + radius * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(1)},${(cy + radius * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(1)}`);
  }
  return `<polygon points="${pts.join(" ")}" fill="${fill}" opacity="0.55"/>`;
}

function starsRing(cx: number, cy: number, R: number, n: number, fill: string): string {
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const a = (i * 2 * Math.PI) / n - Math.PI / 2;
    out += starPath(cx + R * Math.cos(a), cy + R * Math.sin(a), 5.5, fill);
  }
  return out;
}

function decorativeQr(x0: number, y0: number, size: number): string {
  // 21-module visual QR pattern (decorative, not scannable)
  const rows = [
    "111111101001101111111",
    "100000101110001000001",
    "101110101011001011101",
    "101110100100101011101",
    "101110101010001011101",
    "100000100110101000001",
    "111111101010101111111",
    "000000011101100000000",
    "101011010110101010110",
    "010110100101010011010",
    "110100101011010110010",
    "001001010110100100101",
    "010110101001010100110",
    "000000011010100000001",
    "111111011011101011010",
    "100000100101010111110",
    "101110101010010011010",
    "101110100101101110010",
    "101110101010101001010",
    "100000101101010000010",
    "111111101010101111110",
  ];
  const cs = size / 21;
  let out = `<rect x="${x0}" y="${y0}" width="${size}" height="${size}" fill="white" rx="3"/>`;
  for (let r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const row = rows[r] ?? "";
    for (let col = 0; col < row.length; col++) {
      if (row[col] === "1") {
        out += `<rect x="${(x0 + col * cs).toFixed(1)}" y="${(y0 + r * cs).toFixed(1)}" width="${cs.toFixed(1)}" height="${cs.toFixed(1)}" fill="#0a1428"/>`;
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function generatePassportSvg(c: {
  citizenNumber: string;
  name: string;
  surname?: string | null;
  givenNames?: string | null;
  dateOfBirth?: string | null;
  rank: string;
  meritScore: number;
  createdAt: Date | string;
  photoUri?: string | null;
  signatureBase64?: string | null;
}): string {
  const surnameVal = (c.surname ?? c.name.split(" ")[0] ?? c.name).toUpperCase();
  const givenVal = (c.givenNames ?? (c.name.split(" ").slice(1).join(" ") || c.name)).toUpperCase();
  const dobRaw = c.dateOfBirth ?? "";

  // DATE OF BIRTH display: "08 FEB 1990"
  const months = ["JAN","FEB","MAR","APR","MAY","JUN","JUL","AUG","SEP","OCT","NOV","DEC"];
  const dobDisplay = dobRaw
    ? `${dobRaw.slice(8, 10)} ${months[parseInt(dobRaw.slice(5, 7), 10) - 1] ?? "?"} ${dobRaw.slice(0, 4)}`
    : "—";

  // DATE/TIME OF ISSUE
  const issued = new Date(c.createdAt);
  const issueDT = `${p2(issued.getDate())}.${p2(issued.getMonth() + 1)}.${issued.getFullYear()}  ${p2(issued.getHours())}:${p2(issued.getMinutes())}`;

  // MRZ (ICAO TD3, transliterated to Latin)
  const mrzSurname = translit(surnameVal).replace(/\s+/g, "<");
  const mrzGiven  = translit(givenVal).replace(/\s+/g, "<");
  const mrzName = `${mrzSurname}<<${mrzGiven}`;
  const mrz1 = `IN${mrzName}`.padEnd(30, "<").slice(0, 30);
  const dobMrz = dobRaw ? `${dobRaw.slice(2,4)}${dobRaw.slice(5,7)}${dobRaw.slice(8,10)}` : "000000";
  const mrz2 = `${c.citizenNumber}<TN${dobMrz}M9912319`.padEnd(30, "<").slice(0, 30);

  const esc = (s: string) =>
    s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");

  const stars = starsRing(594, 190, 54, 12, "#d4a020");
  const qr = decorativeQr(472, 68, 98);

  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="680" height="430" viewBox="0 0 680 430">
  <defs>
    <pattern id="dp" width="11" height="11" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
      <circle cx="5.5" cy="5.5" r="0.7" fill="#7aadd0" opacity="0.45"/>
    </pattern>
    <clipPath id="card"><rect width="680" height="430" rx="14"/></clipPath>
    <linearGradient id="bgGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#c0d4ec"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#d4e6f6"/>
    </linearGradient>
  </defs>

  <!-- Card background -->
  <rect width="680" height="430" rx="14" fill="url(#bgGrad)"/>
  <rect width="680" height="430" fill="url(#dp)" clip-path="url(#card)"/>

  <!-- Large ∞ watermark -->
  <text x="388" y="310" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="270" fill="#5a90be" opacity="0.13" text-anchor="middle">∞</text>

  <!-- Stars ring (right) -->
  ${stars}

  <!-- ══ HEADER ══ -->
  <!-- Emblem (top-left, real image) -->
  ${EMBLEM_DATA_URI
    ? `<defs><clipPath id="pEmblemClip"><circle cx="43" cy="32" r="22"/></clipPath></defs>
  <image href="${EMBLEM_DATA_URI}" x="21" y="10" width="44" height="44" clip-path="url(#pEmblemClip)" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"/>
  <circle cx="43" cy="32" r="22" fill="none" stroke="#8ab4d4" stroke-width="1"/>`
    : `<rect x="18" y="15" width="50" height="35" rx="3" fill="#1e3a8a"/>
  <text x="43" y="36" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="11" fill="#d0e4ff" text-anchor="middle">∞</text>`}

  <!-- TIMENATION left -->
  <text x="76" y="33" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="21" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a3a8a" letter-spacing="0.5">TIMENATION</text>
  <text x="76" y="47" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10" fill="#4468a8">Republic of TimeNation</text>

  <!-- Flag (top-right, real image) -->
  ${FLAG_DATA_URI
    ? `<defs><clipPath id="pFlagClip"><rect x="590" y="12" width="72" height="43" rx="3"/></clipPath></defs>
  <image href="${FLAG_DATA_URI}" x="590" y="12" width="72" height="43" clip-path="url(#pFlagClip)" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"/>
  <rect x="590" y="12" width="72" height="43" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#8ab4d4" stroke-width="0.8"/>`
    : `<text x="662" y="33" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="21" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a3a8a" text-anchor="end" letter-spacing="0.5">TIMENATION</text>
  <text x="662" y="47" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10" fill="#4468a8" text-anchor="end">FUTURE BEGINS NOW</text>`}

  <!-- Divider -->
  <line x1="18" y1="60" x2="662" y2="60" stroke="#8ab0d4" stroke-width="0.9"/>

  <!-- ══ PHOTO AREA ══ -->
  <rect x="18" y="70" width="152" height="213" rx="5" fill="#dce9f5" stroke="#9bbcd8" stroke-width="1"/>
  <!-- silhouette placeholder -->
  <circle cx="94" cy="145" r="38" fill="#b8cee4"/>
  <ellipse cx="94" cy="223" rx="54" ry="32" fill="#b8cee4"/>
  <text x="94" y="152" font-family="Arial,sans-serif" font-size="9" fill="#7a9abf" text-anchor="middle" opacity="0.7">PHOTO</text>

  <!-- ══ DATA FIELDS ══ -->
  <!-- SURNAME -->
  <text x="188" y="87" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">SURNAME</text>
  <text x="188" y="107" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="18" fill="#090f1e">${esc(surnameVal)}</text>

  <!-- GIVEN NAME -->
  <text x="188" y="128" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">GIVEN NAME</text>
  <text x="188" y="148" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="18" fill="#090f1e">${esc(givenVal)}</text>

  <!-- NATIONALITY -->
  <text x="188" y="169" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">NATIONALITY</text>
  <text x="188" y="189" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="18" fill="#090f1e">TIMENATION</text>

  <!-- DATE OF BIRTH -->
  <text x="188" y="210" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">DATE OF BIRTH</text>
  <text x="188" y="230" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="18" fill="#090f1e">${esc(dobDisplay)}</text>

  <!-- DATE/TIME OF ISSUE -->
  <text x="188" y="251" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">DATE/TIME OF ISSUE</text>
  <text x="188" y="271" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="18" fill="#090f1e">${esc(issueDT)}</text>

  <!-- CITIZEN NO + RANK -->
  <text x="188" y="295" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">CITIZEN NO.</text>
  <text x="188" y="313" font-family="Courier New,monospace" font-size="17" font-weight="bold" fill="#090f1e" letter-spacing="3">${esc(c.citizenNumber)}</text>

  <!-- RANK -->
  <text x="188" y="338" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="9.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1d4ed8" letter-spacing="1.2">RANK / MERIT</text>
  <text x="188" y="356" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="14" fill="#090f1e">${esc(c.rank)}  ·  ${c.meritScore.toFixed(1)}</text>

  <!-- ══ QR CODE (decorative) ══ -->
  ${qr}

  <!-- ══ MINI PHOTO (right, below QR) ══ -->
  <rect x="472" y="178" width="98" height="105" rx="4" fill="#dce9f5" stroke="#9bbcd8" stroke-width="0.8"/>
  <circle cx="521" cy="220" r="24" fill="#b8cee4"/>
  <ellipse cx="521" cy="278" rx="34" ry="22" fill="#b8cee4"/>

  <!-- Signature line -->
  ${c.signatureBase64 ? `<image href="${esc(c.signatureBase64)}" x="472" y="263" width="98" height="30" preserveAspectRatio="xMinYMid meet" opacity="0.92"/>` : ""}
  <line x1="472" y1="296" x2="570" y2="296" stroke="#8ab0d4" stroke-width="0.8"/>
  <text x="488" y="308" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="9" fill="#4a6a9a" font-style="italic">signature</text>

  <!-- ══ MRZ ZONE ══ -->
  <rect x="0" y="363" width="680" height="67" fill="#070e1a" clip-path="url(#card)"/>
  <rect x="0" y="363" width="680" height="2" fill="#2060a8"/>
  <text x="18" y="388" font-family="Courier New,monospace" font-size="14" fill="#5a88b8" letter-spacing="2.5">${esc(mrz1)}</text>
  <text x="18" y="413" font-family="Courier New,monospace" font-size="14" fill="#5a88b8" letter-spacing="2.5">${esc(mrz2)}</text>
</svg>`;
}

// ── GET /citizens ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/citizens", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizens = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .orderBy(citizensTable.createdAt);
  res.json(citizens.map(formatCitizenRow));
});

// ── POST /citizens ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Extended registration: generates 9-digit citizenNumber, sets 1-hour pending delay.
// Accepts both multipart/form-data (with photo) and application/json.
const RegisterCitizenBody = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Имя обязательно"),
  phone: z.string().min(5, "Телефон обязателен"),
  surname: z.string().optional(),
  givenNames: z.string().optional(),
  dateOfBirth: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional().or(z.literal("")).optional(),
  photoUrl: z.string().optional(),
  tosAcceptedAt: z.string().optional(),
  telegramId: z.string().optional(),
  email: z.string().email("Неверный формат email").optional().or(z.literal("")).optional(),
  privacyAgreementAccepted: z.string().optional(), // "true" from FormData
  signatureBase64: z.string().optional(),
});

router.post("/citizens", upload.single("passport_photo"), async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = RegisterCitizenBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Неверные данные" });
    return;
  }

  const existing = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.phone, parsed.data.phone))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing.length > 0) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Гражданин с таким телефоном уже существует" });
    return;
  }

  const citizenNumber = await generateNextCitizenNumber();
  const availableAt = new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000); // +1 hour

  const { name, phone, surname, givenNames, dateOfBirth, tosAcceptedAt, telegramId, email, privacyAgreementAccepted, signatureBase64: rawSignature } = parsed.data;

  // ── 1. Normalise passport photo (resize to 400×514 px JPEG) ─────────────────
  let vaultPhotoRef: string | null = null;
  if (req.file) {
    const normPath = await normalizePassportPhoto(req.file.path);
    // normPath may have changed extension (e.g. .png → .jpg)
    const normFilename = path.basename(normPath);
    vaultPhotoRef = `vault:photos/${normFilename}`;
  }

  // ── 2. Crop signature to bounding box ────────────────────────────────────────
  const signatureBase64 = rawSignature
    ? await cropSignature(rawSignature)
    : null;

  const privacyAccepted = privacyAgreementAccepted === "true";

  const [citizen] = await db
    .insert(citizensTable)
    .values({
      name,                                            // display name stays plaintext
      phone,
      surname:    encryptNullable(surname ?? null),    // PII — encrypted
      givenNames: encryptNullable(givenNames ?? null), // PII — encrypted
      dateOfBirth: dateOfBirth || null,
      photoUrl: vaultPhotoRef,
      citizenNumber,
      registrationStatus: "pending",
      availableAt,
      tosAcceptedAt: tosAcceptedAt ? new Date(tosAcceptedAt) : undefined,
      telegramId: telegramId ?? undefined,
      email: encryptNullable(email || null),           // PII — encrypted
      privacyAgreementAccepted: privacyAccepted ? 1 : 0,
      privacyAgreementDate: privacyAccepted ? new Date() : null,
      signatureBase64,
    })
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ citizenId: citizen!.id, citizenNumber }, "New citizen registered — pending verification");

  res.status(201).json({
    ...formatCitizenRow(citizen!),
    citizenNumber,
    availableAt: availableAt.toISOString(),
    registrationStatus: "pending",
  });
});

// ── GET /citizens/by-phone/:phone ─────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/citizens/by-phone/:phone", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const phone = Array.isArray(req.params.phone)
    ? req.params.phone[0]
    : req.params.phone;

  if (!phone) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Phone is required" });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.phone, phone))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  res.json(formatCitizenRow(citizen));
});

// ── GET /citizens/number/:num/status ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Checks registration status. Auto-issues passport after 1-hour delay.
router.get("/citizens/number/:num/status", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const num = req.params["num"] ?? "";

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.citizenNumber, num))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" });
    return;
  }

  // Legacy citizen (no registrationStatus) → treated as issued
  if (!citizen.registrationStatus || citizen.registrationStatus === "issued") {
    const passportUrl = `/api/citizens/number/${num}/passport`;
    res.json({ status: "issued", citizenNumber: num, idCardUrl: passportUrl });
    return;
  }

  if (citizen.registrationStatus === "rejected") {
    res.json({ status: "rejected", citizenNumber: num });
    return;
  }

  // pending — check if 1 hour has elapsed
  const now = Date.now();
  const due = citizen.availableAt ? citizen.availableAt.getTime() : now;

  if (now >= due) {
    // Auto-issue
    const passportUrl = `/api/citizens/number/${num}/passport`;
    await db
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ registrationStatus: "issued", idCardUrl: passportUrl })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.citizenNumber, num));

    req.log.info({ citizenNumber: num, citizenId: citizen.id }, "Passport auto-issued after 1-hour delay");

    // Log newly-issued citizen to Google Sheets (fire-and-forget)
    logCitizenIssuedToSheets({
      citizenNumber: num,
      citizenId: citizen.id,
      name: citizen.name,
      phone: citizen.phone,
      rank: citizen.rank,
      balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes,
      meritScore: citizen.meritScore,
      registrationStatus: "issued",
      issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    }).catch((err) => req.log.warn({ err }, "Sheets citizen log failed (non-critical)"));

    res.json({ status: "issued", citizenNumber: num, idCardUrl: passportUrl });
    return;
  }

  const timeLeftSeconds = Math.ceil((due - now) / 1000);
  res.json({
    status: "pending",
    citizenNumber: num,
    timeLeftSeconds,
    availableAt: citizen.availableAt?.toISOString(),
  });
});

// ── GET /citizens/number/:num/passport ───────────────────────────────────────
// Returns legacy SVG passport (kept for backwards-compatibility).
router.get("/citizens/number/:num/passport", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const num = req.params["num"] ?? "";

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.citizenNumber, num))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).send("Гражданин не найден");
    return;
  }

  const photoUri = loadPhotoAsDataUri(citizen.photoUrl);
  const svg = generatePassportSvg({
    citizenNumber: citizen.citizenNumber ?? num,
    name: citizen.name,
    surname: tryDecrypt(citizen.surname),     // decrypt PII
    givenNames: tryDecrypt(citizen.givenNames), // decrypt PII
    dateOfBirth: citizen.dateOfBirth,
    rank: citizen.rank,
    meritScore: citizen.meritScore,
    createdAt: citizen.createdAt,
    photoUri,
    signatureBase64: citizen.signatureBase64 ?? null,
  });

  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "image/svg+xml");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
  res.send(svg);
});

// ── GET /citizens/number/:num/id-card ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Returns the new ID card SVG (matches physical TimeNation card template).
router.get("/citizens/number/:num/id-card", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const num = req.params["num"] ?? "";

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.citizenNumber, num))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).send("Гражданин не найден");
    return;
  }

  const photoUri = loadPhotoAsDataUri(citizen.photoUrl);

  const svg = generateIdCardSvg({
    citizenNumber: citizen.citizenNumber ?? num,
    name: citizen.name,
    surname: tryDecrypt(citizen.surname),     // decrypt PII
    givenNames: tryDecrypt(citizen.givenNames), // decrypt PII
    dateOfBirth: citizen.dateOfBirth,
    rank: citizen.rank,
    meritScore: citizen.meritScore,
    createdAt: citizen.createdAt,
    photoUrl: citizen.photoUrl,
    signatureBase64: citizen.signatureBase64 ?? null,
  }, photoUri);

  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "image/svg+xml");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
  res.send(svg);
});

// ── GET /citizens/lookup/:num — public passport lookup by citizen number ───────
router.get("/citizens/lookup/:num", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const num = (req.params["num"] ?? "").trim().replace(/\D/g, "").padStart(9, "0");

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.citizenNumber, num))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин с таким номером не найден в реестре" });
    return;
  }

  if (citizen.registrationStatus !== "issued") {
    res.json({
      status: "pending",
      citizenNumber: num,
      message: "Документы находятся на этапе верификации. Пожалуйста, подождите.",
    });
    return;
  }

  res.json({
    status: "issued",
    citizenNumber: num,
    name: citizen.name,
    rank: citizen.rank,
    idCardUrl: `/api/citizens/number/${num}/id-card`,
    passportUrl: `/api/citizens/number/${num}/passport`,
  });
});

// ── GET /citizens/:id ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/citizens/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const raw = Array.isArray(req.params.id) ? req.params.id[0] : req.params.id;
  const id = parseInt(raw ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid citizen ID" });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  res.json(formatCitizenRow(citizen));
});

// ── PATCH /citizens/:id ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.patch("/citizens/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const raw = Array.isArray(req.params.id) ? req.params.id[0] : req.params.id;
  const id = parseInt(raw ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid citizen ID" });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = UpdateCitizenBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set(parsed.data)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  res.json(formatCitizenRow(citizen));
});

// ── POST /citizens/:id/heritage-transfer ─────────────────────────────────────
const HeritageTransferBody = z.object({
  heirCitizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
});

router.post("/citizens/:id/heritage-transfer", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const raw = Array.isArray(req.params.id) ? req.params.id[0] : req.params.id;
  const id = parseInt(raw ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid citizen ID" });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = HeritageTransferBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { heirCitizenId } = parsed.data;
  if (id === heirCitizenId) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Нельзя передать наследство самому себе" });
    return;
  }

  const [fromCitizen] = await db.select().from(citizensTable).where(eq(citizensTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!fromCitizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  const [heirCitizen] = await db.select().from(citizensTable).where(eq(citizensTable.id, heirCitizenId)).limit(1);
  if (!heirCitizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Heir citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .set({ citizenId: heirCitizenId })
    .where(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.citizenId, id));

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: heirCitizen.balanceMinutes + fromCitizen.balanceMinutes, status: "inactive" })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, heirCitizenId));

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: 0, status: "inactive" })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id));

  req.log.info({ fromCitizenId: id, heirCitizenId }, "Heritage transfer completed");

  res.json({
    message: `Наследство гражданина ${fromCitizen.name} передано гражданину ${heirCitizen.name}`,
    fromCitizenId: id,
    heirCitizenId,
    balanceTransferred: fromCitizen.balanceMinutes,
  });
});

// ── POST /citizens/:id/wallet-link ───────────────────────────────────────────
const WalletLinkBody = z.object({
  walletAddress: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/, "walletAddress must be a valid EVM address"),
});

router.post("/citizens/:id/wallet-link", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const raw = Array.isArray(req.params.id) ? req.params.id[0] : req.params.id;
  const id = parseInt(raw ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid citizen ID" });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = WalletLinkBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { walletAddress } = parsed.data;
  const [existingWallet] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.walletAddress, walletAddress))
    .limit(1);

  if (existingWallet && existingWallet.id !== id) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Этот кошелёк уже привязан к другому гражданину" });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletAddress })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  req.log.info({ citizenId: id, walletAddress }, "Web3 wallet linked");
  res.json(formatCitizenRow(citizen));
});

// ── POST /citizens/:id/age-verify ────────────────────────────────────────────
router.post("/citizens/:id/age-verify", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id, ageVerified: citizensTable.ageVerified })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  if (citizen.ageVerified) {
    res.json({ ok: true, ageVerified: true, alreadyVerified: true });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ ageVerified: 1 })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id));

  req.log.info({ citizenId: id }, "Age verified (18+ — COPPA/DSA)");
  res.json({ ok: true, ageVerified: true });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/deals.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { dealsTable, citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, or } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const router: IRouter = Router();

// ─── Validation schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CreateDealBody = z.object({
  senderId: z.number().int().positive(),
  receiverId: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
  amount: z.number().int().min(1).max(10000),
  description: z.string().min(5).max(1000),
});

const ProofBody = z.object({
  receiverId: z.number().int().positive(),
  proofText: z.string().max(2000).optional(),
  proofUrl: z.string().url().optional(),
});

const ActorBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
});

// ─── POST /api/deals ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Create a new deal. Immediately locks `amount` from sender's balance (escrow).
router.post("/deals", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CreateDealBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { senderId, receiverId, amount, description } = parsed.data;

  const [sender] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id, balance: citizensTable.balanceMinutes })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, senderId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!sender) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Отправитель не найден" }); return; }
  if (sender.balance - 1 < amount) {
    // -1 because founding minute cannot be transferred
    res.status(402).json({ error: `Недостаточно Дарминов. Доступно: ${sender.balance - 1} Д` });
    return;
  }

  // Lock minutes in escrow (deduct from sender immediately)
  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: sender.balance - amount })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, senderId));

  const [deal] = await db
    .insert(dealsTable)
    .values({ senderId, receiverId: receiverId ?? null, amount, description, status: "open" })
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: deal.id, senderId, amount }, "TiMETR escrow deal created");
  res.status(201).json(deal);
});

// ─── GET /api/deals?citizenId=X ───────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/deals", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = z.number().int().positive().safeParse(Number(req.query["citizenId"]));
  if (!citizenId.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const deals = await db
    .select()
    .from(dealsTable)
    .where(or(
      eq(dealsTable.senderId, citizenId.data),
      eq(dealsTable.receiverId, citizenId.data),
    ))
    .orderBy(dealsTable.createdAt);

  res.json({ deals });
});

// ─── GET /api/deals/open ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Open marketplace — deals with no receiver assigned yet
router.get("/deals/open", async (_req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const deals = await db
    .select({
      id: dealsTable.id,
      senderId: dealsTable.senderId,
      senderName: citizensTable.name,
      amount: dealsTable.amount,
      description: dealsTable.description,
      status: dealsTable.status,
      createdAt: dealsTable.createdAt,
    })
    .from(dealsTable)
    .leftJoin(citizensTable, eq(dealsTable.senderId, citizensTable.id))
    .where(eq(dealsTable.status, "open"))
    .orderBy(dealsTable.createdAt);

  res.json({ deals });
});

// ─── GET /api/deals/:id ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/deals/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  res.json(deal);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/accept-task ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Receiver volunteers to take an open deal (assigns themselves).
router.post("/deals/:id/accept-task", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "open") { res.status(409).json({ error: `Сделка уже в статусе «${deal.status}»` }); return; }
  if (deal.senderId === parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Нельзя принять собственную сделку" }); return; }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ receiverId: parsed.data.citizenId, status: "accepted", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, receiverId: parsed.data.citizenId }, "Deal accepted by receiver");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/proof ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Receiver submits Proof-of-Work (text + optional URL).
router.post("/deals/:id/proof", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ProofBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "accepted") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Доказательство можно отправить только для принятой сделки" }); return; }
  if (deal.receiverId !== parsed.data.receiverId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только исполнитель может отправить доказательство" }); return; }
  if (!parsed.data.proofText && !parsed.data.proofUrl) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Нужно указать proof_text или proof_url" });
    return;
  }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({
      proofText: parsed.data.proofText ?? null,
      proofUrl: parsed.data.proofUrl ?? null,
      status: "proof_submitted",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id }, "Proof of work submitted");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/complete ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sender approves proof → releases escrow to receiver.
router.post("/deals/:id/complete", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "proof_submitted") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Нельзя завершить — доказательство ещё не отправлено" }); return; }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только отправитель может подтвердить сделку" }); return; }
  if (!deal.receiverId) { res.status(409).json({ error: "Получатель не назначен" }); return; }

  // Release escrow: credit receiver
  const [receiver] = await db
    .select({ balance: citizensTable.balanceMinutes })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.receiverId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!receiver) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Получатель не найден" }); return; }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: receiver.balance + deal.amount })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.receiverId));

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "completed", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, receiverId: deal.receiverId, amount: deal.amount }, "Deal completed, escrow released");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/decline ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sender declines proof → escrow stays locked, status = declined (dispute phase).
router.post("/deals/:id/decline", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "proof_submitted") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Нечего отклонять — доказательство не отправлено" }); return; }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только отправитель может отклонить сделку" }); return; }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "declined", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id }, "Deal declined by sender — escrow remains locked");
  res.json({ ...updated, note: "Эскроу заморожен. Откройте спор или отмените сделку для возврата." });
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/cancel ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sender cancels deal (open or declined) → returns escrow to sender.
router.post("/deals/:id/cancel", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (!["open", "declined"].includes(deal.status)) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: `Нельзя отменить сделку в статусе «${deal.status}»` });
    return;
  }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только отправитель может отменить сделку" }); return; }

  // Return escrow to sender
  const [sender] = await db
    .select({ balance: citizensTable.balanceMinutes })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.senderId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!sender) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Отправитель не найден" }); return; }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: sender.balance + deal.amount })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.senderId));

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "cancelled", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, senderId: deal.senderId, refunded: deal.amount }, "Deal cancelled, escrow returned to sender");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/dispute ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Either party flags the deal for manual resolution.
router.post("/deals/:id/dispute", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (!["accepted", "proof_submitted", "declined"].includes(deal.status)) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Спор можно открыть только для активной сделки" });
    return;
  }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId && deal.receiverId !== parsed.data.citizenId) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только участник сделки может открыть спор" });
    return;
  }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "disputed", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, initiatorId: parsed.data.citizenId }, "Deal disputed");
  res.json(updated);
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/embassies.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { nationalEmbassiesTable, citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { randomBytes } from "crypto";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const router: IRouter = Router();

// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Haversine distance in metres */
function distanceM(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): number {
  const R = 6_371_000;
  const dLat = ((lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLng = ((lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos((lat1 * Math.PI) / 180) *
    Math.cos((lat2 * Math.PI) / 180) *
    Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
}

// ── Schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CreateEmbassyBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  gpsLat:    z.number().min(-90).max(90),
  gpsLng:    z.number().min(-180).max(180),
  address:   z.string().max(300).optional(),
});

const SetNodeIpBody = z.object({
  nodeIp: z.string().ip({ version: "v4" }).or(z.string().ip({ version: "v6" })),
});

const VerifyLocationBody = z.object({
  token:    z.string().min(32).max(128),
  phoneLat: z.number().min(-90).max(90),
  phoneLng: z.number().min(-180).max(180),
});

// ── GET /api/embassies ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

router.get("/embassies", async (_req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const embassies = await db
    .select({
      id:                 nationalEmbassiesTable.id,
      citizenId:          nationalEmbassiesTable.citizenId,
      citizenName:        citizensTable.name,
      gpsLat:             nationalEmbassiesTable.gpsLat,
      gpsLng:             nationalEmbassiesTable.gpsLng,
      address:            nationalEmbassiesTable.address,
      nodeActive:         nationalEmbassiesTable.nodeActive,
      uptimeScore:        nationalEmbassiesTable.uptimeScore,
      status:             nationalEmbassiesTable.status,
      nodeStatus:         nationalEmbassiesTable.nodeStatus,
      lastPingMs:         nationalEmbassiesTable.lastPingMs,
      isHardwareVerified: nationalEmbassiesTable.isHardwareVerified,
      nftMetadata:        nationalEmbassiesTable.nftMetadata,
      createdAt:          nationalEmbassiesTable.createdAt,
    })
    .from(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .leftJoin(citizensTable, eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.citizenId, citizensTable.id));

  res.json(embassies);
});

// ── GET /api/embassies/:id ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

router.get("/embassies/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid embassy ID" }); return; }

  const [embassy] = await db
    .select()
    .from(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!embassy) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Embassy not found" }); return; }
  res.json(embassy);
});

// ── POST /api/embassies ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

router.post("/embassies", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CreateEmbassyBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, gpsLat, gpsLng, address } = parsed.data;

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" }); return; }

  const [existing] = await db
    .select({ id: nationalEmbassiesTable.id })
    .from(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.citizenId, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing) { res.status(409).json({ error: "Посольство уже зарегистрировано" }); return; }

  const nftMetadata = JSON.stringify({
    type: "Ambassador", citizen: citizen.name, citizenId,
    lat: gpsLat, lng: gpsLng, issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(), network: "TimeNation",
  });

  const [embassy] = await db
    .insert(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .values({ citizenId, gpsLat, gpsLng, address: address ?? null, status: "active", nftMetadata })
    .returning();

  const ambassadorRanks = ["Странник", "Созидатель"];
  if (ambassadorRanks.includes(citizen.rank)) {
    await db.update(citizensTable).set({ rank: "Посол" }).where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  }

  req.log.info({ citizenId, embassyId: embassy!.id }, "Embassy registered");
  res.status(201).json(embassy);
});

// ── PATCH /api/embassies/:id/activate ────────────────────────────────────────

router.patch("/embassies/:id/activate", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid ID" }); return; }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .set({ nodeActive: true })
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  if (!updated) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Embassy not found" }); return; }
  res.json(updated);
});

// ── PATCH /api/embassies/:id/node-ip — установить IP ноды ────────────────────

router.patch("/embassies/:id/node-ip", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid ID" }); return; }

  const parsed = SetNodeIpBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .set({ nodeIp: parsed.data.nodeIp, nodeStatus: "pending_verification" })
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  if (!updated) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Embassy not found" }); return; }
  req.log.info({ embassyId: id, nodeIp: parsed.data.nodeIp }, "Node IP set");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ── GET /api/embassies/:id/node-token — генерация QR-токена ──────────────────

router.get("/embassies/:id/node-token", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid ID" }); return; }

  const [embassy] = await db
    .select()
    .from(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!embassy) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Embassy not found" }); return; }

  // Generate crypto-random 48-byte token (96 hex chars)
  const token       = randomBytes(48).toString("hex");
  const expiresAt   = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000); // 10 minutes

  await db
    .update(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .set({ verificationQrToken: token, tokenExpiresAt: expiresAt })
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, id));

  // QR payload: JSON with token + embassy id so the mobile app knows which embassy to verify
  const qrPayload = JSON.stringify({ embassyId: id, token, lat: embassy.gpsLat, lng: embassy.gpsLng });

  req.log.info({ embassyId: id }, "Node QR token generated");
  res.json({
    token,
    qrPayload,
    expiresAt:    expiresAt.toISOString(),
    expiresInSec: 600,
    embassyId:    id,
    gpsLat:       embassy.gpsLat,
    gpsLng:       embassy.gpsLng,
  });
});

// ── POST /api/embassies/verify-location — мобильная верификация ───────────────

router.post("/embassies/verify-location", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = VerifyLocationBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { token, phoneLat, phoneLng } = parsed.data;

  // Find embassy with matching token
  const [embassy] = await db
    .select()
    .from(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.verificationQrToken, token))
    .limit(1);

  if (!embassy) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Токен не найден или уже использован" });
    return;
  }

  // Check token expiry
  if (!embassy.tokenExpiresAt || new Date() > embassy.tokenExpiresAt) {
    res.status(410).json({ error: "Токен истёк. Сгенерируйте новый QR-код." });
    return;
  }

  // Check GPS proximity (≤15 m)
  const distMetres = distanceM(embassy.gpsLat, embassy.gpsLng, phoneLat, phoneLng);
  const PROXIMITY_M = 15;

  if (distMetres > PROXIMITY_M) {
    res.status(422).json({
      error: `Слишком далеко от ноды: ${distMetres.toFixed(0)} м (допустимо ≤${PROXIMITY_M} м)`,
      distanceM: Math.round(distMetres),
    });
    return;
  }

  // All checks pass — activate node and clear token
  const [updated] = await db
    .update(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .set({
      isHardwareVerified:  true,
      nodeStatus:          "online",
      nodeActive:          true,
      verificationQrToken: null,
      tokenExpiresAt:      null,
      lastValidatedAt:     new Date(),
      anomalyLog:          null,
    })
    .where(eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, embassy.id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info(
    { embassyId: embassy.id, distanceM: distMetres.toFixed(0) },
    "Embassy node hardware-verified — node ONLINE"
  );

  res.json({
    success:     true,
    embassyId:   embassy.id,
    nodeStatus:  "online",
    distanceM:   Math.round(distMetres),
    message:     "Нода верифицирована! Начисление Дарминов за аптайм активировано.",
  });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/emission.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { getDailyApprovedTotal, getQueueSize, DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT } from "../services/emissionService";

const router: IRouter = Router();

// GET /api/emission/status — дневной остаток лимита и FIFO-очередь
router.get("/emission/status", async (_req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const [approved, queueSize] = await Promise.all([
    getDailyApprovedTotal(),
    getQueueSize(),
  ]);

  res.json({
    dailyLimit: DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT,
    approvedToday: approved,
    remaining: Math.max(0, DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT - approved),
    queueSize,
  });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/government.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter, type Request, type Response, type NextFunction } from "express";
import { eq, desc, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { timeAuditLogsTable, aiSystemPromptsTable, citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const router: IRouter = Router();

// ── Government access middleware ──────────────────────────────────────────────

const GOV_PASSWORD = process.env.GOV_ACCESS_PASSWORD ?? "";

function govAuth(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  const raw = req.headers["x-gov-password"];
  const provided = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
  if (!GOV_PASSWORD) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "GOV_ACCESS_PASSWORD не настроен на сервере" });
    return;
  }
  if (!provided || provided !== GOV_PASSWORD) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Доступ запрещён — неверный пароль Министерства" });
    return;
  }
  next();
}

// ── Default system prompt ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Ты — Главный ИИ-Аудитор Времени Министерства Труда TimeNation.
Твоя задача — рассчитать нормативное время выполнения задачи в минутах (1 минута = 1 Дармин) на основе описания работы.

Базовые эталоны оцифровки:
- Разработка / IT: Скрипты/Багфиксы 30–90 мин, Смарт-контракты/Архитектура 180–240+ мин
- Дизайн / Креатив: Графика/Баннеры 45–90 мин, Иллюстрации/3D 120–180 мин
- Тексты / Аналитика: Копирайтинг 20 мин/1к знаков, Аудит/Переводы 30 мин/страница
- Юридические услуги: Договоры/Соглашения 60–120 мин, Консультации 30–60 мин
- Образование: Урок/Лекция 60 мин, Курс из 10 уроков 600–900 мин

Отвечай ТОЛЬКО строгим JSON без markdown-обёртки:
{ "estimated_minutes": number, "complexity_level": "low"|"medium"|"high", "justification": "строка на русском" }`;

// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function getSystemPrompt(): Promise<string> {
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(aiSystemPromptsTable)
    .where(eq(aiSystemPromptsTable.module, "time_auditor"))
    .limit(1);
  if (rows.length > 0) return rows[0].promptText;
  // Seed default on first call
  await db.insert(aiSystemPromptsTable).values({
    module: "time_auditor",
    promptText: DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
  });
  return DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT;
}

function calcDeviation(estimated: number, actual: number): number {
  return Math.abs((actual - estimated) / estimated) * 100;
}

// ── Routes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// Verify government password (used by frontend to unlock the UI)
router.post("/government/auth/verify", (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const { password } = req.body as { password?: string };
  if (!GOV_PASSWORD) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "GOV_ACCESS_PASSWORD не настроен на сервере" });
    return;
  }
  if (password === GOV_PASSWORD) {
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } else {
    res.status(403).json({ ok: false, error: "Неверный пароль" });
  }
});

// POST /government/audit/task — call Gemini, save log
router.post("/government/audit/task", govAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const bodySchema = z.object({
    taskDescription: z.string().min(5),
    citizenId: z.number().int().optional(),
  });
  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверные данные", details: parsed.error.issues });
    return;
  }
  const { taskDescription, citizenId } = parsed.data;

  const apiKey = process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "GOOGLE_API_KEY не настроен" });
    return;
  }

  try {
    const systemPrompt = await getSystemPrompt();
    const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
      model: "gemini-2.0-flash",
      systemInstruction: systemPrompt,
    });

    const result = await model.generateContent(
      `Задача для аудита:\n${taskDescription}`
    );
    const raw = result.response.text().trim();

    // Strip possible markdown code block
    const clean = raw.replace(/^```(?:json)?\n?/, "").replace(/\n?```$/, "").trim();

    let parsed2: { estimated_minutes: number; complexity_level: string; justification: string };
    try {
      parsed2 = JSON.parse(clean) as typeof parsed2;
    } catch {
      req.log.error({ raw }, "Gemini returned non-JSON");
      res.status(502).json({ error: "ИИ вернул неверный формат", raw });
      return;
    }

    const [row] = await db
      .insert(timeAuditLogsTable)
      .values({
        citizenId: citizenId ?? null,
        taskDescription,
        estimatedMinutes: parsed2.estimated_minutes,
        complexityLevel: parsed2.complexity_level,
        justification: parsed2.justification,
        requiresCalibration: 0,
      })
      .returning();

    res.json({ log: row, aiResult: parsed2 });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "government audit task error");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Ошибка вызова ИИ-аудитора" });
  }
});

// PATCH /government/audit/logs/:id — record actual time, compute deviation
router.patch("/government/audit/logs/:id", govAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const id = parseInt(String(req.params.id));
  if (!id) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверный ID" }); return; }

  const bodySchema = z.object({
    actualMinutes: z.number().positive(),
    moderatedByName: z.string().optional(),
  });
  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверные данные" }); return; }

  const { actualMinutes, moderatedByName } = parsed.data;

  const existing = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeAuditLogsTable)
    .where(eq(timeAuditLogsTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);
  if (existing.length === 0) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Запись не найдена" }); return; }

  const deviation = calcDeviation(existing[0].estimatedMinutes, actualMinutes);
  const requiresCalibration = deviation > 20 ? 1 : 0;

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(timeAuditLogsTable)
    .set({
      actualMinutes,
      deviationPercent: parseFloat(deviation.toFixed(2)),
      requiresCalibration,
      moderatedByName: moderatedByName ?? null,
      moderatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(timeAuditLogsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  res.json({ log: updated });
});

// GET /government/audit/logs
router.get("/government/audit/logs", govAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const calibrationOnly = req.query.calibrationOnly === "true";
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeAuditLogsTable)
    .where(
      calibrationOnly
        ? eq(timeAuditLogsTable.requiresCalibration, 1)
        : sql`1=1`
    )
    .orderBy(desc(timeAuditLogsTable.createdAt))
    .limit(200);
  res.json({ logs: rows });
});

// GET /government/system-prompt
router.get("/government/system-prompt", govAuth, async (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const prompt = await getSystemPrompt();
  res.json({ promptText: prompt });
});

// PUT /government/system-prompt
router.put("/government/system-prompt", govAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const bodySchema = z.object({
    promptText: z.string().min(20),
    updatedByName: z.string().optional(),
  });
  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Текст промта слишком короткий" }); return; }

  const existing = await db
    .select()
    .from(aiSystemPromptsTable)
    .where(eq(aiSystemPromptsTable.module, "time_auditor"))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing.length > 0) {
    await db
      .update(aiSystemPromptsTable)
      .set({
        promptText: parsed.data.promptText,
        updatedByName: parsed.data.updatedByName ?? null,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(aiSystemPromptsTable.module, "time_auditor"));
  } else {
    await db.insert(aiSystemPromptsTable).values({
      module: "time_auditor",
      promptText: parsed.data.promptText,
      updatedByName: parsed.data.updatedByName ?? null,
    });
  }

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// GET /government/citizens — list with govRole
router.get("/government/citizens", govAuth, async (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      id: citizensTable.id,
      name: citizensTable.name,
      citizenNumber: citizensTable.citizenNumber,
      govRole: citizensTable.govRole,
      meritScore: citizensTable.meritScore,
      rank: citizensTable.rank,
    })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .orderBy(citizensTable.id)
    .limit(500);
  res.json({ citizens: rows });
});

// PATCH /government/citizens/:id/gov-role
router.patch("/government/citizens/:id/gov-role", govAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const id = parseInt(String(req.params.id));
  if (!id) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверный ID" }); return; }

  const bodySchema = z.object({
    govRole: z.enum(["citizen", "labor_officer", "gov_admin"]),
  });
  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверная роль" }); return; }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ govRole: parsed.data.govRole })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, id));

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/payments.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const router: IRouter = Router();

const STRIPE_SECRET_KEY = process.env["STRIPE_SECRET_KEY"];
const STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET = process.env["STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET"];

// ── Discount engine (shared logic, server is source of truth) ─────────────────

export function calcDarminDiscount(amount: number): number {
  if (amount > 10000) return 10;
  if (amount >= 5000) return 8;
  if (amount >= 1000) return 7;
  if (amount >= 500)  return 6;
  if (amount >= 100)  return 5;
  return 0;
}

export function calcDarminPrice(amount: number): {
  discountPct: number;
  baseEur: number;
  totalEur: number;
  totalCents: number;
  eurPerDarmin: number;
} {
  const discountPct = calcDarminDiscount(amount);
  const baseEur = amount;
  const totalEur = parseFloat((amount * (1 - discountPct / 100)).toFixed(2));
  const totalCents = Math.round(totalEur * 100);
  const eurPerDarmin = parseFloat((totalEur / amount).toFixed(4));
  return { discountPct, baseEur, totalEur, totalCents, eurPerDarmin };
}

// ── Legacy packages (kept for backward compat) ────────────────────────────────

export const MINUTE_PACKAGES = [
  { id: "pack_100",  minutes: 100,  price: 95,  currency: "eur", label: "100 Дарминов",  discountPct: 5 },
  { id: "pack_500",  minutes: 500,  price: 470, currency: "eur", label: "500 Дарминов",  discountPct: 6 },
  { id: "pack_1000", minutes: 1000, price: 930, currency: "eur", label: "1000 Дарминов", discountPct: 7 },
] as const;

router.get("/payments/packages", (_req, res) => {
  res.json(MINUTE_PACKAGES);
});

// ── Dynamic checkout (new) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CustomCheckoutBody = z.object({
  citizenId:  z.number().int().positive(),
  amount:     z.number().int().min(1).max(1_000_000),
  successUrl: z.string().url(),
  cancelUrl:  z.string().url(),
});

router.post("/payments/checkout-custom", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  if (!STRIPE_SECRET_KEY) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "Фиатный шлюз не настроен. Установите STRIPE_SECRET_KEY." });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = CustomCheckoutBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { citizenId, amount, successUrl, cancelUrl } = parsed.data;

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" });
    return;
  }

  const { discountPct, totalEur, totalCents, eurPerDarmin } = calcDarminPrice(amount);

  if (totalCents < 50) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Минимальная сумма — 0.50 €" });
    return;
  }

  const Stripe = (await import("stripe")).default;
  const stripe = new Stripe(STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);

  const discountLabel = discountPct > 0 ? ` (скидка ${discountPct}%)` : "";
  const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    payment_method_types: ["card"],
    line_items: [
      {
        price_data: {
          currency: "eur",
          product_data: {
            name: `${amount} Дарминов — TimeNation${discountLabel}`,
            description:
              `SaaS compute-time credits (${amount} units, ${eurPerDarmin} €/unit) for AI media synthesis ` +
              `on TimeNation infrastructure. Non-monetary software utility tokens. ` +
              `Not a security, financial instrument, or real-world asset.`,
          },
          unit_amount: totalCents,
        },
        quantity: 1,
      },
    ],
    mode: "payment",
    success_url: successUrl,
    cancel_url: cancelUrl,
    metadata: {
      citizenId: String(citizenId),
      minutes:   String(amount),
      discountPct: String(discountPct),
      totalEur:  String(totalEur),
    },
    client_reference_id: String(citizenId),
  });

  logger.info(
    { citizenId, amount, discountPct, totalEur, sessionId: session.id },
    "Stripe custom checkout session created"
  );

  res.json({ url: session.url, sessionId: session.id });
});

// ── Legacy fixed-package checkout ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const CheckoutBody = z.object({
  citizenId:  z.number().int().positive(),
  packageId:  z.string(),
  successUrl: z.string(),
  cancelUrl:  z.string(),
});

router.post("/payments/checkout", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  if (!STRIPE_SECRET_KEY) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "Фиатный шлюз не настроен. Установите STRIPE_SECRET_KEY." });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = CheckoutBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { citizenId, packageId, successUrl, cancelUrl } = parsed.data;

  const pack = MINUTE_PACKAGES.find((p) => p.id === packageId);
  if (!pack) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: `Пакет "${packageId}" не найден` });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  const Stripe = (await import("stripe")).default;
  const stripe = new Stripe(STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);

  const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    payment_method_types: ["card"],
    line_items: [
      {
        price_data: {
          currency: pack.currency,
          product_data: {
            name: "Compute-Time Utility Token — TimeNation SaaS Infrastructure",
            description:
              `SaaS cloud-compute access credits (${pack.minutes} units) for AI media synthesis ` +
              `on TimeNation infrastructure. Non-monetary software utility tokens. ` +
              `Not a security, financial instrument, or real-world asset.`,
          },
          unit_amount: pack.price * 100,
        },
        quantity: 1,
      },
    ],
    mode: "payment",
    success_url: successUrl,
    cancel_url: cancelUrl,
    metadata: {
      citizenId: String(citizenId),
      packageId,
      minutes: String(pack.minutes),
    },
    client_reference_id: String(citizenId),
  });

  logger.info(
    { citizenId, packageId, sessionId: session.id },
    "Stripe checkout session created"
  );

  res.json({ url: session.url, sessionId: session.id });
});

// ── Static pages ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

router.get("/payments/success", (_req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/html");

});
