  res.send(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Отменено</title><style>body{margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:#0D1B2A;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;color:#E8EDF2}.card{text-align:center;padding:40px;max-width:380px}.icon{font-size:64px;margin-bottom:16px}.title{font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:#6B7E93;margin:0 0 12px}.sub{font-size:15px;opacity:.7;margin:0 0 24px}</style></head><body><div class="card"><div class="icon">❌</div><h1 class="title">Платёж отменён</h1><p class="sub">Вернись в приложение и попробуй снова.</p></div></body></html>`);
});

// ── Webhook ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

router.post("/payments/webhook", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  if (!STRIPE_SECRET_KEY || !STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "Webhook не настроен" });
    return;
  }

  const sig = req.headers["stripe-signature"];
  if (!sig) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing stripe-signature header" });
    return;
  }

  const Stripe = (await import("stripe")).default;
  const stripe = new Stripe(STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);

  let event: ReturnType<typeof stripe.webhooks.constructEvent>;
  try {
    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(
      req.body as string | Buffer,
      sig,
      STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET
    );
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Stripe webhook signature verification failed");
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Webhook signature invalid" });
    return;
  }

  if (event.type === "checkout.session.completed") {
    const session = event.data.object as {
      metadata?: { citizenId?: string; minutes?: string };
      id: string;
    };
    const citizenId = parseInt(session.metadata?.citizenId ?? "", 10);
    const minutes   = parseInt(session.metadata?.minutes   ?? "", 10);

    if (!isNaN(citizenId) && !isNaN(minutes) && minutes > 0) {
      const [citizen] = await db
        .select()
        .from(citizensTable)
        .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
        .limit(1);

      if (citizen) {
        await db
          .update(citizensTable)
          .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes + minutes })
          .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

        logger.info(
          { citizenId, minutes, stripeSessionId: session.id },
          "Minutes credited after Stripe payment"
        );
      }
    }
  }

  res.json({ received: true });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/sessions.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { eq, and, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable, sessionsTable, transactionsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { StartSessionBody } from "@workspace/api-zod";
import { logTransactionToSheets } from "../lib/googleSheets";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "../services/telegramService";
import { applyMeritAfterSession } from "../services/meritocracyService";
import { calcTreasuryTax, TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID } from "../services/videoStorage";
import { autoRepayFromService } from "../services/timeBankService";

const router: IRouter = Router();

router.post("/sessions", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = StartSessionBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { fromCitizenId, toCitizenId } = parsed.data;

  if (fromCitizenId === toCitizenId) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Cannot transfer time to yourself" });
    return;
  }

  const [fromCitizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, fromCitizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!fromCitizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Sender citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  const [toCitizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, toCitizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!toCitizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Recipient citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  if (fromCitizen.balanceMinutes <= 1) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: "Недостаточно минут. Родовая Минута гражданства не может быть передана.",
    });
    return;
  }

  const activeSession = await db
    .select()
    .from(sessionsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(sessionsTable.fromCitizenId, fromCitizenId),
        eq(sessionsTable.status, "active")
      )
    )
    .limit(1);

  if (activeSession.length > 0) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Citizen already has an active session" });
    return;
  }

  const [session] = await db
    .insert(sessionsTable)
    .values({ fromCitizenId, toCitizenId, status: "active" })
    .returning();

  req.log.info(
    { sessionId: session.id, fromCitizenId, toCitizenId },
    "TiMETR session started"
  );

  res.status(201).json({
    ...session,
    startedAt: session.startedAt.toISOString(),
    stoppedAt: null,
    durationSeconds: null,
    minutesTransferred: null,
  });
});

router.post("/sessions/:id/stop", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const raw = Array.isArray(req.params.id) ? req.params.id[0] : req.params.id;
  const id = parseInt(raw ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid session ID" });
    return;
  }

  const [session] = await db
    .select()
    .from(sessionsTable)
    .where(eq(sessionsTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!session) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Session not found" });
    return;
  }

  if (session.status !== "active") {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Session is already stopped" });
    return;
  }

  const stoppedAt = new Date();
  const durationSeconds = Math.floor(
    (stoppedAt.getTime() - session.startedAt.getTime()) / 1000
  );

  const minutesTransferred = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(durationSeconds / 60));

  const [fromCitizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, session.fromCitizenId))
    .limit(1);

  const [toCitizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, session.toCitizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!fromCitizen || !toCitizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  const actualMinutes = Math.min(minutesTransferred, fromCitizen.balanceMinutes - 1);
  // 2% казначейский налог. Точная дробь (например 1 Д → 0.02 Д).
  const treasuryTax = actualMinutes > 0 ? calcTreasuryTax(actualMinutes) : 0;
  const toReceives = Math.max(0, actualMinutes - treasuryTax);

  const [updatedSession] = await db
    .update(sessionsTable)
    .set({
      stoppedAt,
      durationSeconds,
      minutesTransferred: actualMinutes,
      status: "completed",
    })
    .where(eq(sessionsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({
      balanceMinutes: fromCitizen.balanceMinutes - actualMinutes,
      totalGiven: fromCitizen.totalGiven + actualMinutes,
    })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, session.fromCitizenId));

  // Автопогашение долга в Банке Времени: если у получателя есть активный долг,
  // направляем заработанные минуты на погашение (остаток идёт на личный баланс).
  const autoRepaid = await autoRepayFromService(session.toCitizenId, toReceives).catch(() => 0);
  const netToReceives = toReceives - autoRepaid;

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({
      balanceMinutes: toCitizen.balanceMinutes + netToReceives,
      totalReceived: toCitizen.totalReceived + toReceives, // totalReceived = полная сумма, без вычета долга
    })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, session.toCitizenId));

  // Зачислить налог Казначейству (id=1)
  if (treasuryTax > 0 && TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID !== session.fromCitizenId && TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID !== session.toCitizenId) {
    try {
      await db.execute(
        sql`UPDATE citizens SET balance_minutes = balance_minutes + ${treasuryTax} WHERE id = ${TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID}`
      );
    } catch { /* казначейство необязательно в dev */ }
  }

  const [transaction] = await db
    .insert(transactionsTable)
    .values({
      sessionId: id,
      fromCitizenId: session.fromCitizenId,
      toCitizenId: session.toCitizenId,
      fromCitizenName: fromCitizen.name,
      toCitizenName: toCitizen.name,
      minutesTransferred: actualMinutes,
      durationSeconds,
    })
    .returning();

  req.log.info(
    {
      sessionId: id,
      fromCitizenName: fromCitizen.name,
      toCitizenName: toCitizen.name,
      minutesTransferred: actualMinutes,
      durationSeconds,
    },
    "TiMETR session completed"
  );

  const sheetsLogged = await logTransactionToSheets({
    sessionId: transaction.id,
    fromCitizenId: transaction.fromCitizenId,
    toCitizenId: transaction.toCitizenId,
    fromCitizenName: transaction.fromCitizenName,
    toCitizenName: transaction.toCitizenName,
    minutesTransferred: transaction.minutesTransferred,
    durationSeconds: transaction.durationSeconds,
    createdAt: transaction.createdAt.toISOString(),
  });

  const registryStatus = sheetsLogged
    ? "✅ Закреплено в Казначействе"
    : "⚠️ Реестр временно недоступен";

  const taxNote = treasuryTax > 0 ? `\n🏦 Налог Казначейства: *${treasuryTax.toFixed(2)} Д* (2%)` : "";

  const notifyFrom =
    `🏛 *Казначейство TimeNation*\n\n` +
    `Транзакция завершена: *${actualMinutes.toFixed(2)} Д* → *${toCitizen.name}*.${taxNote}\n\n` +
    `${registryStatus}`;

  const notifyTo =
    `🏛 *Казначейство TimeNation*\n\n` +
    `Получено *${toReceives.toFixed(2)} Д* от *${fromCitizen.name}*.${taxNote}\n\n` +
    `💰 Ваш новый баланс: *${(toCitizen.balanceMinutes + toReceives).toFixed(2)} Д*\n\n` +
    `${registryStatus}`;

  const meritResult = await applyMeritAfterSession(session.fromCitizenId, actualMinutes).catch((err) => {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Merit calculation failed");
    return null;
  });

  let rankMessage = "";
  if (meritResult) {
    const { oldRank, newRank, meritGained, newScore } = meritResult;
    if (newRank !== oldRank) {
      rankMessage = `\n\n📜 *Статус изменён!* Вы теперь *${newRank}*. Ваше влияние в TimeNation растёт.`;
    } else {
      rankMessage = `\n\nВес +${meritGained.toFixed(2)}. Текущий ранг: *${newRank}* (${newScore} Merit)`;
    }
  }

  await Promise.all([
    sendTransactionNotification(session.fromCitizenId, notifyFrom + rankMessage),
    sendTransactionNotification(session.toCitizenId, notifyTo),
  ]);

  res.json({
    session: {
      ...updatedSession,
      startedAt: updatedSession.startedAt.toISOString(),
      stoppedAt: updatedSession.stoppedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
    },
    transaction: {
      ...transaction,
      createdAt: transaction.createdAt.toISOString(),
    },
    sheetsLogged,
  });
});

router.get("/sessions/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const raw = Array.isArray(req.params.id) ? req.params.id[0] : req.params.id;
  const id = parseInt(raw ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid session ID" });
    return;
  }

  const [session] = await db
    .select()
    .from(sessionsTable)
    .where(eq(sessionsTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!session) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Session not found" });
    return;
  }

  res.json({
    ...session,
    startedAt: session.startedAt.toISOString(),
    stoppedAt: session.stoppedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
  });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/timeBank.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { z } from "zod/v4";
import {
  getTimeBankStatus,
  borrowFromBank,
  repayFromBalance,
  repayLoan,
} from "../services/timeBankService";

const router: IRouter = Router();

// GET /api/timebank/status?citizenId=...
router.get("/timebank/status", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = parseInt(String(req.query["citizenId"] ?? ""), 10);
  if (isNaN(citizenId)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" });
    return;
  }
  try {
    const status = await getTimeBankStatus(citizenId);
    res.json(status);
  } catch (e) {
    req.log.error(e);
    res.status(404).json({ error: (e as Error).message });
  }
});

// POST /api/timebank/borrow  { citizenId, amount }
router.post("/timebank/borrow", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const schema = z.object({
    citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
    amount: z.number().positive().max(480),
  });
  const parsed = schema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверные параметры", issues: parsed.error.issues });
    return;
  }
  try {
    await borrowFromBank(parsed.data.citizenId, parsed.data.amount);
    const status = await getTimeBankStatus(parsed.data.citizenId);
    res.json({ ok: true, ...status });
  } catch (e) {
    req.log.warn({ err: (e as Error).message }, "timebank borrow rejected");
    res.status(400).json({ error: (e as Error).message });
  }
});

// POST /api/timebank/repay  { citizenId, amount, loanId? }
// FIFO-погашение по всем активным займам. loanId опционален (для обратной совместимости).
router.post("/timebank/repay", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const schema = z.object({
    citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
    amount: z.number().positive(),
    loanId: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
  });
  const parsed = schema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Неверные параметры", issues: parsed.error.issues });
    return;
  }
  try {
    if (parsed.data.loanId !== undefined) {
      await repayLoan(parsed.data.citizenId, parsed.data.loanId, parsed.data.amount);
    } else {
      await repayFromBalance(parsed.data.citizenId, parsed.data.amount);
    }
    const status = await getTimeBankStatus(parsed.data.citizenId);
    res.json({ ok: true, ...status });
  } catch (e) {
    req.log.warn({ err: (e as Error).message }, "timebank repay rejected");
    res.status(400).json({ error: (e as Error).message });
  }
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/timeline.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { eq, and, gte, lte } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable, timelineAnchorsTable, timelineCapsulesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logTimelineAnchorToSheets } from "../lib/googleSheets";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "../services/telegramService";

const router: IRouter = Router();

const AnchorBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  timelineCoord: z
    .string()
    .regex(
      /^\d{12}$/,
      "timelineCoord must be 12 digits: YYYYMMDDHHmm"
    ),
});

function validateCoord(coord: string): string | null {
  const year = parseInt(coord.slice(0, 4), 10);
  const month = parseInt(coord.slice(4, 6), 10);
  const day = parseInt(coord.slice(6, 8), 10);
  const hour = parseInt(coord.slice(8, 10), 10);
  const minute = parseInt(coord.slice(10, 12), 10);

  if (year < 1 || year > 9999) return "Год должен быть от 1 до 9999";
  if (month < 1 || month > 12) return "Месяц должен быть от 01 до 12";
  if (day < 1 || day > 31) return "День должен быть от 01 до 31";
  if (hour < 0 || hour > 23) return "Час должен быть от 00 до 23";
  if (minute < 0 || minute > 59) return "Минута должна быть от 00 до 59";
  return null;
}

function formatCoordHuman(coord: string): string {
  const year = coord.slice(0, 4).replace(/^0+/, "") || "1";
  const month = coord.slice(4, 6);
  const day = coord.slice(6, 8);
  const hour = coord.slice(8, 10);
  const min = coord.slice(10, 12);
  return `${day}.${month}.${year} ${hour}:${min}`;
}

router.post("/timeline/anchor", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = AnchorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { citizenId, timelineCoord } = parsed.data;

  const validErr = validateCoord(timelineCoord);
  if (validErr) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: validErr });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  const transferableBalance = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1;
  if (transferableBalance <= 0) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: "Недостаточно минут. Родовая Минута не может быть использована для закрепления координаты.",
    });
    return;
  }

  const [existing] = await db
    .select()
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, timelineCoord))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing) {
    if (existing.citizenId === citizenId) {
      res.status(409).json({ error: "Эта координата уже закреплена за вами" });
    } else {
      res.status(409).json({ error: "Эта координата уже занята другим гражданином" });
    }
    return;
  }

  const [anchor] = await db
    .insert(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .values({ timelineCoord, citizenId, status: "active" })
    .returning();

  const [updatedCitizen] = await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - 1 })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ citizenId, timelineCoord }, "Timeline anchor created");

  const sheetsLogged = await logTimelineAnchorToSheets({
    timelineCoord,
    citizenId,
    citizenName: citizen.name,
    createdAt: anchor.createdAt.toISOString(),
  });

  const humanCoord = formatCoordHuman(timelineCoord);
  const tgMsg =
    `🕰 *TimeNation — Таймлайн*\n\n` +
    `⏳ Координата *${humanCoord}* успешно зафиксирована за вашим родом навсегда!\n\n` +
    `💰 Остаток баланса: *${updatedCitizen.balanceMinutes} мин*`;

  await sendTransactionNotification(citizenId, tgMsg).catch(() => {});

  res.status(201).json({
    anchor: { ...anchor, createdAt: anchor.createdAt.toISOString() },
    newBalance: updatedCitizen.balanceMinutes,
    sheetsLogged,
  });
});

router.get("/timeline/occupied", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const rawYear = req.query["year"];
  const rawMonth = req.query["month"];
  const rawDay = req.query["day"];

  const year = rawYear ? String(rawYear).padStart(4, "0") : null;
  const month = rawMonth ? String(rawMonth).padStart(2, "0") : null;
  const day = rawDay ? String(rawDay).padStart(2, "0") : null;

  let coordMin: string;
  let coordMax: string;

  if (year && month && day) {
    coordMin = `${year}${month}${day}0000`;
    coordMax = `${year}${month}${day}2359`;
  } else if (year && month) {
    coordMin = `${year}${month}010000`;
    coordMax = `${year}${month}312359`;
  } else if (year) {
    coordMin = `${year}010100000`;
    coordMax = `${year}12312359`;
  } else {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "At least year is required" });
    return;
  }

  const anchors = await db
    .select()
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        gte(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, coordMin),
        lte(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, coordMax)
      )
    );

  res.json(
    anchors.map((a) => ({
      timelineCoord: a.timelineCoord,
      citizenId: a.citizenId,
      createdAt: a.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }))
  );
});

// ── GET /timeline/my-anchors?citizenId=X ─────────────────────────────────────
// Returns all timeline anchors owned by a citizen, sorted chronologically.
router.get("/timeline/my-anchors", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = parseInt(String(req.query["citizenId"] ?? ""), 10);
  if (isNaN(citizenId) || citizenId <= 0) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" });
    return;
  }

  const anchors = await db
    .select()
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.citizenId, citizenId))
    .orderBy(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord);

  res.json(
    anchors.map((a) => ({
      timelineCoord: a.timelineCoord,
      status: a.status,
      createdAt: a.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }))
  );
});

// ── POST /timeline/capsule ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Saves a semantic capsule (free-text meaning) for a citizen's timeline anchor.
const CapsuleBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  timelineCoord: z.string().regex(/^\d{12}$/, "timelineCoord must be 12 digits"),
  text: z.string().min(1).max(1000),
});

router.post("/timeline/capsule", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CapsuleBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Неверные данные" });
    return;
  }
  const { citizenId, timelineCoord, text } = parsed.data;

  const citizen = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1)
    .then((r) => r[0]);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" });
    return;
  }

  const anchor = await db
    .select({ id: timelineAnchorsTable.id })
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(and(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.citizenId, citizenId), eq(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, timelineCoord)))
    .limit(1)
    .then((r) => r[0]);

  if (!anchor) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Эта координата вам не принадлежит" });
    return;
  }

  const [capsule] = await db
    .insert(timelineCapsulesTable)
    .values({ citizenId, timelineCoord, text })
    .returning();

  res.status(201).json({ id: capsule!.id, timelineCoord, createdAt: capsule!.createdAt.toISOString() });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/transactions.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { desc, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { transactionsTable, citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";

const router: IRouter = Router();

router.get("/transactions", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenIdRaw = req.query["citizenId"];
  const limitRaw = req.query["limit"];

  const limit = limitRaw ? parseInt(String(limitRaw), 10) : 50;
  const citizenId = citizenIdRaw ? parseInt(String(citizenIdRaw), 10) : null;

  let rows;
  if (citizenId && !isNaN(citizenId)) {
    rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(transactionsTable)
      .where(
        sql`${transactionsTable.fromCitizenId} = ${citizenId} OR ${transactionsTable.toCitizenId} = ${citizenId}`
      )
      .orderBy(desc(transactionsTable.createdAt))
      .limit(Math.min(limit, 200));
  } else {
    rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(transactionsTable)
      .orderBy(desc(transactionsTable.createdAt))
      .limit(Math.min(limit, 200));
  }

  res.json(
    rows.map((t) => ({ ...t, createdAt: t.createdAt.toISOString() }))
  );
});

router.get("/stats", async (_req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizens = await db.select().from(citizensTable);
  const allTransactions = await db
    .select()
    .from(transactionsTable)
    .orderBy(desc(transactionsTable.createdAt))
    .limit(200);

  const recentTransactions = allTransactions.slice(0, 10);

  const totalMinutesAll = allTransactions.reduce(
    (sum, t) => sum + t.minutesTransferred,
    0
  );

  const topGivers = [...citizens]
    .sort((a, b) => b.totalGiven - a.totalGiven)
    .slice(0, 5)
    .map((c) => ({
      ...c,
      transferableBalance: c.balanceMinutes - 1,
      lastActivity: c.lastActivity?.toISOString() ?? null,
      createdAt: c.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }));

  const topReceivers = [...citizens]
    .sort((a, b) => b.totalReceived - a.totalReceived)
    .slice(0, 5)
    .map((c) => ({
      ...c,
      transferableBalance: c.balanceMinutes - 1,
      lastActivity: c.lastActivity?.toISOString() ?? null,
      createdAt: c.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }));

  res.json({
    totalCitizens: citizens.length,
    activeCitizens: citizens.filter((c) => c.status === "active").length,
    totalTransactions: allTransactions.length,
    totalMinutesCirculated: totalMinutesAll,
    topGivers,
    topReceivers,
    recentTransactions: recentTransactions.map((t) => ({
      ...t,
      createdAt: t.createdAt.toISOString(),
    })),
  });
});

export default router;

} from "../services/tvService";

// ─── Style preview cache ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Key: `${style}:${seed}` → { buffer, expiresAt }
const stylePreviewCache = new Map<string, { buffer: Buffer; expiresAt: number }>();
const PREVIEW_TTL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 hours

const STYLE_PROMPTS: Record<string, string> = {
  space:     "deep space nebula cosmic stars dark blue amber glow cinematic 4k",
  cyberpunk: "cyberpunk neon city night rain black purple neon signs futuristic 4k",
  abstract:  "abstract art purple gold violet digital art swirling patterns 4k",
  nature:    "dark forest nature green mint ethereal misty atmospheric 4k",
  minimal:   "minimalist black white geometric clean lines elegant 4k",
};

// ─── GET /api/tv/styles/preview?style=X&seed=Y ───────────────────────────────
// Returns a 1280×720 JPEG thumbnail generated by Pollinations AI.
// Cached in-memory for 24h per style+seed combo.
router.get("/tv/styles/preview", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const style = (req.query["style"] as string | undefined) ?? "";
  const seed = ((req.query["seed"] as string | undefined) ?? "").slice(0, 200);

  if (!VISUAL_STYLES.includes(style as VisualStyle)) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: "Параметр style обязателен: space|cyberpunk|abstract|nature|minimal",
    });
    return;
  }

  const cacheKey = `${style}:${seed}`;
  const now = Date.now();
  const cached = stylePreviewCache.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > now) {
    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "image/jpeg");
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=86400");
    res.send(cached.buffer);
    return;
  }

  const basePrompt = STYLE_PROMPTS[style] ?? style;
  const fullPrompt = seed ? `${basePrompt}, ${seed}` : basePrompt;
  const url =
    `https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/${encodeURIComponent(fullPrompt)}` +
    `?width=1280&height=720&nologo=true&seed=42`;

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30_000);
  let imageRes: Response;
  try {
    imageRes = await fetch(url, { signal: controller.signal });
  } catch (err) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    req.log.warn({ err, style, seed }, "Style preview: Pollinations fetch error");
    res.status(502).json({ error: "Превью временно недоступно" });
    return;
  }
  clearTimeout(timer);

  if (!imageRes.ok) {
    req.log.warn({ status: imageRes.status, style }, "Style preview: Pollinations returned error");
    res.status(502).json({ error: "Превью временно недоступно" });
    return;
  }

  const buffer = Buffer.from(await imageRes.arrayBuffer());
  stylePreviewCache.set(cacheKey, { buffer, expiresAt: now + PREVIEW_TTL_MS });

  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "image/jpeg");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=86400");
  res.send(buffer);
});

// ─── Validation schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const BookSlotBody = z.object({
  slotId: z.number().int().positive(),
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  paymentType: z.enum(["fiat", "timeltr"]),
  successUrl: z.string().optional(),
  cancelUrl: z.string().optional(),
});

const VISUAL_STYLES = ["space", "cyberpunk", "abstract", "nature", "minimal"] as const;
type VisualStyle = typeof VISUAL_STYLES[number];

const CreateCapsuleBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  seedText: z.string().max(500).optional(),
  mode: z.enum(["a", "b"]).default("a"),
  renderPath: z.enum(["light", "heavy"]).default("light"),
  broadcastPolicy: z.enum(["echo", "noise", "private"]).default("noise"),
  contentState: z.enum(["filled", "empty"]).default("filled"),
  hasSovereignLink: z.boolean().default(false),
  // Shariah/anti-Gharar: visual style chosen BEFORE render makes service deterministic
  visualStyle: z.enum(VISUAL_STYLES).optional(),
});

const UpdateCapsuleBody = z.object({
  seedText: z.string().max(500).optional(),
  mode: z.enum(["a", "b"]).optional(),
  renderPath: z.enum(["light", "heavy"]).optional(),
  broadcastPolicy: z.enum(["echo", "noise", "private"]).optional(),
  contentState: z.enum(["filled", "empty"]).optional(),
  hasSovereignLink: z.boolean().optional(),
  visualStyle: z.enum(VISUAL_STYLES).optional(),
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/slots?date=YYYY-MM-DD ────────────────────────────────────────
// Returns all 1440 slots for a given date; generates them on first request.
router.get("/tv/slots", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const date = (req.query["date"] as string | undefined) ?? "";
  if (!isValidDate(date)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Параметр date обязателен в формате YYYY-MM-DD" });
    return;
  }

  await ensureSlotsForDate(date);

  const slots = await db
    .select({
      id: tvSlotsTable.id,
      date: tvSlotsTable.date,
      minuteIndex: tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex,
      slotType: tvSlotsTable.slotType,
      status: tvSlotsTable.status,
      ownerId: tvSlotsTable.ownerId,
      priceEur: tvSlotsTable.priceEur,
      timeltrBurnCost: tvSlotsTable.timeltrBurnCost,
      ownerName: citizensTable.name,
    })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .leftJoin(citizensTable, eq(tvSlotsTable.ownerId, citizensTable.id))
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.date, date))
    .orderBy(tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex);

  // Enrich with derived fields
  const enriched = slots.map((s) => ({
    ...s,
    isRetro: isRetroSlot(s.date, s.minuteIndex),
    hourLabel: String(Math.floor(s.minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0"),
    minLabel: String(s.minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0"),
  }));

  res.json({ date, count: enriched.length, slots: enriched });
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/slots/:id ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/tv/slots/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slot ID" }); return; }

  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }

  res.json({
    ...slot,
    isRetro: isRetroSlot(slot.date, slot.minuteIndex),
    pricing: BURN_MATRIX[slot.slotType as SlotType],
  });
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/slots/book ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// paymentType=fiat   → Stripe EUR checkout session
// paymentType=timeltr → instant hard burn from citizen balance
router.post("/tv/slots/book", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = BookSlotBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { slotId, citizenId, paymentType, successUrl, cancelUrl } = parsed.data;

  // Load slot
  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }
  if (slot.status !== "free") {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Слот уже забронирован" });
    return;
  }

  // Load citizen
  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  // Anti-consecutive guard
  const fingerprint = getFingerprint(req);
  const adjacent = await db
    .select({ id: tvSlotsTable.id })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(tvSlotsTable.date, slot.date),
        inArray(tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex, [slot.minuteIndex - 1, slot.minuteIndex + 1]),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.status, "booked"),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.fingerprintHash, fingerprint)
      )
    );

  if (adjacent.length > 0) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: "Нельзя бронировать соседние минуты подряд (anti-consecutive rule)",
    });
    return;
  }

  // ── TiMETR hard burn ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (paymentType === "timeltr") {
    const burnCost = slot.timeltrBurnCost;
    const treasuryTax = calcTreasuryTax(burnCost);
    const totalCost = burnCost + treasuryTax;
    const transferable = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1; // Родовая минута неприкосновенна
    if (totalCost > transferable) {
      res.status(400).json({
        error: `Недостаточно Дарминов. Нужно ${totalCost} Д (слот ${burnCost} Д + государственная пошлина ${treasuryTax} Д), доступно ${transferable} Д`,
      });
      return;
    }

    // Hard burn — списание с гражданина
    await db
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - totalCost })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

    // Казначейский налог — зачислить системному кошельку (id=1), если существует
    if (TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID !== citizenId) {
      try {
        await db
          .execute(
            sql`UPDATE citizens SET balance_minutes = balance_minutes + ${treasuryTax} WHERE id = ${TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID}`
          );
      } catch {
        /* казначейский аккаунт необязателен в dev */
      }
    }

    await db
      .update(tvSlotsTable)
      .set({
        status: "booked",
        ownerId: citizenId,
        paymentType: "timeltr",
        fingerprintHash: fingerprint,
      })
      .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));

    req.log.info({ citizenId, slotId, burnCost, treasuryTax, totalCost }, "TV slot booked via TiMETR burn");
    res.status(201).json({ ok: true, slotId, burnCost, treasuryTax, totalCost, paymentType: "timeltr" });
    return;
  }

  // ── Fiat (Stripe EUR) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (!STRIPE_SECRET_KEY) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "Stripe не настроен. Установите STRIPE_SECRET_KEY." });
    return;
  }

  const pricing = BURN_MATRIX[slot.slotType as SlotType];
  const finalSuccessUrl =
    successUrl ??
    `${req.protocol}://${req.get("host")}/api/tv/checkout/confirm?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}`;
  const finalCancelUrl = cancelUrl ?? `${req.protocol}://${req.get("host")}/api/tv/checkout/cancel`;

  const Stripe = (await import("stripe")).default;
  const stripe = new Stripe(STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);

  const hour = String(Math.floor(slot.minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const min = String(slot.minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");

  // Billing descriptor: SaaS / compute-utility framing (prevents merchant account suspension)
  const billingDescriptor =
    `Compute-Time Utility Token — TimeNation SaaS Infrastructure | ` +
    `Slot ${slot.date} ${hour}:${min} UTC | ${pricing.label}`;

  const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    payment_method_types: ["card"],
    line_items: [
      {
        price_data: {
          currency: "eur",
          product_data: {
            name: "Compute-Time Utility Token — TimeNation SaaS Infrastructure",
            description:
              `AI media synthesis compute-slot access. Slot: ${slot.date} ${hour}:${min} UTC — ` +
              `${pricing.label}. Non-monetary SaaS infrastructure access token. ` +
              `Not a security, financial instrument, or real-world asset.`,
          },
          unit_amount: pricing.priceEurCents,
        },
        quantity: 1,
      },
    ],
    mode: "payment",
    success_url: finalSuccessUrl,
    cancel_url: finalCancelUrl,
    metadata: {
      type: "tv_slot",
      slotId: String(slotId),
      citizenId: String(citizenId),
      fingerprintHash: fingerprint,
      billingDescriptor,
    },
    client_reference_id: String(citizenId),
  });

  // Save session ID + billing descriptor so webhook can match it
  await db
    .update(tvSlotsTable)
    .set({ stripeSessionId: session.id, billingDescriptor })
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));

  req.log.info({ citizenId, slotId, sessionId: session.id }, "TV slot Stripe checkout created");
  res.json({ url: session.url, sessionId: session.id });
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/slots/book-crypto ──────────────────────────────────────────
// Pathway B: create a Cryptomus stablecoin (USDT/USDC) payment order.
// Returns { paymentUrl, paymentUuid } — frontend redirects user to paymentUrl.
// Cryptomus calls POST /api/tv/crypto/webhook when paid.
const BookCryptoBody = z.object({
  slotId: z.number().int().positive(),
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  successUrl: z.string().optional(),
});

router.post("/tv/slots/book-crypto", sanctionsMiddleware, async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  if (!isCryptomusConfigured()) {
    res.status(503).json({
      error: "Крипто-шлюз не настроен. Установите CRYPTOMUS_MERCHANT_ID и CRYPTOMUS_API_KEY.",
    });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = BookCryptoBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }
  const { slotId, citizenId, successUrl } = parsed.data;

  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }
  if (slot.status !== "free") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Слот уже занят" }); return; }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);
  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  const pricing = BURN_MATRIX[slot.slotType as SlotType];
  // Convert EUR cents → USD (approx: 1 EUR ≈ 1.08 USD for conservative estimate)
  const amountUsd = (pricing.priceEurCents / 100 * 1.08).toFixed(2);

  const hour = String(Math.floor(slot.minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const min = String(slot.minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  const billingDescriptor =
    `Compute-Time Utility Token — TimeNation SaaS | Slot ${slot.date} ${hour}:${min} UTC | ${pricing.label}`;

  const host = `${req.protocol}://${req.get("host")}`;
  const callbackUrl = `${host}/api/tv/crypto/webhook`;
  const finalSuccessUrl = successUrl ?? `${host}/api/tv/checkout/success`;

  const payment = await createCryptoPayment({
    orderId: `slot_${slotId}_c${citizenId}_${Date.now()}`,
    amountUsd,
    billingDescriptor,
    callbackUrl,
    successUrl: finalSuccessUrl,
  });

  // Mark slot as pending crypto payment
  await db
    .update(tvSlotsTable)
    .set({
      cryptoPaymentUuid: payment.uuid,
      paymentStatus: "pending",
      billingDescriptor,
      paymentType: "crypto",
    })
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));

  req.log.info(
    { slotId, citizenId, uuid: payment.uuid, amountUsd },
    "Cryptomus payment created for TV slot"
  );

  res.status(201).json({
    paymentUrl: payment.url,
    paymentUuid: payment.uuid,
    amountUsd,
    currency: payment.currency,
  });
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/crypto/webhook ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Cryptomus calls this URL when payment status changes.
// Confirms slot booking and records paymentStatus='completed'.
router.post("/tv/crypto/webhook", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const body = req.body as Record<string, unknown>;
  const incomingSign = (req.headers["sign"] ?? body["sign"] ?? "") as string;

  if (!verifyCryptomusWebhook(body, incomingSign)) {
    req.log.warn({ body }, "Cryptomus webhook signature mismatch");
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid signature" });
    return;
  }

  const uuid = body["uuid"] as string | undefined;
  const status = body["status"] as string | undefined;
  const citizenIdRaw = (body["order_id"] as string | undefined)?.match(/c(\d+)/)?.[1];

  req.log.info({ uuid, status }, "Cryptomus webhook received");

  if (!uuid || status !== "paid") {
    // Not yet paid — acknowledge but don't unlock
    res.json({ ok: true });
    return;
  }

  const citizenId = citizenIdRaw ? parseInt(citizenIdRaw, 10) : null;

  // Find slot by cryptoPaymentUuid
  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.cryptoPaymentUuid, uuid))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) {
    req.log.warn({ uuid }, "Cryptomus webhook: slot not found");
    res.json({ ok: true });
    return;
  }

  if (slot.status === "free") {
    await db
      .update(tvSlotsTable)
      .set({
        status: "booked",
        ownerId: citizenId ?? slot.ownerId,
        paymentType: "crypto",
        paymentStatus: "completed",
      })
      .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slot.id));

    req.log.info(
      { slotId: slot.id, uuid, citizenId },
      "TV slot confirmed via Cryptomus webhook"
    );
  }

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/checkout/confirm?session_id=... ──────────────────────────────
// Stripe redirects here after successful payment; confirms slot booking.
router.get("/tv/checkout/confirm", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const sessionId = req.query["session_id"] as string | undefined;
  if (!sessionId || !STRIPE_SECRET_KEY) {
    res.status(400).send("<h1>Ошибка подтверждения</h1>");
    return;
  }

  const Stripe = (await import("stripe")).default;
  const stripe = new Stripe(STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);

  let session;
  try {
    session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve(sessionId);
  } catch {
    res.status(400).send("<h1>Недействительная сессия</h1>");
    return;
  }

  if (session.payment_status !== "paid") {
    res.status(402).send("<h1>Оплата не завершена</h1>");
    return;
  }

  const slotId = parseInt(session.metadata?.["slotId"] ?? "", 10);
  const citizenId = parseInt(session.metadata?.["citizenId"] ?? "", 10);
  const fingerprintHash = session.metadata?.["fingerprintHash"] ?? null;

  if (!isNaN(slotId) && !isNaN(citizenId)) {
    const [slot] = await db
      .select()
      .from(tvSlotsTable)
      .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId))
      .limit(1);

    if (slot && slot.status === "free") {
      await db
        .update(tvSlotsTable)
        .set({
          status: "booked",
          ownerId: citizenId,
          paymentType: "fiat",
          paymentStatus: "completed",
          stripeSessionId: sessionId,
          fingerprintHash,
        })
        .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));

      logger.info({ citizenId, slotId, sessionId }, "TV slot confirmed after Stripe payment");
    }
  }

  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/html");
  res.send(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Слот забронирован</title>
<style>body{margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:#0D1B2A;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;color:#E8EDF2}.c{text-align:center;padding:40px}.icon{font-size:64px}.t{font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:#C9A84C;margin:12px 0}.s{opacity:.7;margin:0 0 8px}.n{font-size:13px;opacity:.45}</style>
</head><body><div class="c"><div class="icon">📡</div><h1 class="t">Слот активирован!</h1><p class="s">Вернись в приложение — настрой капсулу.</p><p class="n">Закрой эту вкладку</p></div></body></html>`);
});

<style>body{margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:#0D1B2A;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;color:#E8EDF2}.c{text-align:center;padding:40px}.icon{font-size:64px}.t{font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:#6B7E93;margin:12px 0}.s{opacity:.7}</style>
</head><body><div class="c"><div class="icon">❌</div><h1 class="t">Бронирование отменено</h1><p class="s">Вернись в приложение и попробуй снова.</p></div></body></html>`);

// ─── GET /api/tv/capsule/:slotId ──────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/tv/capsule/:slotId", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["slotId"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slotId" }); return; }

  const [capsule] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Капсула не найдена" }); return; }
  res.json(capsule);
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/capsule/:slotId ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Create a capsule for an already-booked slot.
router.post("/tv/capsule/:slotId", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["slotId"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slotId" }); return; }

  const parsed = CreateCapsuleBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, seedText, mode, renderPath, broadcastPolicy, contentState, hasSovereignLink, visualStyle } =
    parsed.data;

  // Verify slot ownership
  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(and(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId), eq(tvSlotsTable.status, "booked")))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Забронированный слот не найден" });
    return;
  }
  if (slot.ownerId !== citizenId) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только владелец слота может создать капсулу" });
    return;
  }

  // Check for existing capsule
  const [existing] = await db
    .select({ id: tvCapsulesTable.id })
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Капсула для этого слота уже существует. Используй PATCH." });
    return;
  }

  const sovereignUrl = hasSovereignLink
    ? generateSovereignUrl(slot.date, slot.minuteIndex)
    : null;

  const retro = isRetroSlot(slot.date, slot.minuteIndex);

  const [capsule] = await db
    .insert(tvCapsulesTable)
    .values({
      slotId,
      citizenId,
      seedText: seedText ?? null,
      mode,
      renderPath,
      broadcastPolicy,
      contentState,
      hasSovereignLink,
      sovereignUrl,
      visualStyle: visualStyle ?? null,
      isRetro: retro,
      status: "draft",
    })
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ slotId, citizenId, broadcastPolicy, isRetro: retro }, "TV capsule created");
  res.status(201).json(capsule);
});

// ─── PATCH /api/tv/capsule/:slotId ────────────────────────────────────────────
// Update an existing capsule (seed, mode, policies).
router.patch("/tv/capsule/:slotId", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["slotId"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slotId" }); return; }

  const citizenId = z.number().int().positive().safeParse(req.body?.citizenId);
  if (!citizenId.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const parsed = UpdateCapsuleBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const [capsule] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Капсула не найдена" }); return; }
  if (capsule.citizenId !== citizenId.data) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только владелец может редактировать капсулу" });
    return;
  }
  if (capsule.status === "broadcast") {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Нельзя редактировать уже вышедшую в эфир капсулу" });
    return;
  }

  const { seedText, mode, renderPath, broadcastPolicy, contentState, hasSovereignLink, visualStyle } =
    parsed.data;

  // Regenerate sovereign URL if requested
  let sovereignUrl = capsule.sovereignUrl;
  if (hasSovereignLink === true && !capsule.hasSovereignLink) {
    const [slot] = await db
      .select()
      .from(tvSlotsTable)
      .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId))
      .limit(1);
    if (slot) sovereignUrl = generateSovereignUrl(slot.date, slot.minuteIndex);
  } else if (hasSovereignLink === false) {
    sovereignUrl = null;
  }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({
      ...(seedText !== undefined && { seedText }),
      ...(mode !== undefined && { mode }),
      ...(renderPath !== undefined && { renderPath }),
      ...(broadcastPolicy !== undefined && { broadcastPolicy }),
      ...(contentState !== undefined && { contentState }),
      ...(hasSovereignLink !== undefined && { hasSovereignLink, sovereignUrl }),
      ...(visualStyle !== undefined && { visualStyle }),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ slotId, citizenId: citizenId.data }, "TV capsule updated");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/moment/:date/:time ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Look up capsule by sovereign URL path (e.g. /api/tv/moment/1972-06-09/0930)
router.get("/tv/moment/:date/:time", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const { date, time } = req.params as { date: string; time: string };
  if (!isValidDate(date) || !/^\d{4}$/.test(time)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Формат: /api/tv/moment/YYYY-MM-DD/HHmm" });
    return;
  }

  const sovereignUrl = `timenation.tv/moment/${date}-${time}`;
  const [capsule] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.sovereignUrl, sovereignUrl))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Момент не найден. Возможно, капсула приватная или не существует." });
    return;
  }

  if (capsule.broadcastPolicy === "private") {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Этот момент отмечен как Absolute Privacy." });
    return;
  }

  res.json({ sovereignUrl, capsule });
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/capsule/:slotId/render ──────────────────────────────────────
// Trigger LIGHT render pipeline: Mode A/B manifesto → ElevenLabs TTS → FFmpeg
router.post("/tv/capsule/:slotId/render", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["slotId"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slotId" }); return; }

  const citizenId = z.number().int().positive().safeParse(req.body?.citizenId);
  if (!citizenId.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  // Verify ownership
  const [capsule] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Капсула не найдена" }); return; }
  if (capsule.citizenId !== citizenId.data) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только владелец может запустить рендер" });
    return;
  }
  if (capsule.status === "pending_render") {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Рендер уже запущен" });
    return;
  }
  if (capsule.status === "broadcast") {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Капсула уже в эфире" });
    return;
  }

  // ── Age verification (COPPA / EU DSA) ──────────────────────────────────────
  const [citizenRecord] = await db
    .select({ ageVerified: citizensTable.ageVerified })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId.data))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizenRecord) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" });
    return;
  }
  if (!citizenRecord.ageVerified) {
    res.status(403).json({
      error: "Для генерации AI-контента необходимо подтвердить возраст 18+.",
      code: "AGE_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED",
    });
    return;
  }

  // ── Sanctions check (OFAC / international compliance) ──────────────────────
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    sanctionsMiddleware(req, res, (err?: unknown) => {
      if (err) reject(err); else resolve();
    });
  }).catch(() => { /* res already sent by middleware */ });
  if (res.headersSent) return;

  // ── Content moderation (EU DSA / US/RU/CN frameworks) ───────────────────────
  if (capsule.seedText) {
    const modResult = moderateText(capsule.seedText);
    if (!modResult.allowed) {
      res.status(422).json({
        error: "Ваш текст не прошел проверку глобальной безопасности контента.",
        code: "CONTENT_MODERATION_BLOCK",
      });
      return;
    }
  }

  const renderLog = req.log.child({ slotId, citizenId: citizenId.data });
  renderLog.info({ mode: capsule.mode }, "Render enqueued");

  // Mark pending immediately so client can see the status change
  await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({ status: "pending_render", renderProgress: "generating_prompt", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));

  // Enqueue into bounded render queue (max MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS simultaneous HF calls).
  // Returns immediately with a jobId — client polls GET /api/tv/capsule/:slotId for progress.
  // Heavy-path (Quarter/Half/Apex) slots get more wall-clock time; light-path (citizen) slots
  // are capped aggressively so the queue stays responsive for free-tier bookings.
  const renderTimeoutMs =
    capsule.renderPath === "heavy"
      ? RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS
      : RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS;

  const jobId = enqueueRender({
    jobId: `${slotId}-${Date.now()}`,
    slotId,
    citizenId: citizenId.data,
    capsuleSeedText: capsule.seedText ?? null,
    timeoutMs: renderTimeoutMs,
  });

  renderLog.info({ jobId }, "Render job enqueued");
  res.status(202).json({ ok: true, status: "pending_render", slotId, jobId, timeoutMs: renderTimeoutMs });
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/capsule/:id/report  (DMCA / takedown) ───────────────────────
// US DMCA § 512 notice-and-takedown: immediately suspends capsule from public view.
router.post("/tv/capsule/:id/report", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const capsuleId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(capsuleId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const bodySchema = z.object({
    reason: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
    reporterEmail: z.string().email().optional(),
    copyrightOwner: z.string().max(200).optional(),
  });
  const body = bodySchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!body.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "reason обязателен" }); return; }

  const [capsule] = await db
    .select({ id: tvCapsulesTable.id, reportStatus: tvCapsulesTable.reportStatus })
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.id, capsuleId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Капсула не найдена" }); return; }
  if (capsule.reportStatus === "removed") {
    res.status(410).json({ error: "Контент уже удалён" });
    return;
  }

  const reportNote = [
    body.data.reason,
    body.data.copyrightOwner ? `Copyright owner: ${body.data.copyrightOwner}` : null,
    body.data.reporterEmail ? `Reporter: ${body.data.reporterEmail}` : null,
  ].filter(Boolean).join(" | ");

  await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({
      isReported: true,
      reportStatus: "suspended",
      reportReason: reportNote,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.id, capsuleId));

  req.log.info({ capsuleId, reason: body.data.reason }, "DMCA report filed — capsule suspended");
  res.json({
    ok: true,
    message: "Жалоба принята. Контент временно скрыт до проверки.",
    capsuleId,
  });
});

// ─── POST /api/tv/slots/:id/air ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Owner manually transitions a booked+rendered slot to "broadcast".
// Useful for retro slots that the cron missed (slot time already in the past).
router.post("/tv/slots/:id/air", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slot id" }); return; }

  const body = z.object({ citizenId: z.number().int().positive() }).safeParse(req.body);
  if (!body.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }
  const { citizenId } = body.data;

  const [slot] = await db.select().from(tvSlotsTable).where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId)).limit(1);
  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }
  if (slot.ownerId !== citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только владелец может выпустить слот в эфир" }); return; }
  if (slot.status !== "booked") {
    res.status(409).json({ error: `Статус слота: ${slot.status}. Можно выпустить только booked-слот.` });
    return;
  }

  const [capsule] = await db.select().from(tvCapsulesTable).where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId)).limit(1);
  if (!capsule?.mediaUrl) {
    res.status(422).json({ error: "Сначала отрендерируйте капсулу — видео ещё не готово" });
    return;
  }

  await db.update(tvSlotsTable).set({ status: "broadcast" }).where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));
  await db.update(tvCapsulesTable).set({ status: "broadcast" }).where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));

  req.log.info({ slotId, citizenId }, "Slot manually aired");
  res.json({ ok: true, slotId, mediaUrl: capsule.mediaUrl });
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/live-slot?date=YYYY-MM-DD&minute=0-1439 ─────────────────────
// Live broadcast engine: returns the slot & video URL for a given UTC minute.
// Used by the Chronos Live Player for seamless 24/7 switching.
router.get("/tv/live-slot", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const date =
    (req.query["date"] as string | undefined) ??
    new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const minuteRaw = parseInt((req.query["minute"] as string | undefined) ?? "", 10);

  if (isNaN(minuteRaw)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "minute параметр обязателен (0–1439)" });
    return;
  }

  const minute = ((minuteRaw % 1440) + 1440) % 1440;
  await ensureSlotsForDate(date);

  const [slot] = await db
    .select({
      id: tvSlotsTable.id,
      slotType: tvSlotsTable.slotType,
      status: tvSlotsTable.status,
      ownerId: tvSlotsTable.ownerId,
      ownerName: citizensTable.name,
    })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .leftJoin(citizensTable, eq(tvSlotsTable.ownerId, citizensTable.id))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(tvSlotsTable.date, date),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex, minute),
        inArray(tvSlotsTable.status, ["broadcast", "booked"])
      )
    )
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) {
    res.json({
      minuteIndex: minute,
      videoUrl: null,
      hasContent: false,
      ownerName: null,
      slotType: null,
      slotId: null,
    });
    return;
  }

  const [capsule] = await db
    .select({ mediaUrl: tvCapsulesTable.mediaUrl })
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slot.id))
    .limit(1);

  const filename = capsule?.mediaUrl?.split("/").pop();
  const videoUrl = filename ? `/api/tv/media/${filename}` : null;

  res.json({
    minuteIndex: minute,
    videoUrl,
    hasContent: !!videoUrl,
    ownerName: slot.ownerName ?? null,
    slotType: slot.slotType,
    slotId: slot.id,
  });
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/schedule?date=YYYY-MM-DD ────────────────────────────────────
// Public schedule summary: booked slots + capsule render status for a date.
router.get("/tv/schedule", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const date = (req.query["date"] as string | undefined) ?? "";
  if (!isValidDate(date)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Параметр date обязателен в формате YYYY-MM-DD" });
    return;
  }

  await ensureSlotsForDate(date);

  const slots = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.date, date))
    .orderBy(tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex);

  const bookedSlots = slots.filter((s) => s.status === "booked" || s.status === "broadcast");

  // Load capsules for booked slots
  const bookedIds = bookedSlots.map((s) => s.id);
  const capsules =
    bookedIds.length > 0
      ? await db
          .select()
          .from(tvCapsulesTable)
          .where(inArray(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, bookedIds))
      : [];
  const capsuleBySlot = new Map(capsules.map((c) => [c.slotId, c]));

  // Load owner names
  const ownerIds = [...new Set(bookedSlots.map((s) => s.ownerId).filter(Boolean))] as number[];
  const owners =
    ownerIds.length > 0
      ? await db
          .select({ id: citizensTable.id, name: citizensTable.name })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(inArray(citizensTable.id, ownerIds))
      : [];
  const ownerMap = new Map(owners.map((o) => [o.id, o.name]));

  const schedule = bookedSlots.map((s) => {
    const capsule = capsuleBySlot.get(s.id);
    return {
      slotId: s.id,
      minuteIndex: s.minuteIndex,
      slotType: s.slotType,
      hourLabel: String(Math.floor(s.minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0"),
      minLabel: String(s.minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0"),
      ownerName: s.ownerId ? (ownerMap.get(s.ownerId) ?? null) : null,
      hasRenderedVideo: !!(capsule?.mediaUrl),
      catcastUrl: capsule?.catcastUrl ?? null,
      mediaUrl: capsule?.mediaUrl ?? null,
      isRetro: isRetroSlot(s.date, s.minuteIndex),
    };
  });

  res.json({
    date,
    totalSlots: slots.length,
    bookedSlots: bookedSlots.length,
    freeSlots: slots.length - bookedSlots.length,
    schedule,
  });
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/render/queue ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Returns current render queue depth and active count (for health checks / admin).
router.get("/tv/render/queue", (_req, res): void => {
  res.json(getRenderQueueStats());
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/render/jobs/:jobId ──────────────────────────────────────────
// Poll render job status by the jobId returned from POST …/render.
router.get("/tv/render/jobs/:jobId", (req, res): void => {
  const jobId = req.params["jobId"] ?? "";
  const status = getRenderJobStatus(jobId);
  if (!status) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Job не найден или уже истёк" });
    return;
  }
  res.json({ jobId, ...status });
});

// ─── GET /api/tv/media/:filename ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Serve generated MP4 files from the media directory
router.get("/tv/media/:filename", (req, res): void => {
  const filename = req.params["filename"] ?? "";
  if (!filename.endsWith(".mp4") || filename.includes("..") || filename.includes("/")) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid filename" });
    return;
  }

  const filePath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, filename);
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Медиафайл не найден. Запустите рендер." });
    return;
  }

  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "video/mp4");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=86400");
  fs.createReadStream(filePath).pipe(res);
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/wallet.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { ethers } from "ethers";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";
import crypto from "node:crypto";

const router: IRouter = Router();

const NONCE_TTL_MINUTES = 10;

function buildSignMessage(citizenId: number, walletAddress: string, nonce: string): string {
  return [
    "Welcome to TimeNation — the Digital State where Time is Currency.",
    "",
    `Linking wallet to Passport ID: ${citizenId}`,
    `Wallet: ${walletAddress}`,
    `Nonce: ${nonce}`,
    `Expires in: ${NONCE_TTL_MINUTES} minutes`,
  ].join("\n");
}

router.get("/auth/wallet-nonce", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = z.object({
    walletAddress: z.string().min(10),
    citizenId: z.coerce.number().int().positive(),
  }).safeParse(req.query);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "walletAddress and citizenId are required" });
    return;
  }

  const { walletAddress, citizenId } = parsed.data;

  const citizen = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (citizen.length === 0) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  if (citizen[0]!.walletVerified && citizen[0]!.walletAddress) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Wallet already verified for this citizen" });
    return;
  }

  const conflicting = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.walletAddress, walletAddress.toLowerCase()))
    .limit(1);

  if (conflicting.length > 0 && conflicting[0]!.id !== citizenId) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "This wallet address is linked to another citizen" });
    return;
  }

  const nonce = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex");

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletAddress: walletAddress.toLowerCase(), walletNonce: nonce })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

  const message = buildSignMessage(citizenId, walletAddress, nonce);

  req.log.info({ citizenId, walletAddress }, "Wallet nonce generated");

  res.json({ nonce, message });
});

const VerifyBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  walletAddress: z.string().min(10),
  signature: z.string().min(10),
});

router.post("/auth/wallet-verify", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = VerifyBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { citizenId, walletAddress, signature } = parsed.data;

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  if (!citizen.walletNonce) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "No nonce found. Request a nonce first." });
    return;
  }

  if (citizen.walletAddress !== walletAddress.toLowerCase()) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Wallet address does not match nonce session" });
    return;
  }

  const message = buildSignMessage(citizenId, walletAddress, citizen.walletNonce);

  let recoveredAddress: string;
  try {
    recoveredAddress = ethers.verifyMessage(message, signature);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Invalid signature format");
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid signature format" });
    return;
  }

  if (recoveredAddress.toLowerCase() !== walletAddress.toLowerCase()) {
    res.status(401).json({
      error: "Signature verification failed — recovered address does not match",
      recovered: recoveredAddress.toLowerCase(),
      provided: walletAddress.toLowerCase(),
    });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletNonce: null, walletVerified: 1, walletAddress: walletAddress.toLowerCase() })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

  const [updated] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  req.log.info({ citizenId, walletAddress }, "Wallet verified and linked");

  res.json({
    success: true,
    walletAddress: walletAddress.toLowerCase(),
    citizen: {
      ...updated,
      transferableBalance: (updated?.balanceMinutes ?? 0) - 1,
      lastActivity: updated?.lastActivity?.toISOString() ?? null,
      createdAt: updated!.createdAt.toISOString(),
    },
  });
});

router.delete("/auth/wallet-unlink", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = z.object({ citizenId: z.coerce.number().int().positive() }).safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId is required" });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletAddress: null, walletNonce: null, walletVerified: 0 })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, parsed.data.citizenId));

  res.json({ success: true });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/assetsService.ts ---
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// tsx runs with CWD = workspace root (/home/runner/workspace),
// but __dirname = absolute path to this source file's directory.
// Try both common resolutions so the loader is robust.
function findAssetsDir(): string {
  const candidates = [
    // tsx: __dirname is the src directory
    path.join(__dirname, "../../assets"),
    // fallback: relative to workspace root
    path.join(process.cwd(), "artifacts/api-server/assets"),
    // fallback: adjacent to package root
    path.join(process.cwd(), "assets"),
  ];
  for (const dir of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, "emblem.jpg"))) {
      logger.info({ dir }, "assetsService: found assets directory");
      return dir;
    }
  }
  logger.warn({ candidates }, "assetsService: assets directory not found, graphics will use SVG fallbacks");
  return candidates[0]!;
}

const ASSETS_DIR = findAssetsDir();

function loadAsDataUri(filename: string): string {
  const filePath = path.join(ASSETS_DIR, filename);
  try {
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
    const ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
    const mime = ext === ".png" ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg";
    return `data:${mime};base64,${buf.toString("base64")}`;
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

// Loaded once at module init — cached for lifetime of process
export const EMBLEM_DATA_URI: string = loadAsDataUri("emblem.jpg");
export const FLAG_DATA_URI: string   = loadAsDataUri("flag.jpg");

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/catcastService.ts ---
import fs from "fs";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Catcast.tv Upload Service ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// catcast-d architecture (github.com/mrcatman/catcast-d):
//   Auth:   POST /api/auth/login  { username, password } → JWT token
//   Media:  POST /api/media       { channel_id, title, description } → { id, uuid }
//   Upload: POST /api/media/{id}/upload                  → { key }
//   TUS:    POST /api/files (tusd) with Upload-Metadata: id=<id>, upload_key=<key>
//   nginx routes /api → php:4002, /api/files → tusd:1080

const CATCAST_BASE = process.env["CATCAST_BASE_URL"] ?? "https://catcast.tv";
const CATCAST_CHANNEL_ID = process.env["CATCAST_CHANNEL_ID"] ?? "27813";
const AI_DISCLOSURE =
  "AI Generated / Сгенерировано ИИ / Generated via Artificial Intelligence technologies.";

// In-memory JWT token cache (55min TTL — JWT default is 1h in catcast-d)
let cachedToken: { token: string; expiresAt: number } | null = null;

async function getAuthToken(): Promise<string | null> {
  const email = process.env["CATCAST_EMAIL"];
  const password = process.env["CATCAST_PASSWORD"];
  if (!email || !password) {
    logger.info("CATCAST_EMAIL/CATCAST_PASSWORD not set — Catcast upload skipped");
    return null;
  }

  if (cachedToken && Date.now() < cachedToken.expiresAt) return cachedToken.token;

  // catcast-d AuthController uses "username" field (not "email")
  // The email stored in secrets IS the username on catcast.tv
  const username = email.includes("@") ? email.split("@")[0] : email;

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${CATCAST_BASE}/api/auth/login`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        Accept: "application/json",
        "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
        Referer: `${CATCAST_BASE}/`,
        Origin: CATCAST_BASE,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15_000),
    });

    const contentType = res.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
    if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) {
      // catcast.tv production nginx returns SPA HTML for all paths —
      // their API is only accessible from the browser (same-origin).
      // To use Catcast upload, set CATCAST_BASE_URL to a self-hosted catcast-d instance.
      logger.warn(
        { base: CATCAST_BASE, status: res.status },
        "Catcast: API returned HTML (SPA). " +
        "Set CATCAST_BASE_URL env var to a self-hosted catcast-d instance URL to enable uploads."
      );
      return null;
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      logger.warn({ status: res.status, body }, "Catcast auth failed");
      return null;
    }

    const data = (await res.json()) as {
      token?: string;
      access_token?: string;
      data?: { token?: string };
    };
    const token = data.token ?? data.access_token ?? data.data?.token;
    if (!token) {
      logger.warn({ data }, "Catcast: no token in auth response");
      return null;
    }

    cachedToken = { token, expiresAt: Date.now() + 55 * 60 * 1000 };
    logger.info("Catcast auth: token obtained");
    return token;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Catcast auth fetch failed");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── TUS upload (catcast-d uses tusd at /api/files) ────────────────────────────
async function tusUpload(
  filePath: string,
  mediaId: string | number,
  uploadKey: string,
  token: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const fileBuffer = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
  const fileSize = fileBuffer.length;
  const tusUrl = `${CATCAST_BASE}/api/files`;

  // Build base64 metadata per TUS spec
  const meta = [
    `id ${Buffer.from(String(mediaId)).toString("base64")}`,
    `upload_key ${Buffer.from(uploadKey).toString("base64")}`,
    `filename ${Buffer.from(`timenation_slot.mp4`).toString("base64")}`,
    `filetype ${Buffer.from("video/mp4").toString("base64")}`,
  ].join(",");

  // TUS Creation request
  const createRes = await fetch(tusUrl, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Tus-Resumable": "1.0.0",
      "Upload-Length": String(fileSize),
      "Upload-Metadata": meta,
      "Content-Type": "application/offset+octet-stream",
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
    },
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15_000),
  });

  if (!createRes.ok && createRes.status !== 201) {
    logger.warn({ status: createRes.status }, "TUS creation failed");
    return false;
  }

  const uploadUrl = createRes.headers.get("location");
  if (!uploadUrl) {
    logger.warn("TUS: no Location header in creation response");
    return false;
  }

  // TUS PATCH — send the full file in one chunk (MVP; resumable for large files)
  const resolvedUploadUrl = uploadUrl.startsWith("http")
    ? uploadUrl
    : `${CATCAST_BASE}${uploadUrl}`;

  const patchRes = await fetch(resolvedUploadUrl, {
    method: "PATCH",
    headers: {
      "Tus-Resumable": "1.0.0",
      "Upload-Offset": "0",
      "Content-Type": "application/offset+octet-stream",
      "Content-Length": String(fileSize),
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    },
    body: fileBuffer,
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(300_000), // 5 min for large files
  });

  if (!patchRes.ok) {
    logger.warn({ status: patchRes.status }, "TUS PATCH failed");
    return false;
  }

  return true;
}

export interface CatcastUploadResult {
  videoId: string;
  videoUrl: string;
}

export async function uploadToCatcast(
  filePath: string,
  slotId: number,
  citizenName: string,
  seedText: string
): Promise<CatcastUploadResult | null> {
  if (!process.env["CATCAST_EMAIL"]) return null;

  const token = await getAuthToken();
  if (!token) return null;

  const title = `TimeNation-TV — Минута №${slotId}`;
  const description = [
    seedText ? `«${seedText.slice(0, 800)}»` : null,
    `Гражданин: ${citizenName}`,
    AI_DISCLOSURE,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("\n\n");

  try {
    // Step 1: Create media record
    const mediaRes = await fetch(`${CATCAST_BASE}/api/media`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        Accept: "application/json",
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        channel_id: CATCAST_CHANNEL_ID,
        title,
        description,
        privacy_status: 0, // 0 = public in catcast-d
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15_000),
    });

    if (!mediaRes.ok) {
      const body = await mediaRes.text();
      logger.warn({ status: mediaRes.status, body }, "Catcast media create failed");
      if (mediaRes.status === 401) cachedToken = null;
      return null;
    }

    const media = (await mediaRes.json()) as {
      id?: number | string;
      uuid?: string;
    };
    const mediaId = media.id;
    const mediaUuid = media.uuid;
    if (!mediaId) {
      logger.warn({ media }, "Catcast: no media id in response");
      return null;
    }

    // Step 2: Request TUS upload key
    const keyRes = await fetch(`${CATCAST_BASE}/api/media/${mediaId}/upload`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        Accept: "application/json",
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
      },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),
    });

    if (!keyRes.ok) {
      logger.warn({ status: keyRes.status }, "Catcast upload key request failed");
      return null;
    }

    const keyData = (await keyRes.json()) as { key?: string };
    const uploadKey = keyData.key;
    if (!uploadKey) {
      logger.warn({ keyData }, "Catcast: no upload key in response");
      return null;
    }

    // Step 3: TUS upload
    const uploadOk = await tusUpload(filePath, mediaId, uploadKey, token);
    if (!uploadOk) return null;

    const videoUrl = mediaUuid
      ? `${CATCAST_BASE}/media/${mediaUuid}`
      : `${CATCAST_BASE}/media/${mediaId}`;

    logger.info({ slotId, mediaId, videoUrl }, "Catcast upload complete");
    return { videoId: String(mediaId), videoUrl };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err, slotId }, "Catcast upload error");
    return null;
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/cinematicPromptService.ts ---
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Cinematic Prompt System ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Converts citizen's Russian seed text into a detailed English text-to-video
// prompt suitable for HuggingFace / Pollinations AI generation.

let _genai: GoogleGenerativeAI | null = null;

function getClient(): GoogleGenerativeAI | null {
  if (_genai) return _genai;
  const key = process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
  if (!key) return null;
  _genai = new GoogleGenerativeAI(key);
  return _genai;
}

export type VisualStyle = "space" | "cyberpunk" | "abstract" | "nature" | "minimal";

const STYLE_DESCRIPTORS: Record<VisualStyle, string> = {
  space:
    "deep space, cosmic nebula, star clusters, floating galaxies, dark void with luminous " +
    "gas clouds, cinematic astronomy, dramatic cosmic scale",
  cyberpunk:
    "neon-lit megacity, rain-soaked streets, holographic billboards, glowing circuit grids, " +
    "dystopian future, neon purple and cyan, electric atmosphere",
  abstract:
    "flowing abstract shapes, morphing geometric patterns, vibrant color gradients, " +
    "fluid motion, surreal digital art, kaleidoscopic motion",
  nature:
    "lush ancient forest, mist over mountains, flowing rivers, golden hour sunlight " +
    "through canopy, organic life, serene wilderness, epic natural landscape",
  minimal:
    "clean minimalist composition, subtle monochromatic palette, elegant negative space, " +
    "soft light gradients, modern geometric forms, calm and precise",
};

const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a cinematic text-to-video prompt engineer for TimeNation TV.

TASK: Convert the citizen's Russian seed thought into a precise English text-to-video prompt 
suitable for AI video generation models (like text-to-video-ms-1.7b).

RULES:
- Output ONLY the prompt — no explanations, no quotes, no extra text.
- Length: 15–25 words maximum (models work best with concise prompts).
- Language: English only.
- Style: cinematic, atmospheric, photorealistic or high-quality digital art.
- Always end with cinematic quality tags: "cinematic, 4k, dramatic lighting, high quality".
- The prompt should visually evoke the emotion/theme of the seed text, not narrate it literally.
- Focus on visual imagery, motion, atmosphere, lighting — things a camera can capture.
- Do NOT include people's names, text overlays, or logos in the prompt.`;

/** Generate a cinematic English video prompt from Russian seed text */
export async function generateCinematicPrompt(
  seedText: string,
