  visualStyle: VisualStyle = "space"
): Promise<string> {
  const styleDesc = STYLE_DESCRIPTORS[visualStyle];
  const fallback = buildFallbackPrompt(seedText, visualStyle, styleDesc);

  const client = getClient();
  if (!client) {
    logger.info({ visualStyle }, "Gemini not configured — using fallback cinematic prompt");
    return fallback;
  }

  const userPrompt =
    `Visual style context: ${styleDesc}\n` +
    `Citizen seed text (Russian): «${seedText.trim().slice(0, 400)}»\n\n` +
    `Generate the cinematic text-to-video prompt now.`;

  try {
    const model = client.getGenerativeModel({
      model: "gemini-2.0-flash",
      systemInstruction: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
      generationConfig: {
        maxOutputTokens: 80,
        temperature: 0.9,
        topP: 0.95,
      },
    });

    const result = await model.generateContent(userPrompt);
    const text = result.response.text().trim();

    if (!text || text.length < 10) {
      logger.warn({ seedText: seedText.slice(0, 60) }, "Gemini returned empty cinematic prompt, using fallback");
      return fallback;
    }

    logger.info(
      { visualStyle, promptLength: text.length, prompt: text.slice(0, 80) },
      "Cinematic prompt generated via Gemini"
    );
    return text;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err, visualStyle }, "Gemini cinematic prompt failed — using fallback");
    return fallback;
  }
}

/** Deterministic fallback prompt without LLM */
function buildFallbackPrompt(
  seedText: string,
  visualStyle: VisualStyle,
  styleDesc: string
): string {
  const wordCount = seedText.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
  const intensity = wordCount > 20 ? "epic, sweeping motion" : "calm, atmospheric motion";
  return `${styleDesc}, ${intensity}, cinematic, 4k, dramatic lighting, high quality`;
}

// ── Render tier types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export type RenderTier = "citizen" | "quarter" | "half" | "apex";

// Quality tags escalate with slot prestige
const TIER_QUALITY_TAGS: Record<RenderTier, string> = {
  citizen: "cinematic, 4k, dramatic lighting, high quality",
  quarter: "cinematic, 8k, photorealistic, atmospheric, depth of field, film grain, ultra high quality",
  half:    "cinematic, 8k, photorealistic, volumetric lighting, bokeh, award-winning cinematography, epic scale, ultra high quality",
  apex:    "cinematic, 8k, photorealistic, hyper-detailed, volumetric light shafts, perfect composition, IMAX quality",
};

/** Tier-aware cinematic prompt — higher tiers get richer quality instructions */
export async function generateCinematicPromptTiered(
  seedText: string,
  visualStyle: VisualStyle = "space",
  tier: RenderTier = "citizen"
): Promise<string> {
  const qualityTags = TIER_QUALITY_TAGS[tier];
  const styleDesc = STYLE_DESCRIPTORS[visualStyle];
  const fallback = `${styleDesc}, ${tier === "citizen" ? "calm, atmospheric motion" : "epic, sweeping motion"}, ${qualityTags}`;

  const client = getClient();
  if (!client) {
    logger.info({ visualStyle, tier }, "Gemini not configured — using fallback tiered prompt");
    return fallback;
  }

  const tierNote =
    tier === "apex" ? " Be extremely detailed: elaborate camera movement, rich atmosphere, epic scale." :
    tier === "half" ? " Be highly detailed: describe lighting, mood, and depth carefully." :
    tier === "quarter" ? " Be precise and evocative with atmosphere and lighting." : "";

  const systemInstruction = SYSTEM_PROMPT
    .replace(
      `Always end with cinematic quality tags: "cinematic, 4k, dramatic lighting, high quality".`,
      `Always end with these exact quality tags: "${qualityTags}".${tierNote}`
    );

  try {
    const model = client.getGenerativeModel({
      model: "gemini-2.0-flash",
      systemInstruction,
      generationConfig: {
        maxOutputTokens: tier === "apex" ? 120 : tier === "half" ? 100 : 80,
        temperature: tier === "apex" ? 1.0 : tier === "half" ? 0.95 : 0.9,
        topP: 0.95,
      },
    });

    const userPrompt =
      `Visual style: ${styleDesc}\n` +
      `Render tier: ${tier.toUpperCase()}\n` +
      `Citizen seed (Russian): «${seedText.trim().slice(0, 400)}»\n\n` +
      `Generate the cinematic prompt now.`;

    const result = await model.generateContent(userPrompt);
    const text = result.response.text().trim();

    if (!text || text.length < 10) {
      logger.warn({ tier }, "Gemini returned empty tiered prompt, using fallback");
      return fallback;
    }

    logger.info({ visualStyle, tier, promptLength: text.length }, "Tiered cinematic prompt generated");
    return text;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err, visualStyle, tier }, "Gemini tiered prompt failed — using fallback");
    return fallback;
  }
}

// ── Multi-Scene Prompts for Prime Time (:00) ─────────────────────────────────

export interface MultiScenePrompts {
  scene1: string;
  scene2: string;
  scene3: string;
}

const MULTI_SCENE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a cinematic director for TimeNation TV Prime Time broadcast.

TASK: Analyze the citizen's text and split it into 3 distinct visual scenes with a clear narrative arc.
For each scene, write a separate English text-to-video visual prompt.

RULES:
- Output ONLY valid JSON: {"scene1": "...", "scene2": "...", "scene3": "..."}
- Each prompt: 15-25 words, English only, no nested quotes
- Narrative arc: scene1=wide establishing shot, scene2=dynamic movement/development, scene3=peak/transcendent resolution
- Each prompt ends with: "cinematic, 8k, dramatic lighting, ultra quality"
- Scenes should feel VISUALLY DISTINCT (different composition, color palette, or mood)
- Do NOT include text overlays, names, logos, or human faces
- Output ONLY the JSON object — no markdown, no explanations`;

/** Generate 3 scene visual prompts for Prime Time multi-scene montage */
export async function generateMultiScenePrompts(
  seedText: string,
  manifestoText: string,
  visualStyle: VisualStyle = "space"
): Promise<MultiScenePrompts> {
  const styleDesc = STYLE_DESCRIPTORS[visualStyle];
  const qualityTag = TIER_QUALITY_TAGS["apex"];

  const buildFallback = (): MultiScenePrompts => ({
    scene1: `${styleDesc}, wide majestic establishing shot, calm and infinite, ${qualityTag}`,
    scene2: `${styleDesc}, dynamic motion, energy building, dramatic tension, sweeping camera, ${qualityTag}`,
    scene3: `${styleDesc}, epic peak moment, brilliant light burst, transcendent resolution, ${qualityTag}`,
  });

  const client = getClient();
  if (!client) {
    logger.info({ visualStyle }, "Gemini not configured — using fallback multi-scene prompts");
    return buildFallback();
  }

  const contextText = (manifestoText.trim() || seedText.trim()).slice(0, 600);

  const userPrompt =
    `Visual style context: ${styleDesc}\n` +
    `Citizen text (Russian): «${contextText}»\n\n` +
    `Split into 3 scenes. Return ONLY the JSON object.`;

  try {
    const model = client.getGenerativeModel({
      model: "gemini-2.0-flash",
      systemInstruction: MULTI_SCENE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
      generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: 300, temperature: 0.95, topP: 0.95 },
    });

    const result = await model.generateContent(userPrompt);
    let raw = result.response.text().trim();
    // Strip markdown code fences if present
    raw = raw.replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, "").replace(/\s*```$/i, "").trim();

    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<MultiScenePrompts>;
    if (!parsed.scene1 || !parsed.scene2 || !parsed.scene3) {
      throw new Error("Incomplete JSON from Gemini");
    }

    logger.info(
      { visualStyle, s1: parsed.scene1.slice(0, 50), s2: parsed.scene2.slice(0, 50) },
      "Multi-scene prompts generated via Gemini"
    );
    return { scene1: parsed.scene1, scene2: parsed.scene2, scene3: parsed.scene3 };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Gemini multi-scene failed — using fallback prompts");
    return buildFallback();
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/cryptomusService.ts ---
/**
 * Cryptomus payment gateway integration
 * Docs: https://doc.cryptomus.com/payments/creating-static
 *
 * Required secrets (set in Replit Secrets):
 *   CRYPTOMUS_MERCHANT_ID  — UUID from Cryptomus merchant dashboard
 *   CRYPTOMUS_API_KEY      — Payment API key (not payout key)
 */
import crypto from "crypto";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const MERCHANT_ID = process.env["CRYPTOMUS_MERCHANT_ID"];
const API_KEY = process.env["CRYPTOMUS_API_KEY"];
const BASE_URL = "https://api.cryptomus.com/v1";

export function isCryptomusConfigured(): boolean {
  return !!(MERCHANT_ID && API_KEY);
}

// ── Signature: md5( base64(JSON) + api_key ) ─────────────────────────────────
function sign(body: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const json = JSON.stringify(body);
  const b64 = Buffer.from(json).toString("base64");
  return crypto.createHash("md5").update(b64 + API_KEY).digest("hex");
}

async function request<T>(
  endpoint: string,
  body: Record<string, unknown>
): Promise<T> {
  const signature = sign(body);
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      merchant: MERCHANT_ID!,
      sign: signature,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`Cryptomus ${endpoint} → HTTP ${res.status}: ${text}`);
  }
  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
}

// ── Create payment ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface CryptomusPayment {
  uuid: string;
  url: string;
  amount: string;
  currency: string;
  status: string;
}

interface CreatePaymentResponse {
  state: number;
  result: CryptomusPayment;
}

export async function createCryptoPayment(opts: {
  orderId: string;           // e.g. "slot_123"
  amountUsd: string;         // e.g. "4.99"
  billingDescriptor: string; // shown to buyer
  callbackUrl: string;       // webhook URL
  successUrl: string;
}): Promise<CryptomusPayment> {
  if (!isCryptomusConfigured()) {
    throw new Error("Cryptomus не настроен: установите CRYPTOMUS_MERCHANT_ID и CRYPTOMUS_API_KEY");
  }

  const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
    amount: opts.amountUsd,
    currency: "USD",
    order_id: opts.orderId,
    url_callback: opts.callbackUrl,
    url_return: opts.successUrl,
    is_payment_multiple: false,
    lifetime: 3600,           // 1 hour to pay
    to_currency: "USDT",      // preferred stablecoin
    additional_data: opts.billingDescriptor,
  };

  const data = await request<CreatePaymentResponse>("/payment", body);
  logger.info({ orderId: opts.orderId, uuid: data.result.uuid }, "Cryptomus payment created");
  return data.result;
}

// ── Verify webhook signature ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Cryptomus sends: body JSON + sign header (md5 of base64(body_without_sign) + api_key)
export function verifyCryptomusWebhook(
  rawBody: Record<string, unknown>,
  incomingSign: string
): boolean {
  const { sign: _s, ...bodyWithoutSign } = rawBody;
  const expected = sign(bodyWithoutSign);
  return expected === incomingSign;
}

// ── Payment status ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface PaymentStatus {
  uuid: string;
  order_id: string;
  status: string;   // paid | wait | cancel | process | ...
  is_final: boolean;
}

interface StatusResponse {
  state: number;
  result: PaymentStatus;
}

export async function getPaymentStatus(uuid: string): Promise<PaymentStatus> {
  const data = await request<StatusResponse>("/payment/info", { uuid });
  return data.result;
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/emissionService.ts ---
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { emissionLogsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, gte, lte, sum, and, isNotNull, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// Суточный лимит эмиссии Дарминов по всей сети
export const DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT = 350;

function todayUTC(): Date {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return d;
}

function tomorrowUTC(): Date {
  const d = todayUTC();
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 1);
  return d;
}

/** Суммарная одобренная эмиссия за сегодня (UTC) */
export async function getDailyApprovedTotal(): Promise<number> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ total: sum(emissionLogsTable.amount) })
    .from(emissionLogsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(emissionLogsTable.isApproved, true),
        gte(emissionLogsTable.createdAt, todayUTC()),
      )
    );
  return Number(row?.total ?? 0);
}

/** Размер FIFO-очереди (записи, ожидающие следующего дня) */
export async function getQueueSize(): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ id: emissionLogsTable.id })
    .from(emissionLogsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(emissionLogsTable.isApproved, false),
        isNotNull(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor),
      )
    );
  return rows.length;
}

/**
 * Запросить эмиссию Дарминов для гражданина.
 * Если дневной лимит исчерпан — записывает в FIFO-очередь (isApproved=false, scheduledFor=следующий 00:00 UTC).
 * Возвращает { approved: true } если зачислено немедленно, { approved: false, scheduledFor } если встало в очередь.
 */
export async function requestEmission(
  citizenId: number,
  amount: number,
  reason: string
): Promise<{ approved: boolean; scheduledFor?: Date }> {
  const currentTotal = await getDailyApprovedTotal();

  if (currentTotal + amount <= DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT) {
    await db.insert(emissionLogsTable).values({
      citizenId,
      amount,
      reason,
      dailyTotal: currentTotal + amount,
      isApproved: true,
      scheduledFor: null,
    });
    logger.info({ citizenId, amount, reason, dailyTotal: currentTotal + amount }, "Emission approved");
    return { approved: true };
  }

  const scheduledFor = tomorrowUTC();
  await db.insert(emissionLogsTable).values({
    citizenId,
    amount,
    reason,
    dailyTotal: currentTotal,
    isApproved: false,
    scheduledFor,
  });
  logger.warn(
    { citizenId, amount, reason, currentTotal, scheduledFor },
    "Emission queued (daily limit reached)"
  );
  return { approved: false, scheduledFor };
}

/**
 * Cron-задача 00:00 UTC — дрейнировать FIFO-очередь.
 * Одобряет pending-записи, пока не упрётся в лимит нового дня.
 * Возвращает количество обработанных записей.
 */
export async function drainEmissionQueue(): Promise<number> {
  const pending = await db
    .select()
    .from(emissionLogsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(emissionLogsTable.isApproved, false),
        isNotNull(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor),
        lte(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor!, new Date()),
      )
    )
    .orderBy(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor);

  if (pending.length === 0) return 0;

  let processed = 0;
  let runningTotal = 0;

  for (const entry of pending) {
    if (runningTotal + entry.amount > DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT) break;

    await db
      .update(emissionLogsTable)
      .set({ isApproved: true, dailyTotal: runningTotal + entry.amount })
      .where(eq(emissionLogsTable.id, entry.id));

    runningTotal += entry.amount;
    processed++;
  }

  logger.info({ processed, newDailyTotal: runningTotal }, "Emission queue drained");
  return processed;
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/encryptionService.ts ---
/**
 * AES-256-GCM symmetric encryption for PII fields (email, surname, givenNames).
 *
 * Key source (priority order):
 *   1. ENCRYPTION_KEY env var — 32-byte hex (64 hex chars) or base64 (44 chars)
 *   2. Derived from SESSION_SECRET via scrypt (development fallback, logs a warning)
 *
 * Format: `v1:<iv_hex>:<authTag_hex>:<ciphertext_hex>`
 * Prefix `v1:` lets future versions detect format changes.
 * Values that don't start with `v1:` are treated as plaintext (backwards-compat).
 */

import crypto from "crypto";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const ALGO = "aes-256-gcm";

function resolveKey(): Buffer {
  const raw = process.env["ENCRYPTION_KEY"];
  if (raw) {
    if (raw.length === 64) return Buffer.from(raw, "hex");
    if (raw.length === 44) return Buffer.from(raw, "base64");
    if (raw.length >= 32)  return Buffer.from(raw.slice(0, 32));
  }

  // Fallback: derive from SESSION_SECRET (acceptable in development)
  const secret = process.env["SESSION_SECRET"] ?? "timenation-dev-secret";
  const salt   = Buffer.from("timenation-pii-salt-v1");
  logger.warn("ENCRYPTION_KEY not set — deriving key from SESSION_SECRET (set ENCRYPTION_KEY in production)");
  return crypto.scryptSync(secret, salt, 32);
}

// Resolved once at module load
const KEY: Buffer = resolveKey();

// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Encrypt plaintext → `v1:<iv>:<authTag>:<ciphertext>` (hex-encoded parts) */
export function encrypt(plaintext: string): string {
  const iv     = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(ALGO, KEY, iv);
  const ct     = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const tag    = cipher.getAuthTag();
  return `v1:${iv.toString("hex")}:${tag.toString("hex")}:${ct.toString("hex")}`;
}

/** Decrypt a `v1:…` ciphertext back to plaintext. Returns null on any error. */
export function decrypt(ciphertext: string): string | null {
  if (!ciphertext.startsWith("v1:")) return null;
  try {
    const parts = ciphertext.split(":");
    if (parts.length !== 4) return null;
    const [, ivHex, tagHex, ctHex] = parts as [string, string, string, string];
    const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(ALGO, KEY, Buffer.from(ivHex, "hex"));
    decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(tagHex, "hex"));
    return decipher.update(Buffer.from(ctHex, "hex")).toString("utf8") + decipher.final("utf8");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Safe decrypt: returns the original value if it is not encrypted (legacy rows)
 * or null/undefined as-is. Never throws.
 */
export function tryDecrypt(value: string | null | undefined): string | null | undefined {
  if (value == null) return value;
  if (!value.startsWith("v1:")) return value; // plaintext legacy row
  return decrypt(value) ?? value;             // return raw on failure (don't lose data)
}

/** Encrypt a nullable string — returns null unchanged */
export function encryptNullable(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  return encrypt(value);
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/freeVideoApiService.ts ---
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import os from "os";
import { spawn } from "child_process";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Free Video API Service ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Provider chain:
//   1. HuggingFace text-to-video-ms-1.7b  (free, optional HF_TOKEN for higher limits)
//   2. Pollinations AI slideshow (8 images → FFmpeg crossfade → 60s video)
//   3. null → caller falls back to solid-color light path

const HF_API_URL =
  "https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/damo-vilab/text-to-video-ms-1.7b";

const HF_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000; // 120 seconds — model may be loading/queued

const POLLINATIONS_IMAGES = 8;
const CROSSFADE_DURATION_SEC = 2; // seconds per crossfade transition
const TARGET_VIDEO_DURATION_SEC = 60;
// per_img = (total + C*(N-1)) / N → ensures output is exactly 60s
// With N=8, C=2: (60 + 14) / 8 = 9.25s per image
const PER_IMG_SEC = (TARGET_VIDEO_DURATION_SEC + CROSSFADE_DURATION_SEC * (POLLINATIONS_IMAGES - 1)) / POLLINATIONS_IMAGES;

// ── Utility: fetch with AbortController timeout ───────────────────────────────
async function fetchWithTimeout(
  url: string,
  init: RequestInit,
  timeoutMs: number,
  externalSignal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<Response> {
  const timeoutController = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => timeoutController.abort(), timeoutMs);
  const signal = externalSignal
    ? AbortSignal.any([timeoutController.signal, externalSignal])
    : timeoutController.signal;
  try {
    return await fetch(url, { ...init, signal });
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

// ── Provider 1: HuggingFace text-to-video ─────────────────────────────────────
async function generateViaHuggingFace(
  prompt: string,
  tmpDir: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string | null> {
  const hfToken = process.env["HF_TOKEN"];
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
  };
  if (hfToken) {
    headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${hfToken}`;
  }

  logger.info(
    { prompt: prompt.slice(0, 80), hasToken: !!hfToken },
    "HuggingFace text-to-video: requesting"
  );

  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetchWithTimeout(
      HF_API_URL,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({ inputs: prompt }),
      },
      HF_TIMEOUT_MS,
      signal
    );
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const isAbort =
      err instanceof Error && (err.name === "AbortError" || err.message.includes("abort"));
    if (isAbort) {
      logger.warn({ prompt: prompt.slice(0, 60) }, "HuggingFace timeout after 120s — switching to Pollinations");
    } else {
      logger.warn({ err }, "HuggingFace network error — switching to Pollinations");
    }
    return null;
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const body = await response.text().catch(() => "");
    // 503 = model loading (may return in retry), but we don't retry to keep queue moving
    logger.warn(
      { status: response.status, body: body.slice(0, 200) },
      "HuggingFace returned error — switching to Pollinations"
    );
    return null;
  }

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.includes("video") && !contentType.includes("octet-stream")) {
    logger.warn(
      { contentType },
      "HuggingFace response is not video — switching to Pollinations"
    );
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const arrayBuf = await response.arrayBuffer();
    if (arrayBuf.byteLength < 1024) {
      logger.warn({ bytes: arrayBuf.byteLength }, "HuggingFace returned too-small blob");
      return null;
    }
    const videoPath = path.join(tmpDir, "hf_video.mp4");
    fs.writeFileSync(videoPath, Buffer.from(arrayBuf));
    logger.info({ bytes: arrayBuf.byteLength, videoPath }, "HuggingFace video saved");
    return videoPath;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "HuggingFace: failed to save video blob");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Provider 2: Pollinations AI slideshow ─────────────────────────────────────
async function generateViaPollinationsSlideshow(
  prompt: string,
  tmpDir: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string | null> {
  logger.info(
    { prompt: prompt.slice(0, 80), count: POLLINATIONS_IMAGES },
    "Pollinations slideshow: fetching images"
  );

  const encodedPrompt = encodeURIComponent(prompt);

  // Fetch all images in parallel
  const imageBuffers = await Promise.all(
    Array.from({ length: POLLINATIONS_IMAGES }, async (_, i) => {
      const url =
        `https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/${encodedPrompt}` +
        `?width=1280&height=720&nologo=true&seed=${i * 7 + 42}`;
      try {
        const res = await fetchWithTimeout(url, {}, 30_000, signal);
        if (!res.ok) {
          logger.warn({ status: res.status, i }, "Pollinations image fetch failed");
          return null;
        }
        const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
        if (buf.byteLength < 1024) return null;
        return buf;
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn({ err, i }, "Pollinations image error");
        return null;
      }
    })
  );

  const validImages = imageBuffers.filter((b): b is Buffer<ArrayBuffer> => b !== null);
  if (validImages.length === 0) {
    logger.warn("Pollinations: all image fetches failed");
    return null;
  }

  logger.info({ count: validImages.length }, "Pollinations: images fetched, building crossfade slideshow");

  // Save images to disk
  const imagePaths = validImages.map((buf, i) => {
    const imgPath = path.join(tmpDir, `slide_${i}.jpg`);
    fs.writeFileSync(imgPath, buf);
    return imgPath;
  });

  const N = imagePaths.length;
  const C = CROSSFADE_DURATION_SEC;
  // Per-image duration adjusted so output equals TARGET_VIDEO_DURATION_SEC exactly
  const perImg = (TARGET_VIDEO_DURATION_SEC + C * (N - 1)) / N;

  const outputPath = path.join(tmpDir, "slideshow.mp4");

  try {
    if (N === 1) {
      // Edge case: only 1 image — simple loop
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-loop", "1", "-t", String(TARGET_VIDEO_DURATION_SEC), "-i", imagePaths[0]!,
        "-vf", "scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1,fps=25",
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "28", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
        "-an", "-y", outputPath,
      ], signal);
    } else {
      // Build -loop 1 -t {perImg} -i {img} inputs for each image
      const inputArgs: string[] = [];
      for (const imgPath of imagePaths) {
        inputArgs.push("-loop", "1", "-t", perImg.toFixed(3), "-i", imgPath);
      }

      // Build filter_complex: scale each input, then chain xfade between consecutive pairs
      // offset_i = (i+1)*perImg - i*C  (cumulative offset in output timeline)
      const scaleFilters = imagePaths.map(
        (_, i) =>
          `[${i}:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,` +
          `pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1,fps=25[sv${i}]`
      );

      const xfadeChains: string[] = [];
      let prevLabel = `sv0`;
      for (let i = 0; i < N - 1; i++) {
        const offset = ((i + 1) * perImg - i * C).toFixed(3);
        const outLabel = i === N - 2 ? "vout" : `xf${i}`;
        xfadeChains.push(
          `[${prevLabel}][sv${i + 1}]xfade=transition=fade:duration=${C}:offset=${offset}[${outLabel}]`
        );
        prevLabel = outLabel;
      }

      const filterComplex = [...scaleFilters, ...xfadeChains].join(";");

      await runFFmpeg([
        ...inputArgs,
        "-filter_complex", filterComplex,
        "-map", "[vout]",
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "28", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
        "-t", String(TARGET_VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        "-an", "-y", outputPath,
      ], signal);
    }

    logger.info({ outputPath, images: N, perImgSec: perImg.toFixed(2) }, "Pollinations crossfade slideshow created");
    return outputPath;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Pollinations: FFmpeg crossfade failed");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Main entry point ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * Attempt to generate an AI background video for the given prompt.
 * Returns a path to a local temporary video file, or null on total failure.
 * Caller is responsible for cleaning up the tmpDir.
 */
export async function generateAIBackground(
  prompt: string,
  tmpDir?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<{ videoPath: string | null; ownsTmpDir: boolean; tmpDir: string }> {
  const dir = tmpDir ?? fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "tn-aibg-"));
  const ownsTmpDir = !tmpDir;

  // Try HuggingFace first
  const hfPath = await generateViaHuggingFace(prompt, dir, signal);
  if (hfPath) {
    return { videoPath: hfPath, ownsTmpDir, tmpDir: dir };
  }

  // Fallback: Pollinations slideshow
  const slidePath = await generateViaPollinationsSlideshow(prompt, dir, signal);
  if (slidePath) {
    return { videoPath: slidePath, ownsTmpDir, tmpDir: dir };
  }

  // Total failure — caller will use solid-color fallback
  logger.warn({ prompt: prompt.slice(0, 60) }, "All AI video providers failed — using solid-color fallback");
  return { videoPath: null, ownsTmpDir, tmpDir: dir };
}

// ── FFmpeg helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function runFFmpeg(args: string[], signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const proc = spawn("ffmpeg", args, { stdio: ["ignore", "ignore", "pipe"] });
    let stderr = "";
    proc.stderr?.on("data", (d: Buffer) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
    proc.on("close", (code) => {
      if (code === 0) resolve();
      else reject(new Error(`FFmpeg exited ${code}: ${stderr.slice(-800)}`));
    });
    proc.on("error", (err) => reject(err));
    if (signal) {
      signal.addEventListener("abort", () => { proc.kill("SIGTERM"); }, { once: true });
    }
  });
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/geminiService.ts ---
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Gemini client (lazy-init, falls back gracefully if key missing) ───────────
let _genai: GoogleGenerativeAI | null = null;

function getClient(): GoogleGenerativeAI | null {
  if (_genai) return _genai;
  const key = process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
  if (!key) {
    logger.warn("GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — Gemini manifest generation disabled");
    return null;
  }
  _genai = new GoogleGenerativeAI(key);
  return _genai;
}

// ── System prompt for manifesto generation ────────────────────────────────────
// Designed for 60-second voiced broadcast (≈120–140 words at natural Russian speech pace)
const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Ты — TimeNation Thought Expander, нейронный усилитель голоса гражданина цифрового государства.

ЦЕЛЬ: Взять короткую мысль гражданина и развернуть её в точный 60-секундный манифест о синхронизированном глобальном вещании и суверенитете времени — в точном голосе самого гражданина.

ПРАВИЛА:
- Длина: 120–140 слов. Это ровно 60 секунд звучания при естественном темпе речи.
- Язык: живой русский, первое лицо («я», «мы»). Звучит как живой человек — не как академический манифест.
- Стиль: личный, убеждённый, кинематографически точный. Без пафоса ради пафоса.
- Структура: вступление (2 предл.) → ядро мысли (3–4 предл.) → контекст TimeNation (2 предл.) → финал с зафиксированной координатой (1 предл.).
- Только связный текст, пригодный для озвучки. Никаких заголовков, списков, кавычек, скобок.
- Упомяни: 1440 минут в сутках, глобальное вещание как акт суверенитета.
- НЕ упоминай реальные деньги, реальную политику или конкретные страны.
- Завершить словом о зафиксированном моменте: "Координата запечатана", "Сеть слышит меня", "Таймлайн принял мой сигнал" или аналогичным.`;

// ── Retry with exponential backoff ───────────────────────────────────────────
async function withRetry<T>(
  fn: () => Promise<T>,
  retries = 3,
  baseDelayMs = 1000
): Promise<T> {
  let lastErr: unknown;
  for (let i = 0; i < retries; i++) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (err) {
      lastErr = err;
      const delay = baseDelayMs * 2 ** i;
      logger.warn({ err, attempt: i + 1, retries }, `Gemini retry in ${delay}ms`);
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delay));
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}

// ── Generate manifesto via Gemini ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function generateManifesto(
  seedText: string,
  slotDate: string,
  minuteIndex: number
): Promise<string | null> {
  const client = getClient();
  if (!client) return null;

  const hour = String(Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const min = String(minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");

  const userPrompt =
    `Дата эфира: ${slotDate} ${hour}:${min} UTC\n` +
    `Зерно мысли гражданина: «${seedText.trim().slice(0, 400)}»\n\n` +
    `Напиши манифест-капсулу для этой временной координаты.`;

  try {
    const manifesto = await withRetry(async () => {
      const model = client.getGenerativeModel({
        model: "gemini-2.0-flash",
        systemInstruction: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        generationConfig: {
          maxOutputTokens: 512,
          temperature: 0.88,
          topP: 0.93,
        },
      });

      const result = await model.generateContent(userPrompt);
      const text = result.response.text().trim();
      if (!text) throw new Error("Empty Gemini response");
      return text;
    });

    logger.info(
      { slotDate, minuteIndex, words: manifesto.split(/\s+/).length },
      "Gemini manifesto generated"
    );
    return manifesto;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err, seedText: seedText.slice(0, 80) }, "Gemini manifest generation failed");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Check if Gemini is configured ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function isGeminiConfigured(): boolean {
  return !!process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/idCardService.ts ---
/**
 * ID Card generator — matches the TimeNation template (blue card, photo right, MRZ bottom).
 * Output: SVG string with embedded photo (base64 data-URI) if available.
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { MEDIA_DIR } from "./renderService";
import { EMBLEM_DATA_URI, FLAG_DATA_URI } from "./assetsService";

// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function p2(n: number) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }

function esc(s: string) {
  return s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}

const MONTHS = ["JAN","FEB","MAR","APR","MAY","JUN","JUL","AUG","SEP","OCT","NOV","DEC"] as const;

function formatDob(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!raw || raw.length < 10) return "—";
  const m = MONTHS[parseInt(raw.slice(5, 7), 10) - 1] ?? "?";
  return `${raw.slice(8, 10)} ${m} ${raw.slice(0, 4)}`;
}

// ── Transliteration (Cyrillic → Latin, ICAO-style) ────────────────────────────

const TRANSLIT: Record<string, string> = {
  А:"A", Б:"B", В:"V", Г:"G", Д:"D", Е:"E", Ё:"E", Ж:"ZH", З:"Z",
  И:"I", Й:"I", К:"K", Л:"L", М:"M", Н:"N", О:"O", П:"P", Р:"R",
  С:"S", Т:"T", У:"U", Ф:"F", Х:"KH", Ц:"TS", Ч:"CH", Ш:"SH",
  Щ:"SHCH", Ъ:"", Ы:"Y", Ь:"", Э:"E", Ю:"IU", Я:"IA",
};
function translit(s: string): string {
  return s.toUpperCase().split("").map(c => TRANSLIT[c] ?? c).join("");
}

// ── MRZ (ICAO TD1, 3×30 chars) ────────────────────────────────────────────────

function mrzCheck(str: string): number {
  const weights = [7, 3, 1];
  const VALUES: Record<string, number> = { "<": 0 };
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) VALUES[String(i)] = i;
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) VALUES[String.fromCharCode(65 + i)] = i + 10;
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    sum += (VALUES[str[i] ?? "<"] ?? 0) * (weights[i % 3] ?? 1);
  }
  return sum % 10;
}

function buildMrz(c: {
  citizenNumber: string;
  surname: string | null | undefined;
  givenNames: string | null | undefined;
  name: string;
  dateOfBirth: string | null | undefined;
}): [string, string] {
  const sn = translit(c.surname ?? c.name.split(" ")[0] ?? c.name).replace(/\s+/g, "<").slice(0, 15);
  const gn = translit((c.givenNames ?? c.name.split(" ").slice(1).join(" ")) || c.name).replace(/\s+/g, "<").slice(0, 13);
  const line1 = `ID${("TN" + (c.citizenNumber ?? "").padStart(9, "0")).slice(0, 9).padEnd(9, "<")}${mrzCheck((c.citizenNumber ?? "").padEnd(9, "<"))}${("" + "").padEnd(15, "<")}`.padEnd(30, "<").slice(0, 30);
  const dobMrz = c.dateOfBirth ? `${c.dateOfBirth.slice(2,4)}${c.dateOfBirth.slice(5,7)}${c.dateOfBirth.slice(8,10)}` : "000000";
  const line2 = `${dobMrz}${mrzCheck(dobMrz)}M9912319`.padEnd(29, "<").slice(0, 29) + "0";
  const namePart = `${sn}<<${gn}`.padEnd(30, "<").slice(0, 30);
  return [line1, line2 + " " + namePart.slice(0, 28)];
}

// ── EU-style star ring ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function starRing(cx: number, cy: number, r: number, count: number, fill: string): string {
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const a = (2 * Math.PI * i) / count - Math.PI / 2;
    const x = (cx + r * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(1);
    const y = (cy + r * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(1);
    out += `<text x="${x}" y="${y}" font-family="Arial,sans-serif" font-size="11" fill="${fill}" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" opacity="0.75">★</text>`;
  }
  return out;
}

// ── Photo loader (disk → base64 data-URI) ─────────────────────────────────────

/** VAULT_DIR is exported from citizens router; lazy-resolved here to avoid circular deps */
function resolveVaultDir(): string {
  return path.join(process.cwd(), "secure_vault");
}

export function loadPhotoAsDataUri(photoUrl: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!photoUrl || photoUrl === "vault:protected") return null;

  let filePath: string;

  if (photoUrl.startsWith("vault:")) {
    // New secure vault path: vault:photos/photo_123.jpg
    const rel = photoUrl.slice("vault:".length); // e.g. "photos/photo_123.jpg"
    filePath = path.join(resolveVaultDir(), rel);
  } else if (photoUrl.startsWith("/media/")) {
    // Legacy path: /media/avatars/avatar_123.jpg
    const rel = photoUrl.slice("/media/".length);
    filePath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, rel);
  } else {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return null;
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
    const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
    const mime = ext === ".png" ? "image/png" : ext === ".webp" ? "image/webp" : "image/jpeg";
    return `data:${mime};base64,${buf.toString("base64")}`;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Main generator ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface IdCardCitizen {
  citizenNumber: string;
  name: string;
  surname?: string | null;
  givenNames?: string | null;
  dateOfBirth?: string | null;
  rank: string;
  meritScore: number;
  createdAt: Date | string;
  photoUrl?: string | null;
  signatureBase64?: string | null;
}

export function generateIdCardSvg(c: IdCardCitizen, photoDataUri?: string | null): string {
  const surnameVal  = (c.surname  ?? c.name.split(" ")[0] ?? c.name).toUpperCase();
  const givenVal    = (c.givenNames ?? (c.name.split(" ").slice(1).join(" ") || c.name)).toUpperCase();
  const dobDisplay  = formatDob(c.dateOfBirth);

  const issued = new Date(c.createdAt);
  const issueDT = `${p2(issued.getDate())}.${p2(issued.getMonth() + 1)}.${issued.getFullYear()}  ${p2(issued.getHours())}:${p2(issued.getMinutes())} UTC`;

  const [mrz1, mrz2] = buildMrz({
    citizenNumber: c.citizenNumber,
    surname: c.surname,
    givenNames: c.givenNames,
    name: c.name,
    dateOfBirth: c.dateOfBirth,
  });

  // Layout constants
  const W = 856, H = 540;  // ID-1 aspect ratio scaled up
  const PHOTO_X = 584, PHOTO_Y = 82, PHOTO_W = 240, PHOTO_H = 308;
  const DATA_X = 32;
  const MRZ_Y = H - 96;

  const stars = starRing(PHOTO_X + PHOTO_W / 2, PHOTO_Y + PHOTO_H / 2, PHOTO_W / 2 + 26, 12, "#b8d4f0");

  const photoEl = photoDataUri
    ? `<image href="${esc(photoDataUri)}" x="${PHOTO_X}" y="${PHOTO_Y}" width="${PHOTO_W}" height="${PHOTO_H}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" clip-path="url(#photoClip)"/>`
    : `<!-- silhouette --><circle cx="${PHOTO_X + PHOTO_W / 2}" cy="${PHOTO_Y + 110}" r="58" fill="#b8d0e8"/>
       <ellipse cx="${PHOTO_X + PHOTO_W / 2}" cy="${PHOTO_Y + PHOTO_H - 20}" rx="84" ry="58" fill="#b8d0e8"/>`;

  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink"
     width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}">
  <defs>
    <pattern id="dp" width="13" height="13" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
      <circle cx="6.5" cy="6.5" r="0.85" fill="#7aadd0" opacity="0.38"/>
    </pattern>
    <linearGradient id="bg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0%"   stop-color="#bdd4ed"/>
      <stop offset="60%"  stop-color="#cfe2f4"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#d8eaf8"/>
    </linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="mrzBg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0%"   stop-color="#06101e"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#091525"/>
    </linearGradient>
    <clipPath id="card">  <rect width="${W}" height="${H}" rx="18"/></clipPath>
    <clipPath id="photoClip"><rect x="${PHOTO_X}" y="${PHOTO_Y}" width="${PHOTO_W}" height="${PHOTO_H}" rx="6"/></clipPath>
  </defs>

  <!-- ── Background ─────────────────────────────────────────── -->
  <rect width="${W}" height="${H}" rx="18" fill="url(#bg)"/>
  <rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="url(#dp)" clip-path="url(#card)"/>

  <!-- Large ∞ watermark (center-right) -->
  <text x="490" y="390" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="380"
        fill="#4a80b8" opacity="0.085" text-anchor="middle"
        clip-path="url(#card)">∞</text>

  <!-- EU-style stars around photo -->
  ${stars}

  <!-- ── Header ─────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
  <!-- Emblem (top-left, real image) -->
  ${EMBLEM_DATA_URI ? `<defs><clipPath id="emblemClipL"><circle cx="53" cy="37" r="26"/></clipPath></defs>
  <image href="${EMBLEM_DATA_URI}" x="27" y="11" width="52" height="52" clip-path="url(#emblemClipL)" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"/>
  <circle cx="53" cy="37" r="26" fill="none" stroke="#8ab4d4" stroke-width="1.2"/>` : `<rect x="22" y="16" width="62" height="42" rx="4" fill="#1a3988"/>
  <text x="53" y="41" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="13" fill="#d4ecff" text-anchor="middle">∞</text>`}

  <text x="92"  y="34" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="24" font-weight="bold" fill="#132e7a" letter-spacing="1">TIMENATION</text>
  <text x="92"  y="51" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="12" fill="#3a5a9e">Republic of TimeNation</text>

  <!-- Flag (top-right, real image) -->
  ${FLAG_DATA_URI ? `<defs><clipPath id="flagClipR"><rect x="${W - 110}" y="10" width="88" height="54" rx="4"/></clipPath></defs>
  <image href="${FLAG_DATA_URI}" x="${W - 110}" y="10" width="88" height="54" clip-path="url(#flagClipR)" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"/>
  <rect x="${W - 110}" y="10" width="88" height="54" rx="4" fill="none" stroke="#8ab4d4" stroke-width="1"/>` : `<text x="${W - 24}" y="34" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="24" font-weight="bold" fill="#132e7a" text-anchor="end" letter-spacing="1">TIMENATION</text>
  <text x="${W - 24}" y="51" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="12" fill="#3a5a9e" text-anchor="end">FUTURE BEGINS NOW</text>`}

  <line x1="22" y1="72" x2="${W - 22}" y2="72" stroke="#88aed0" stroke-width="1.1"/>

  <!-- ── Photo box ───────────────────────────────────────────── -->
  <rect x="${PHOTO_X - 2}" y="${PHOTO_Y - 2}" width="${PHOTO_W + 4}" height="${PHOTO_H + 4}" rx="8" fill="none" stroke="#8ab4d2" stroke-width="1.4"/>
  <rect x="${PHOTO_X}" y="${PHOTO_Y}" width="${PHOTO_W}" height="${PHOTO_H}" rx="6" fill="#d0e4f4"/>
  ${photoEl}
  <!-- Subtle vignette on photo -->
  <rect x="${PHOTO_X}" y="${PHOTO_Y}" width="${PHOTO_W}" height="${PHOTO_H}" rx="6"
        fill="none" stroke="#6090b8" stroke-width="0.6" clip-path="url(#photoClip)"/>

  <!-- ── Data fields (left side) ───────────────────────────── -->

  <!-- SURNAME -->
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="96"  font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a4fc4" letter-spacing="1.5">SURNAME</text>
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="120" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="22" font-weight="500" fill="#080f20">${esc(surnameVal)}</text>

  <!-- GIVEN NAME -->
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="146" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a4fc4" letter-spacing="1.5">GIVEN NAME</text>
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="170" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="22" font-weight="500" fill="#080f20">${esc(givenVal)}</text>

  <!-- NATIONALITY -->
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="196" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a4fc4" letter-spacing="1.5">NATIONALITY</text>
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="220" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="22" font-weight="500" fill="#080f20">TIMENATION</text>

  <!-- DATE OF BIRTH -->

  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="270" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="22" font-weight="500" fill="#080f20">${esc(dobDisplay)}</text>

  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="296" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a4fc4" letter-spacing="1.5">DATE / TIME OF ISSUE</text>
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="320" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="22" font-weight="500" fill="#080f20">${esc(issueDT)}</text>

  <!-- Citizen No. + Rank (bottom-left of data area) -->
  <line x1="${DATA_X}" y1="336" x2="560" y2="336" stroke="#8ab0d0" stroke-width="0.7" stroke-dasharray="3,3"/>

  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="355" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a4fc4" letter-spacing="1.5">DOCUMENT NO.</text>
  <text x="${DATA_X}" y="378" font-family="Courier New,monospace" font-size="20" font-weight="bold" fill="#080f20" letter-spacing="4">${esc(c.citizenNumber)}</text>

  <text x="240" y="355" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="10.5" font-weight="bold" fill="#1a4fc4" letter-spacing="1.5">RANK / MERIT</text>
  <text x="240" y="378" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" font-size="18" fill="#080f20">${esc(c.rank)}  ·  ${c.meritScore.toFixed(1)}</text>

  <!-- ── Signature line ──────────────────────────────────────── -->
  ${c.signatureBase64 ? `<image href="${esc(c.signatureBase64)}" x="${DATA_X}" y="388" width="222" height="30" preserveAspectRatio="xMinYMid meet" opacity="0.92"/>` : ""}
  <line x1="${DATA_X}" y1="424" x2="322" y2="424" stroke="#7090b8" stroke-width="0.9"/>
  <text x="${DATA_X + 4}" y="436" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="10" fill="#4a6a9a" font-style="italic">SIGNATURE OF HOLDER</text>

  <!-- ── MRZ Zone ─────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
  <rect x="0" y="${MRZ_Y}" width="${W}" height="${H - MRZ_Y}" fill="url(#mrzBg)" clip-path="url(#card)"/>
  <rect x="0" y="${MRZ_Y}" width="${W}" height="2.5" fill="#1a5296"/>

  <!-- MRZ line 1 -->
  <text x="22" y="${MRZ_Y + 34}" font-family="Courier New,monospace" font-size="16.5" fill="#4a88c8" letter-spacing="3.2">${esc(mrz1.padEnd(30,"<"))}</text>
  <!-- MRZ line 2 -->
  <text x="22" y="${MRZ_Y + 62}" font-family="Courier New,monospace" font-size="16.5" fill="#4a88c8" letter-spacing="3.2">${esc(mrz2.padEnd(30,"<"))}</text>

  <!-- Emblem stamp bottom-right (real image) -->
  ${EMBLEM_DATA_URI
    ? `<defs><clipPath id="stampClip"><circle cx="${W - 60}" cy="${MRZ_Y + 48}" r="34"/></clipPath></defs>
  <image href="${EMBLEM_DATA_URI}" x="${W - 94}" y="${MRZ_Y + 14}" width="68" height="68"
         clip-path="url(#stampClip)" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" opacity="0.55"/>
  <circle cx="${W - 60}" cy="${MRZ_Y + 48}" r="34" fill="none" stroke="#1e3a8a" stroke-width="1" opacity="0.4"/>`
    : `<circle cx="${W - 60}" cy="${MRZ_Y + 48}" r="34" fill="none" stroke="#1e3a8a" stroke-width="1.2" opacity="0.5"/>
  <text x="${W - 60}" y="${MRZ_Y + 43}" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="18" fill="#1e3a8a" text-anchor="middle" opacity="0.55">∞</text>`}
</svg>`;
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/imageService.ts ---
/**
 * imageService — Sharp-based image processing for citizen documents.
 *
 * cropSignature:        Trims transparent/white padding from a signature PNG (bounding-box crop).
 * normalizePassportPhoto: Resizes/crops an uploaded photo to passport proportions (400×514 px JPEG).
 */

import sharp from "sharp";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Signature bounding-box crop ───────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Accepts a PNG data-URI (from signature_pad), finds the tightest bounding box
 * of non-transparent pixels, crops to it (with a 4 px padding), and returns a
 * new data-URI. Falls back to the original if the canvas is blank or on error.
 */
export async function cropSignature(dataUri: string): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const base64 = dataUri.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
    const buffer = Buffer.from(base64, "base64");

    const { data, info } = await sharp(buffer)
      .ensureAlpha()
      .raw()
      .toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true });

    const { width, height, channels } = info;
    let top = height, left = width, right = 0, bottom = 0;
    let found = false;

    for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
        const idx = (y * width + x) * channels;
        const alpha = data[idx + 3] ?? 0;
        if (alpha > 10) {
          if (x < left)   left   = x;
          if (x > right)  right  = x;
          if (y < top)    top    = y;
          if (y > bottom) bottom = y;
          found = true;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!found || right <= left || bottom <= top) return dataUri;

    const PAD = 4;
    const exLeft   = Math.max(0, left   - PAD);
    const exTop    = Math.max(0, top    - PAD);
    const exWidth  = Math.min(width  - exLeft, right  - left + 1 + PAD * 2);
    const exHeight = Math.min(height - exTop,  bottom - top  + 1 + PAD * 2);

    const cropped = await sharp(buffer)
      .extract({ left: exLeft, top: exTop, width: exWidth, height: exHeight })
      .png()
      .toBuffer();

    logger.info(
      { original: `${width}×${height}`, cropped: `${exWidth}×${exHeight}` },
      "Signature cropped"
    );

    return `data:image/png;base64,${cropped.toString("base64")}`;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "cropSignature failed — using original");
    return dataUri;
  }
}

// ── Passport photo normalisation ──────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Passport proportions: 35×45 mm  ≈  400×514 px @ ~291 dpi.
 * The image is resized with cover-crop (top-biased to keep the face), then
 * saved as a progressive JPEG (quality 85) at the same vault path.
 *
 * Returns the normalised vault path (always ends in `.jpg`).
 * The caller must update `photoUrl` in the DB if the path changes.
 */
export async function normalizePassportPhoto(vaultFilePath: string): Promise<string> {
  const PASSPORT_W = 400;
  const PASSPORT_H = 514;

  const ext = path.extname(vaultFilePath).toLowerCase();
  const jpgPath = ext === ".jpg" || ext === ".jpeg"
    ? vaultFilePath
    : vaultFilePath.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ".jpg");

  try {
    await sharp(vaultFilePath)
      .resize(PASSPORT_W, PASSPORT_H, {
        fit: "cover",
        position: "top",   // bias toward top so face is kept
      })
      .jpeg({ quality: 85, progressive: true })
      .toFile(jpgPath);

    if (jpgPath !== vaultFilePath) {
      try { fs.unlinkSync(vaultFilePath); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    }

    logger.info({ jpgPath, size: `${PASSPORT_W}×${PASSPORT_H}` }, "Passport photo normalised");
    return jpgPath;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err, vaultFilePath }, "normalizePassportPhoto failed — keeping original");
    return vaultFilePath;
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/meritocracyService.ts ---
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, lt, or, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "./telegramService";

// ── Single Source of Truth for ranks ─────────────────────────────────────────

export const RANKS = [
  { threshold: 0,    name: "Гражданин" },
  { threshold: 100,  name: "Оператор времени" },
  { threshold: 500,  name: "Хранитель времени" },
  { threshold: 1000, name: "Архитектор потока" },
  { threshold: 2500, name: "Мастер хронологии" },
  { threshold: 5000, name: "Синхронизатор вечности" },
] as const;

export type RankName = (typeof RANKS)[number]["name"];

/**
 * Calculates the rank for a given merit score.
 * Iterates from highest threshold downward and returns the first match.
 */
export function calculateRank(meritScore: number): RankName {
  for (let i = RANKS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (meritScore >= RANKS[i]!.threshold) return RANKS[i]!.name;
  }
  return RANKS[0]!.name;
}

/** @deprecated Use calculateRank() */
export const getUserRank = calculateRank;

export function getRankIndex(rankName: string): number {
  return RANKS.findIndex((r) => r.name === rankName);
}

// ── Merit gain formula ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface MeritGainParams {
  minutesTransferred: number;
  dailyMinutesTotal: number;
  daysSinceRegistration: number;
}

export function calculateMeritGain(params: MeritGainParams): number {
  const { minutesTransferred, dailyMinutesTotal, daysSinceRegistration } = params;

  let merit = 1;
  merit += Math.floor(minutesTransferred / 10) * 0.1;
  if (dailyMinutesTotal > 60) merit += 5;
  const loyaltyCoeff = 1 + daysSinceRegistration / 100;
  merit = merit * loyaltyCoeff;

  return Math.round(merit * 100) / 100;
}

// ── Apply merit after a TiMETR session ────────────────────────────────────────

export async function applyMeritAfterSession(
  citizenId: number,
  minutesTransferred: number
): Promise<{ oldRank: string; newRank: string; meritGained: number; newScore: number }> {
  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error(`Citizen ${citizenId} not found`);

  const now = new Date();
  const daysSinceRegistration = Math.floor(
    (now.getTime() - citizen.createdAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
  );

  const meritGained = calculateMeritGain({
    minutesTransferred,
    dailyMinutesTotal: citizen.totalGiven,
    daysSinceRegistration,
  });

  const oldRank = citizen.rank;
  const newScore = Math.round((citizen.meritScore + meritGained) * 100) / 100;
  const newRank = calculateRank(newScore);

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ meritScore: newScore, rank: newRank, lastActivity: now })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

  logger.info(
    { citizenId, oldRank, newRank, meritGained, newScore },
    "Merit applied after session"
  );

  return { oldRank, newRank, meritGained, newScore };
}

// ── Weekly entropy (−0.5 merit for inactive citizens) ─────────────────────────

export async function applyEntropy(): Promise<void> {
  const sevenDaysAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

  const inactiveCitizens = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(
      or(
        isNull(citizensTable.lastActivity),
        lt(citizensTable.lastActivity, sevenDaysAgo)
      )
    );

  logger.info({ count: inactiveCitizens.length }, "Applying entropy to inactive citizens");

  for (const citizen of inactiveCitizens) {
    if (citizen.meritScore <= 0) continue;

    const oldRank = citizen.rank;
    const newScore = Math.max(0, Math.round((citizen.meritScore - 0.5) * 100) / 100);
    const newRank = calculateRank(newScore);

    await db
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ meritScore: newScore, rank: newRank })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizen.id));

    if (newRank !== oldRank && getRankIndex(newRank) < getRankIndex(oldRank)) {
      await sendTransactionNotification(
        citizen.id,
        `⚠️ *Казначейство TimeNation*\n\n` +
        `Внимание! Энтропия затронула ваш профиль.\n` +
        `Ваш статус понижен до *${newRank}*.\n\n` +
        `Проведите TiMETR-сессию, чтобы вернуть влияние.`
      ).catch(() => {});

      logger.info(
        { citizenId: citizen.id, oldRank, newRank },
        "Entropy rank downgrade"
      );
    }
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/nodeValidatorService.ts ---
/**
 * Node Validator Service — Heartbeat & GeoIP audit for embassy nodes.
 *
 * Every INTERVAL_MS it:
 *  1. Fetches all embassies that have a nodeIp set.
 *  2. Measures HTTP latency to the node IP.
 *  3. Looks up the IP via ip-api.com (free, no key required).
 *  4. Compares GeoIP coordinates with the declared embassy GPS.
 *  5. If the distance exceeds ANOMALY_KM or the country doesn't match,
 *     flags the node as "pending_verification" and logs the anomaly.
 *  6. Otherwise records the ping time and keeps status unchanged.
 */

import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { nationalEmbassiesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { isNotNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const INTERVAL_MS  = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes
const ANOMALY_KM   = 300;            // flag if GeoIP is >300 km from declared GPS
const FETCH_TIMEOUT = 6_000;         // 6 s timeout per node probe

// ── Haversine distance (km) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

function haversineKm(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): number {
  const R = 6371;
  const dLat = ((lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLng = ((lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos((lat1 * Math.PI) / 180) *
    Math.cos((lat2 * Math.PI) / 180) *
    Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
}

// ── GeoIP lookup ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface GeoIpResult {
  status: string;
  country: string;
  countryCode: string;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  city: string;
}

async function geoIpLookup(ip: string): Promise<GeoIpResult | null> {
  try {
    const ctrl = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), FETCH_TIMEOUT);
    const res = await fetch(`http://ip-api.com/json/${ip}?fields=status,country,countryCode,lat,lon,city`, {
      signal: ctrl.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    return await res.json() as GeoIpResult;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Latency probe via fetch timing ────────────────────────────────────────────

async function probePingMs(ip: string): Promise<number | null> {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  try {
    const ctrl = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), FETCH_TIMEOUT);
    // HEAD to port 80; most nodes won't have HTTP but the TCP RST is measurable
    await fetch(`http://${ip}`, { method: "HEAD", signal: ctrl.signal });
    clearTimeout(timer);
    return Date.now() - t0;
  } catch {
    // Connection refused or timeout — still record a conservative roundtrip if fast
    const elapsed = Date.now() - t0;
    return elapsed < FETCH_TIMEOUT ? elapsed : null;
  }
}

// ── Main validation pass ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function runValidationPass() {
  const embassies = await db
    .select()
    .from(nationalEmbassiesTable)
    .where(isNotNull(nationalEmbassiesTable.nodeIp));

  if (embassies.length === 0) return;

  logger.info({ count: embassies.length }, "NodeValidator: starting pass");

  for (const embassy of embassies) {
    const ip = embassy.nodeIp!;
    const pingMs = await probePingMs(ip);
    const geo    = await geoIpLookup(ip);

    const now = new Date();
    let anomaly: string | null = null;
    let newNodeStatus = embassy.nodeStatus;

    if (geo && geo.status === "success") {
      const distKm = haversineKm(embassy.gpsLat, embassy.gpsLng, geo.lat, geo.lon);

      if (distKm > ANOMALY_KM) {
        anomaly = `GeoIP distance anomaly: IP resolves to ${geo.city}, ${geo.country} (${distKm.toFixed(0)} km from declared location)`;
        if (newNodeStatus === "online") newNodeStatus = "pending_verification";
      }

      await db
        .update(nationalEmbassiesTable)
        .set({
          lastPingMs:      pingMs ?? embassy.lastPingMs,
          geoIpCountry:    geo.countryCode,
          geoIpLat:        geo.lat,
          geoIpLng:        geo.lon,
          lastValidatedAt: now,
          nodeStatus:      newNodeStatus,
          anomalyLog:      anomaly ?? embassy.anomalyLog,
        })
        .where(
          // @ts-ignore — drizzle eq import available via db
          (await import("drizzle-orm")).eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, embassy.id)
        );

      if (anomaly) {
        logger.warn({ embassyId: embassy.id, ip, anomaly }, "NodeValidator: anomaly detected");
      } else {
        logger.info({ embassyId: embassy.id, ip, pingMs, distKm: distKm.toFixed(0) }, "NodeValidator: node OK");
      }
    } else {
      // GeoIP lookup failed — just update ping
      await db
        .update(nationalEmbassiesTable)
        .set({ lastPingMs: pingMs ?? embassy.lastPingMs, lastValidatedAt: now })
        .where(
          (await import("drizzle-orm")).eq(nationalEmbassiesTable.id, embassy.id)
        );
    }
  }
}

// ── Start the daemon ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

let _timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

export function startNodeValidator() {
  if (_timer) return;
  // Delay first run by 30 s to let the server warm up
  setTimeout(() => {
    runValidationPass().catch(err =>
      logger.error({ err }, "NodeValidator: pass failed")
    );
    _timer = setInterval(() => {
      runValidationPass().catch(err =>
        logger.error({ err }, "NodeValidator: pass failed")
      );
    }, INTERVAL_MS);
  }, 30_000);

  logger.info({ intervalMin: INTERVAL_MS / 60000 }, "NodeValidator daemon started");
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/renderQueue.ts ---
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tvSlotsTable, tvCapsulesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { renderCapsule, renderLightVideo } from "./renderService";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "./telegramService";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Render Queue ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Limits concurrent AI renders so slow HuggingFace requests (up to 120s each)
// don't pile up and starve other API handlers.
//
// Design: simple in-process FIFO queue with a configurable concurrency cap.
// The DB capsule status (pending_render → rendered / draft) is the durable
// state; this queue is the execution gatekeeper only.

const MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS = parseInt(
  process.env["MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS"] ?? "2",
  10
);

/**
 * Per-job wall-clock timeout. If a render job exceeds this, it is abandoned
 * and an immediate solid-color fallback is produced so the queue keeps moving.
 * Default: 180 s. Override with RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS env var (generic fallback).
 */
const RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS = parseInt(
  process.env["RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS"] ?? "180000",
  10
);

/**
 * Timeout for light-path renders (citizen 1-minute slots).
 * Default: 120 s. Override with RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS env var.
 */
export const RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS = parseInt(
  process.env["RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS"] ?? "120000",
  10
);

/**
 * Timeout for heavy-path renders (Quarter / Half / Apex premium slots).
 * Default: 300 s. Override with RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS env var.
 */
export const RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS = parseInt(
  process.env["RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS"] ?? "300000",
  10
);

/** Sentinel error thrown by the per-job deadline race. */
class RenderTimeoutError extends Error {
  readonly elapsed: number;
  constructor(slotId: number, elapsed: number) {
    super(`Render timed out after ${elapsed}ms for slotId=${slotId}`);
    this.name = "RenderTimeoutError";
    this.elapsed = elapsed;
  }
}

interface RenderJob {
  jobId: string;
  slotId: number;
  citizenId: number;
  capsuleSeedText: string | null;
  /** Per-job timeout override in ms. Defaults to RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS when omitted. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

let activeCount = 0;
const queue: Array<() => void> = [];

/** Jobs indexed by jobId for status look-up. */
const jobStatus = new Map<
  string,
  { slotId: number; status: "queued" | "running" | "done" | "failed" }
>();

function drainQueue(): void {
  while (activeCount < MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS && queue.length > 0) {
    const next = queue.shift();
    next?.();
  }
}

/**
 * Enqueue a render job.
 * Returns a jobId immediately. The job will run when a concurrency slot opens.
 */
export function enqueueRender(job: RenderJob): string {
  const { jobId, slotId, citizenId, capsuleSeedText } = job;
  const effectiveTimeoutMs = job.timeoutMs ?? RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS;

  jobStatus.set(jobId, { slotId, status: "queued" });

  const run = () => {
    activeCount++;
    const entry = jobStatus.get(jobId);
    if (entry) entry.status = "running";

    const renderLog = logger.child({ slotId, citizenId, jobId });
    renderLog.info({ queueDepth: queue.length, active: activeCount, timeoutMs: effectiveTimeoutMs }, "Render job started");

    void (async () => {
      const startTime = Date.now();

      // AbortController shared with the render job — aborted when the deadline fires
      const abortController = new AbortController();

      // Build a deadline promise that rejects once effectiveTimeoutMs elapses
      let deadlineTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      const deadline = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
        deadlineTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
          abortController.abort();
          reject(new RenderTimeoutError(slotId, elapsed));
        }, effectiveTimeoutMs);
      });

      try {
        const result = await Promise.race([renderCapsule(slotId, abortController.signal), deadline]);

        clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);

        if (result.mediaUrl) {
          await db
            .update(tvSlotsTable)
            .set({ status: "broadcast" })
            .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));
          await db
            .update(tvCapsulesTable)
            .set({ status: "broadcast", renderProgress: "done", updatedAt: new Date() })
            .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
          renderLog.info("Capsule auto-published to broadcast");
        }

        const entry2 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
        if (entry2) entry2.status = "done";

        const tgMessage =
          `📡 *Капсула готова к эфиру!*\n\n` +
          `Слот #${slotId} отрендерен.\n` +
          (result.mediaUrl
            ? `🎬 Видео доступно в приложении TimeNation TV.`
            : `⚠️ Видео не сгенерировано.`);
        sendTransactionNotification(citizenId, tgMessage).catch((err) => {
          renderLog.warn({ err }, "Telegram notification failed after render");
        });
      } catch (err) {
        clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);

        if (err instanceof RenderTimeoutError) {
          // ── Timeout path: solid-color immediate fallback ──────────────────
          const elapsed = err.elapsed;
          renderLog.warn(
            { slotId, jobId, elapsed, timeoutMs: effectiveTimeoutMs },
            "Render job exceeded wall-clock timeout — falling back to solid-color render"
          );

          try {
            const [capsule] = await db
              .select({
                seedText: tvCapsulesTable.seedText,
                visualStyle: tvCapsulesTable.visualStyle,
              })
              .from(tvCapsulesTable)
              .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
              .limit(1);

            const manifestoText =
              capsule?.seedText ?? "TimeNation — суверенное государство времени.";

            const mediaUrl = await renderLightVideo(
              null,
              manifestoText,
              slotId,
              capsule?.visualStyle ?? null
            );

            await db
              .update(tvCapsulesTable)
              .set({ status: "rendered", renderProgress: "done", updatedAt: new Date() })
              .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
            await db
              .update(tvSlotsTable)
              .set({ status: "broadcast" })
              .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));

            renderLog.info(
              { slotId, jobId, elapsed, mediaUrl },
              "Solid-color fallback render complete after timeout"
            );

            const entry3 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
            if (entry3) entry3.status = "done";

            sendTransactionNotification(
              citizenId,
              `⏱ *Слот #${slotId} отрендерен* (упрощённый режим — AI-генерация превысила лимит времени)`
            ).catch((tgErr) => {
              renderLog.warn({ tgErr }, "Telegram notification failed after timeout fallback");
            });
          } catch (fallbackErr) {
            renderLog.error(
              { fallbackErr, slotId, jobId, elapsed },
              "Solid-color fallback render also failed after timeout"
            );

            const entry4 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
            if (entry4) entry4.status = "failed";

            await db
              .update(tvCapsulesTable)
              .set({ status: "draft", renderProgress: null, updatedAt: new Date() })
              .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
              .catch(() => {});
          }
        } else {
          // ── Normal error path ─────────────────────────────────────────────
          renderLog.error({ err }, "Render job failed");

          const entry5 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
          if (entry5) entry5.status = "failed";

          await db
            .update(tvCapsulesTable)
            .set({ status: "draft", renderProgress: null, updatedAt: new Date() })
            .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
            .catch(() => {});
        }
      } finally {
        activeCount--;
        renderLog.info(
          { remaining: queue.length, active: activeCount },
          "Render job finished — draining queue"
        );
        drainQueue();

        // Evict completed job status after 1 hour to prevent unbounded growth
        setTimeout(() => jobStatus.delete(jobId), 60 * 60 * 1000);
      }
    })();
  };

  if (activeCount < MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS) {
    run();
  } else {
    logger.info(
      { slotId, jobId, queueDepth: queue.length, active: activeCount },
      "Render job queued — concurrency cap reached"
    );
    queue.push(run);
  }

  return jobId;
}

/** Queue depth and active count for health/debug endpoints. */
export function getRenderQueueStats(): {
  active: number;
  queued: number;
  maxConcurrent: number;
} {
  return { active: activeCount, queued: queue.length, maxConcurrent: MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS };
}

/** Look up a job's current status. Returns null if unknown/evicted. */
export function getRenderJobStatus(jobId: string): {
  slotId: number;
  status: "queued" | "running" | "done" | "failed";
} | null {
  return jobStatus.get(jobId) ?? null;
}

/**
 * On server startup, re-queue any capsules stuck in `pending_render`.
 * These are jobs that were lost when the server crashed or was restarted.
 * The tvCapsulesTable.status column is the durable source of truth.
 */
export async function rehydrateRenderQueue(): Promise<void> {
  const stuck = await db
    .select({
      slotId: tvCapsulesTable.slotId,
      citizenId: tvCapsulesTable.citizenId,
      seedText: tvCapsulesTable.seedText,
      renderPath: tvCapsulesTable.renderPath,
    })
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.status, "pending_render"));

  if (stuck.length === 0) {
    logger.info("Render queue rehydration: no stuck jobs found");
    return;
  }

  logger.info(
    { count: stuck.length },
    "Render queue rehydration: re-queuing capsules stuck in pending_render"
  );

  for (const capsule of stuck) {
    const jobId = randomUUID();
    const timeoutMs =
      capsule.renderPath === "heavy"
        ? RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS
        : RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS;
    enqueueRender({
      jobId,
      slotId: capsule.slotId,
      citizenId: capsule.citizenId,
      capsuleSeedText: capsule.seedText,
      timeoutMs,
    });
  }
}

import { spawn } from "child_process";

  generateCinematicPromptTiered,
  generateMultiScenePrompts,
  type VisualStyle as CinematicVisualStyle,
  type RenderTier,
} from "./cinematicPromptService";

// ── Constants ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// ── Mode A: Template Manifesto Expansion (~130 words, 60-second fallback) ─────
// Used when Gemini is unavailable. Approx 120–140 words = 60 sec at natural pace.
export function expandSeedToManifesto(seed: string): string {
  const clean = seed.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  const idx = Math.abs(hashStr(clean)) % 3;

  const openings = [
    `Я говорю это прямо сейчас, в одну из 1440 минут, из которых состоят сутки. Моя мысль: «${clean}».`,
    `В глобальном эфире TimeNation каждая минута — координата. Сегодня моя: «${clean}».`,
    `Мир вещает непрерывно. Я выбираю свою минуту, чтобы сказать: «${clean}».`,
  ];

  const bodies = [
    `Эту минуту нельзя стереть, купить задним числом или подделать. Она выкуплена, зафиксирована в сети. Дармин сгорел — но координата осталась навсегда. Глобальное вещание — это не привилегия корпораций. Это право каждого гражданина, у которого есть что сказать миру прямо сейчас.`,
    `1440 слотов в сутках. Каждый — чья-то история, чей-то сигнал в будущее. Время — единственная абсолютно синхронная точка отсчёта на планете. Нет часового пояса, который обошёл бы полночь. В этой универсальности — сила. Я здесь не ради охвата. Я здесь ради точности своего голоса.`,
    `Прямо сейчас на планете идёт непрерывный эфир, который никто не контролирует централизованно. Граждане TimeNation занимают свои минуты — и каждая несёт реальную мысль реального человека. Не алгоритм, не бюджет. Просто голос, синхронизированный с глобальным временем. Именно так должно работать вещание будущего.`,
  ];

  const closings = [
    `Таймлайн принял мой сигнал. Координата запечатана.`,
    `Сеть слышит меня. Момент зафиксирован навсегда.`,
    `Минута выкуплена. Мой голос — часть глобального эфира.`,
  ];

  return `${openings[idx]}\n\n${bodies[idx]}\n\n${closings[idx]}`;
}

function hashStr(s: string): number {
  let h = 0;
  for (const c of s) { h = (Math.imul(31, h) + c.charCodeAt(0)) | 0; }
  return h;
}

// ── Google Cloud TTS (Service Account — Wavenet quality) ──────────────────────

function getGoogleTTSAuth() {
  const raw = process.env["GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON"];
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const credentials = JSON.parse(raw);
    return new google.auth.GoogleAuth({
      credentials,
      scopes: ["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform"],
    });
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "Failed to parse GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON for TTS");
    return null;
  }
}

async function googleCloudTTS(text: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const auth = getGoogleTTSAuth();
  if (!auth) return null;
  try {
    const accessToken = await auth.getAccessToken();
    if (!accessToken) return null;
    const response = await fetch(
      "https://texttospeech.googleapis.com/v1/text:synthesize",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          input: { text: text.slice(0, 5000) },
          voice: { languageCode: GOOGLE_TTS_LANG, name: GOOGLE_TTS_VOICE },
          audioConfig: { audioEncoding: "MP3", speakingRate: 1.0, pitch: 0 },
        }),
      }
    );
    if (!response.ok) {
      const body = await response.text();
      logger.warn({ status: response.status, body }, "Google Cloud TTS error");
      return null;
    }
    const data = (await response.json()) as { audioContent?: string };
    if (!data.audioContent) return null;
    return Buffer.from(data.audioContent, "base64");
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Google Cloud TTS failed");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Google Translate TTS fallback (no API key, chunked for >200 chars) ────────

/** Split text into sentence-aware chunks ≤200 chars */
function splitIntoChunks(text: string, maxLen = 180): string[] {
  const chunks: string[] = [];
  // Split at sentence boundaries first
  const sentences = text.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+/);
  let cur = "";
  for (const sent of sentences) {
    if ((cur + " " + sent).trim().length <= maxLen) {
      cur = (cur + " " + sent).trim();
    } else {
      if (cur) chunks.push(cur);
      // If single sentence is too long, split by comma/clause
      if (sent.length > maxLen) {
        const parts = sent.split(/,\s*/);
        let sub = "";
        for (const p of parts) {
          if ((sub + ", " + p).trim().length <= maxLen) {
            sub = sub ? sub + ", " + p : p;
          } else {
            if (sub) chunks.push(sub);
            sub = p;
          }
        }
        if (sub) cur = sub;
        else cur = "";
      } else {
        cur = sent;
      }
    }
  }
  if (cur) chunks.push(cur);
  return chunks.filter(Boolean);
}

async function fetchChunkTTS(chunk: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const url = `https://translate.google.com/translate_tts?ie=UTF-8&tl=ru&client=tw-ob&q=${encodeURIComponent(chunk)}`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; TimeNation/1.0)" },
    });
    if (!res.ok) { logger.warn({ status: res.status, chunk: chunk.slice(0, 30) }, "gTTS chunk failed"); return null; }
    return Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "gTTS chunk fetch error");
    return null;
  }
}

async function googleTranslateTTS(text: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const chunks = splitIntoChunks(text);
  if (chunks.length === 0) return null;
  logger.info({ chunks: chunks.length }, "gTTS: fetching chunks");

  const buffers = await Promise.all(chunks.map(fetchChunkTTS));
  const valid = buffers.filter((b): b is Buffer => b !== null);
  if (valid.length === 0) return null;
  if (valid.length === 1) return valid[0]!;

  // Concatenate MP3 chunks via FFmpeg concat filter
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "gtts-"));
  try {
    const tmpFiles = valid.map((buf, i) => {
      const f = path.join(tmpDir, `chunk_${i}.mp3`);
      fs.writeFileSync(f, buf);
      return f;
    });
    const listFile = path.join(tmpDir, "list.txt");
    fs.writeFileSync(listFile, tmpFiles.map(f => `file '${f}'`).join("\n"));
    const outFile = path.join(tmpDir, "combined.mp3");

    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const ff = spawn("ffmpeg", ["-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", listFile, "-c", "copy", outFile]);
      ff.on("close", code => code === 0 ? resolve() : reject(new Error(`ffmpeg concat exit ${code}`)));
    });

    return fs.readFileSync(outFile);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "gTTS: ffmpeg concat failed, returning first chunk");
    return valid[0] ?? null;
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// ── Main TTS entry point (Cloud TTS → gTTS fallback) ──────────────────────────

export async function generateTTSBuffer(text: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  // Try Google Cloud TTS first (Wavenet, highest quality)
  const cloudBuf = await googleCloudTTS(text);
  if (cloudBuf) {
    logger.info("TTS: using Google Cloud TTS (Wavenet)");
    return cloudBuf;
  }
  // Fallback: Google Translate TTS (no API key required)
  logger.info("TTS: Cloud TTS unavailable, falling back to Google Translate TTS");
  return googleTranslateTTS(text);
}

// ── Text helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function wrapLine(line: string, maxLen = 52): string[] {
  const words = line.split(/\s+/);
  const result: string[] = [];
  let cur = "";
  for (const w of words) {
    if (cur.length + w.length + 1 > maxLen) {
      if (cur) result.push(cur);
      cur = w;
    } else {
      cur = cur ? `${cur} ${w}` : w;
    }
  }
  if (cur) result.push(cur);
  return result;
}

function splitSegments(text: string, count: number): string[] {
  if (!text.trim()) return Array(count).fill("TimeNation TV");
  const sentences = text.split(/(?<=[.!?»])\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const perGroup = Math.ceil(sentences.length / count);
  const segments: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < sentences.length; i += perGroup) {
    segments.push(sentences.slice(i, i + perGroup).join(" ").trim());
  }
  // Pad if fewer segments than expected
  while (segments.length < count) segments.push(segments.at(-1) ?? "");
  return segments.slice(0, count);
}

/** Escape text for FFmpeg drawtext filter value */
function esc(s: string): string {
  return s
