    .replace(/\\/g, "\\\\")
    .replace(/'/g, "\u2019")   // curly apostrophe — avoids shell quoting issues
    .replace(/:/g, "\\:")
    .replace(/,/g, "\\,")
    .replace(/\[/g, "\\[")
    .replace(/\]/g, "\\]");
}

// ── Visual style themes (Shariah anti-Gharar: user pre-selects before payment) ─
export const VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES = {
  space:     { bg: "0x0D1B2A", title: "0x6B7E93", text: "0xC9A84C", accent: "0x4A90D9" },
  cyberpunk: { bg: "0x0A0014", title: "0xFF00FF", text: "0x00FFFF", accent: "0xFF6B00" },
  abstract:  { bg: "0x1A0A2E", title: "0x9B59B6", text: "0xE8D5B7", accent: "0xF39C12" },
  nature:    { bg: "0x0F1B0F", title: "0x4CAF50", text: "0xA8D8A8", accent: "0x8BC34A" },
  minimal:   { bg: "0x111111", title: "0x666666", text: "0xEEEEEE", accent: "0xFFFFFF" },
} as const;
export type VisualStyle = keyof typeof VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES;

// ── FFmpeg LIGHT render ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function renderLightVideo(
  audioBuffer: Buffer | null,
  manifestoText: string,
  slotId: number,
  visualStyle?: string | null,
  onProgress?: ProgressReporter
): Promise<string> {
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `tn-tv-${slotId}-`));
  const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);

  try {
    const segCount = Math.ceil(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC / SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC);
    const segments = splitSegments(manifestoText, segCount);

    // Apply visual style theme (Shariah anti-Gharar: user chose this before render)
    const style = visualStyle && visualStyle in VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES
      ? VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES[visualStyle as VisualStyle]
      : VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES.space;

    // Build drawtext filters: title always visible + rotating text segments
    const titleFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TIMENATION TV':fontsize=18:fontcolor=${style.title}:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=48:alpha=0.8`;

    const slotFilter =
      `drawtext=text='SLOT ${slotId}':fontsize=14:fontcolor=${style.accent}@0.6:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=78`;

    const segFilters: string[] = [];
    segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
      const t0 = i * SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const t1 = t0 + SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const lines = wrapLine(seg, 52).slice(0, 4);
      const totalH = lines.length * 42;
      const baseY = Math.round((720 - totalH) / 2);
      lines.forEach((line, li) => {
        segFilters.push(
          `drawtext=text='${esc(line)}':fontsize=28:fontcolor=${style.text}:` +
          `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=${baseY + li * 42}:` +
          `enable='between(t\\,${t0}\\,${t1})'`
        );
      });
    });

    // EU AI Act / China CAC: permanent AI watermark, bottom-right corner
    const watermarkFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TimeNation AI':fontsize=14:fontcolor=white@0.75:` +
      `x=w-tw-10:y=h-th-10:shadowcolor=black@0.5:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    const vf = [titleFilter, slotFilter, ...segFilters, watermarkFilter].join(",");

    // Cryptographic metadata injection (EU AI Act / CAC Article 25)
    const metadataArgs = [
      "-metadata", `title=TimeNation-TV — Slot ${slotId}`,
      "-metadata", "comment=Author: TimeNation AI, Type: Synthesized Media",
      "-metadata", "artist=TimeNation",
      "-metadata", `description=AI Generated / Сгенерировано ИИ / Generated via Artificial Intelligence technologies. Visual style: ${visualStyle ?? "space"}.`,
    ];

    await onProgress?.("encoding");
    if (audioBuffer) {
      // ── 2-pass audio/video sync (as per Synchronized AI Capsule spec) ──────
      // Pass 1: generate visual-only MP4 with exactly VIDEO_DURATION_SEC duration
      const audioPath = path.join(tmpDir, "audio.mp3");
      const visualPath = path.join(tmpDir, "visual_only.mp4");
      fs.writeFileSync(audioPath, audioBuffer);

      await runFFmpeg([
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", `color=c=${style.bg}:s=1280x720:r=25`,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "30",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        "-y", visualPath,
      ]);

      // Pass 2: merge ElevenLabs audio into visual — copy video stream (no re-encode)
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-i", visualPath,
        "-i", audioPath,
        "-c:v", "copy",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k",
        "-map", "0:v:0",
        "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-shortest",
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    } else {
      // No audio: single pass with silent track
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", `color=c=${style.bg}:s=1280x720:r=25`,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "30",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    }
    logger.info({ slotId, hasAudio: !!audioBuffer }, "LIGHT render complete");
    return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

function runFFmpeg(args: string[]): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const proc = spawn("ffmpeg", args, { stdio: ["ignore", "ignore", "pipe"] });
    let stderr = "";
    proc.stderr?.on("data", (d: Buffer) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
    proc.on("close", (code) => {
      if (code === 0) resolve();
      else reject(new Error(`FFmpeg exited ${code}: ${stderr.slice(-1000)}`));
    });
    proc.on("error", (err) => reject(err));
  });
}

// ── Progress reporter ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
type ProgressReporter = (stage: string) => Promise<void>;

async function setRenderProgress(slotId: number, stage: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await db
      .update(tvCapsulesTable)
      .set({ renderProgress: stage, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err, slotId, stage }, "Failed to update renderProgress");
  }
}

// ── AI Background Render ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Renders a video using an AI-generated background (HuggingFace or Pollinations),
// overlaying the text manifesto and TTS audio on top.
// Falls back to solid-color renderLightVideo if AI video unavailable.
async function renderWithAIBackground(
  audioBuffer: Buffer | null,
  manifestoText: string,
  slotId: number,
  visualStyle?: string | null,
  seedText?: string | null,
  citizenId?: number | null,
  onProgress?: ProgressReporter,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  tier: RenderTier = "citizen"
): Promise<string> {
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `tn-ai-${slotId}-`));
  const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);

  try {
    // Step 1: Generate cinematic prompt via Gemini
    const style = (visualStyle && visualStyle in VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES
      ? visualStyle
      : "space") as CinematicVisualStyle;

    const cinematicPrompt = await generateCinematicPromptTiered(
      seedText ?? manifestoText.slice(0, 400),
      style,
      tier
    );

    // Step 1b: Load citizen name and rank for overlay
    let citizenName = "Гражданин TimeNation";
    let citizenRank = "Гражданин";
    if (citizenId) {
      try {
        const [citizen] = await db
          .select({ name: citizensTable.name, rank: citizensTable.rank })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
          .limit(1);
        if (citizen) {
          citizenName = citizen.name ?? citizenName;
          citizenRank = citizen.rank ?? citizenRank;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn({ err, citizenId }, "Could not load citizen data for overlay");
      }
    }

    // Step 2: Fetch AI background video (HuggingFace → Pollinations → null)
    await onProgress?.("fetching_video");
    const { videoPath: aiVideoPath } = await generateAIBackground(
      cinematicPrompt,
      tmpDir,
      signal
    );

    if (!aiVideoPath) {
      // Total failure — fall back to solid-color light render
      logger.info({ slotId, style }, "AI background unavailable — falling back to solid-color render");
      return renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, visualStyle);
    }

    // Step 3: Build overlay filters (manifesto text + citizen rank/name)
    const theme = VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES[style];
    const segCount = Math.ceil(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC / SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC);
    const segments = splitSegments(manifestoText, segCount);

    const titleFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TIMENATION TV':fontsize=18:fontcolor=${theme.title}:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=48:alpha=0.8`;

    const slotFilter =
      `drawtext=text='SLOT ${slotId}':fontsize=14:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.6:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=78`;

    // Citizen rank (bottom-left, always visible)
    const rankFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenRank)}':fontsize=15:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.9:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-34:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    // Citizen name (bottom-left below rank, always visible)
    const nameFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenName)}':fontsize=17:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-12:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    const segFilters: string[] = [];
    segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
      const t0 = i * SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const t1 = t0 + SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const lines = wrapLine(seg, 52).slice(0, 4);
      const totalH = lines.length * 42;
      const baseY = Math.round((720 - totalH) / 2);
      lines.forEach((line, li) => {
        segFilters.push(
          `drawtext=text='${esc(line)}':fontsize=28:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
          `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=${baseY + li * 42}:` +
          `enable='between(t\\,${t0}\\,${t1})'`
        );
      });
    });

    const watermarkFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TimeNation AI':fontsize=14:fontcolor=white@0.75:` +
      `x=w-tw-10:y=h-th-10:shadowcolor=black@0.5:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    // Semi-transparent dark overlay to ensure text readability over any background
    const darkenFilter = `colorchannelmixer=aa=0.55`;

    const vf = [
      // Scale AI video to 1280×720, then darken slightly for readability
      `scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease`,
      `pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2`,
      `setsar=1`,
      darkenFilter,
      titleFilter,
      slotFilter,
      rankFilter,
      nameFilter,
      ...segFilters,
      watermarkFilter,
    ].join(",");

    const metadataArgs = [
      "-metadata", `title=TimeNation-TV — Slot ${slotId}`,
      "-metadata", "comment=Author: TimeNation AI, Type: Synthesized Media",
      "-metadata", "artist=TimeNation",
      "-metadata", `description=AI Generated / Сгенерировано ИИ / AI cinematic background. Visual style: ${style}. Prompt: ${cinematicPrompt.slice(0, 120)}.`,
    ];

    await onProgress?.("encoding");
    if (audioBuffer) {
      const audioPath = path.join(tmpDir, "audio.mp3");
      fs.writeFileSync(audioPath, audioBuffer);

      await runFFmpeg([
        "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", aiVideoPath,
        "-i", audioPath,
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "28",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k",
        "-map", "0:v:0",
        "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        "-shortest",
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    } else {
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", aiVideoPath,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "28",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-map", "0:v:0",
        "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    }

    logger.info({ slotId, style, cinematicPrompt: cinematicPrompt.slice(0, 80) }, "AI background render complete");
    return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err, slotId }, "renderWithAIBackground failed — falling back to solid-color");
    return renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, visualStyle, onProgress);
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// ── Prime Time Render (:00 — multi-scene 3-part montage with xfade) ─────────────
async function renderPrimeVideo(
  audioBuffer: Buffer | null,
  manifestoText: string,
  slotId: number,
  visualStyle?: string | null,
  seedText?: string | null,
  citizenId?: number | null,
  onProgress?: ProgressReporter,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `tn-prime-${slotId}-`));
  const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);

  try {
    const style = (visualStyle && visualStyle in VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES
      ? visualStyle
      : "space") as CinematicVisualStyle;
    const theme = VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES[style];

    // Load citizen identity for overlay
    let citizenName = "Гражданин TimeNation";
    let citizenRank = "Гражданин";
    if (citizenId) {
      try {
        const [citizen] = await db
          .select({ name: citizensTable.name, rank: citizensTable.rank })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
          .limit(1);
        if (citizen) {
          citizenName = citizen.name ?? citizenName;
          citizenRank = citizen.rank ?? citizenRank;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn({ err, citizenId }, "Could not load citizen data for PRIME overlay");
      }
    }

    // Step 1 — Generate 3 unique scene prompts via Gemini
    await onProgress?.("generating_scenes");
    const scenePrompts = await generateMultiScenePrompts(
      seedText ?? manifestoText.slice(0, 400),
      manifestoText,
      style
    );
    logger.info(
      { slotId, s1: scenePrompts.scene1.slice(0, 50) },
      "PRIME: 3 scene prompts generated"
    );

    // Step 2 — AI background per scene (sequential, per-scene solid-color fallback)
    await onProgress?.("fetching_scenes");
    const sceneVideoFiles: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const prompt = [scenePrompts.scene1, scenePrompts.scene2, scenePrompts.scene3][i]!;
      const sceneDir = path.join(tmpDir, `scene${i + 1}`);
      fs.mkdirSync(sceneDir, { recursive: true });

      try {
        const { videoPath } = await generateAIBackground(prompt, sceneDir, signal);
        if (!videoPath) throw new Error("generateAIBackground returned null");
        sceneVideoFiles.push(videoPath);
        logger.info({ slotId, scene: i + 1 }, `PRIME scene ${i + 1} AI background ready`);
      } catch (err) {
        // Per-scene fallback: solid-color 22s clip
        const fallbackPath = path.join(sceneDir, "fallback.mp4");
        await runFFmpeg([
          "-f", "lavfi", "-i", `color=c=${theme.bg}:s=1280x720:r=25`,
          "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
          "-t", "22", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "30",
          "-c:a", "aac", "-y", fallbackPath,
        ]);
        sceneVideoFiles.push(fallbackPath);
        logger.warn({ slotId, scene: i + 1, err }, `PRIME scene ${i + 1} AI failed — solid-color fallback used`);
      }
    }

    // Step 3 — Xfade montage: 3×22s clips → ~62s composite, trimmed to 60s
    //   offset=18: scene1 full 18s → 2s dissolve into scene2 (starts at t=18, ends t=20)
    //   offset=36: scene2 full from t=20-36 → 2s dissolve into scene3 (ends t=38)
    //   scene3 visible from t=38 to t=60
    await onProgress?.("compositing");
    const compositePath = path.join(tmpDir, "composite.mp4");

    await runFFmpeg([
      "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "22", "-i", sceneVideoFiles[0]!,
      "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "22", "-i", sceneVideoFiles[1]!,
      "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "22", "-i", sceneVideoFiles[2]!,
      "-filter_complex",
        "[0:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease," +
        "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1[sv0];" +
        "[1:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease," +
        "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1[sv1];" +
        "[2:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease," +
        "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1[sv2];" +
        "[sv0][sv1]xfade=transition=dissolve:duration=2:offset=18[v01];" +
        "[v01][sv2]xfade=transition=dissolve:duration=2:offset=36[vout]",
      "-map", "[vout]",
      "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "26",
      "-t", "60", "-y", compositePath,
    ]);
    logger.info({ slotId }, "PRIME xfade composite assembled");

    // Step 4 — Text overlay + audio over the composite
    await onProgress?.("encoding");

    // 3 text segments: each shown only during its scene's "clear" window
    const segments = splitSegments(manifestoText, 3);
    const sceneTimes = [
      { t0: 0,  t1: 18 },  // scene 1: fully visible 0-18s
      { t0: 20, t1: 38 },  // scene 2: fully visible 20-38s
      { t0: 40, t1: 60 },  // scene 3: fully visible 40-60s
    ] as const;

    const titleFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TIMENATION PRIME':fontsize=20:fontcolor=${theme.title}:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=44:alpha=0.9`;
    const slotFilter =
      `drawtext=text='SLOT ${slotId} — ПРАЙМ ТАЙМ':fontsize=14:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.85:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=72`;
    const rankFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenRank)}':fontsize=15:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.9:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-34:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;
    const nameFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenName)}':fontsize=17:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-12:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    const segFilters: string[] = [];
    segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
      const { t0, t1 } = sceneTimes[i]!;
      const lines = wrapLine(seg, 52).slice(0, 4);
      const totalH = lines.length * 42;
      const baseY = Math.round((720 - totalH) / 2);
      lines.forEach((line, li) => {
        segFilters.push(
          `drawtext=text='${esc(line)}':fontsize=28:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
          `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=${baseY + li * 42}:` +
          `enable='between(t\\,${t0}\\,${t1})'`
        );
      });
    });

    const watermarkFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TimeNation AI — PRIME':fontsize=14:fontcolor=white@0.75:` +
      `x=w-tw-10:y=h-th-10:shadowcolor=black@0.5:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;
    const darkenFilter = `colorchannelmixer=aa=0.55`;

    const vf = [darkenFilter, titleFilter, slotFilter, rankFilter, nameFilter, ...segFilters, watermarkFilter].join(",");

    const metadataArgs = [
      "-metadata", `title=TimeNation-TV — Slot ${slotId} — PRIME TIME`,
      "-metadata", "comment=Author: TimeNation AI, Type: Synthesized Media — 3-Scene Prime Montage",
      "-metadata", "artist=TimeNation",
      "-metadata", `description=AI Prime Montage / 3 scenes: ${scenePrompts.scene1.slice(0, 40)} | ${scenePrompts.scene2.slice(0, 40)} | ${scenePrompts.scene3.slice(0, 40)}.`,
    ];

    if (audioBuffer) {
      const audioPath = path.join(tmpDir, "audio.mp3");
      fs.writeFileSync(audioPath, audioBuffer);
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-i", compositePath,
        "-i", audioPath,
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "fast", "-crf", "24",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k",
        "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC), "-shortest",
        ...metadataArgs, "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    } else {
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-i", compositePath,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "fast", "-crf", "24",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        ...metadataArgs, "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    }

    logger.info(
      { slotId, style, s1: scenePrompts.scene1.slice(0, 40), s2: scenePrompts.scene2.slice(0, 40), s3: scenePrompts.scene3.slice(0, 40) },
      "PRIME TIME 3-scene montage render complete"
    );
    return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;

  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err, slotId }, "renderPrimeVideo failed — falling back to apex AI background");
    return renderWithAIBackground(
      audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, visualStyle, seedText, citizenId, onProgress, signal, "apex"
    );
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// ── Main Orchestrator ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function renderCapsule(slotId: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<{
  manifestoText: string;
  mediaUrl: string | null;
  catcastUrl: string | null;
  status: string;
}> {
  const [capsule] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) throw new Error(`No capsule found for slotId=${slotId}`);

  // Mark as processing
  const reportProgress: ProgressReporter = (stage) => setRenderProgress(slotId, stage);

  await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({ status: "pending_render", renderProgress: "generating_prompt", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));

  // 1. Load slot for date/minuteIndex (needed for Gemini prompt context)
  const [slot] = await db
    .select({ date: tvSlotsTable.date, minuteIndex: tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  // 2. Determine manifesto text
  let manifestoText: string;
  if (capsule.contentState === "empty") {
    manifestoText = "TimeNation — пространство тишины. Момент принадлежит тебе.";
  } else if (capsule.mode === "b") {
    // Mode B: raw seed, no LLM expansion
    manifestoText = capsule.seedText ?? "";
  } else {
    // Mode A: Gemini AI packaging → fallback to template if key missing or error
    if (capsule.seedText && slot) {
      const geminiResult = await generateManifesto(
        capsule.seedText,
        slot.date,
        slot.minuteIndex
      );
      manifestoText = geminiResult ?? expandSeedToManifesto(capsule.seedText);
      if (!geminiResult) {
        logger.info({ slotId }, "Gemini unavailable — used template manifesto fallback");
      }
    } else {
      manifestoText = capsule.seedText
        ? expandSeedToManifesto(capsule.seedText)
        : "TimeNation — суверенное государство времени.";
    }
  }

  // 2. Three-tier render routing by slot type ──────────────────────────────
  //    apex (:00)    → renderPrimeVideo   (3-scene Gemini + FFmpeg xfade montage)
  //    half (:30)    → renderWithAIBackground tier=half  (premium quality prompt)
  //    quarter (:15/:45) → renderWithAIBackground tier=quarter (high quality prompt)
  //    citizen       → renderWithAIBackground tier=citizen (standard quality)
  const slotType = slot ? getSlotType(slot.minuteIndex) : "citizen";
  const tier: RenderTier = slotType;

  let mediaUrl: string | null = null;
  let finalStatus = "draft";

  // Generate TTS audio once (shared by all paths)
  await reportProgress("generating_tts");
  const audioBuffer = manifestoText ? await generateTTSBuffer(manifestoText) : null;

  if (slotType === "apex") {
    // PRIME TIME (:00) — 3-scene multi-montage (Gemini splits text → 3 AI frames → xfade)
    try {
      mediaUrl = await renderPrimeVideo(
        audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId,
        capsule.visualStyle, capsule.seedText, capsule.citizenId,
        reportProgress, signal
      );
      finalStatus = "rendered";
      logger.info({ slotId }, "PRIME TIME render complete");
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error({ err, slotId }, "renderPrimeVideo failed — falling back to apex AI background");
      try {
        mediaUrl = await renderWithAIBackground(
          audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId,
          capsule.visualStyle, capsule.seedText, capsule.citizenId,
          reportProgress, signal, "apex"
        );
        finalStatus = "rendered";
      } catch (bgErr) {
        logger.error({ bgErr, slotId }, "APEX AI background also failed — solid-color fallback");
        try {
          mediaUrl = await renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, capsule.visualStyle, reportProgress);
          finalStatus = "rendered";
        } catch (lightErr) {
          logger.error({ lightErr, slotId }, "renderLightVideo fallback also failed");
          finalStatus = "draft";
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    // HALF / QUARTER / CITIZEN — AI background with tier-specific prompt quality
    try {
      mediaUrl = await renderWithAIBackground(
        audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId,
        capsule.visualStyle, capsule.seedText, capsule.citizenId,
        reportProgress, signal, tier
      );
      finalStatus = "rendered";
      logger.info({ slotId, tier }, `${tier.toUpperCase()} slot rendered with AI background`);
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error({ err, slotId, tier }, `${tier.toUpperCase()} renderWithAIBackground failed — solid-color fallback`);
      try {
        mediaUrl = await renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, capsule.visualStyle, reportProgress);
        finalStatus = "rendered";
      } catch (lightErr) {
        logger.error({ lightErr, slotId }, "renderLightVideo also failed");
        finalStatus = "draft";
      }
    }
  }

  // 4. Build storage record and persist
  const localPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);
  let sizeBytes: number | undefined;
  try {
    sizeBytes = fs.existsSync(localPath) ? fs.statSync(localPath).size : undefined;
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
  const storageRecord = buildStorageRecord(slotId, localPath, sizeBytes);
  // Use hot CDN URL as the canonical mediaUrl if available
  const canonicalMediaUrl = storageRecord.hotUrl ?? mediaUrl;

  await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({
      manifestoText: manifestoText || null,
      mediaUrl: canonicalMediaUrl,
      status: finalStatus,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));

  // 5. Upload to Catcast.tv (async, non-blocking for caller)
  let catcastUrl: string | null = null;
  if (finalStatus === "rendered" && mediaUrl) {
    const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);
    const [citizen] = await db
      .select({ name: citizensTable.name })
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, capsule.citizenId))
      .limit(1);
    const citizenName = citizen?.name ?? "Гражданин TimeNation";

    const catcastResult = await uploadToCatcast(
      outputPath,
      slotId,
      citizenName,
      capsule.seedText ?? ""
    ).catch((err) => {
      logger.warn({ err, slotId }, "Catcast upload threw, skipping");
      return null;
    });

    if (catcastResult) {
      catcastUrl = catcastResult.videoUrl;
      await db
        .update(tvCapsulesTable)
        .set({
          catcastVideoId: catcastResult.videoId,
          catcastUrl: catcastResult.videoUrl,
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        })
        .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
      logger.info({ slotId, catcastUrl }, "Capsule published to Catcast");
    }
  }

  return { manifestoText, mediaUrl, catcastUrl, status: finalStatus };
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/telegramService.ts ---
import { Telegraf } from "telegraf";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable, transactionsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, and, ilike } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

let bot: Telegraf | null = null;

export function getBot(): Telegraf | null {
  return bot;
}

// ─── Legacy import data ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// amount кодирует: minutes * 100 + seconds (например, 261 = 2 мин 61 сек).
// fromName / toName сопоставляются с citizens.name в БД при импорте.
// sessionId = 0 — sentinel-маркер «импортированная запись».
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
interface LegacyTransaction {
  fromName: string;
  toName: string;
  amount: number; // minutes * 100 + seconds
  createdAt: string; // ISO 8601
}

const LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS: LegacyTransaction[] = [
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Olga Fedorova", amount: 261,  createdAt: "2026-05-27T07:31:00Z" },
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Olga Fedorova", amount: 299,  createdAt: "2026-05-27T09:05:00Z" },
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Olga Fedorova", amount: 262,  createdAt: "2026-05-27T09:42:00Z" },
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Gleb Lerner",   amount: 1160, createdAt: "2026-05-29T21:51:00Z" },
];

// ─── Admin guard ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Установи ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID в Replit Secrets — числовой ID твоего аккаунта.
// Узнать свой ID можно у @userinfobot в Telegram.
const ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID = process.env.ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID ?? "";

function isAdmin(telegramId: string): boolean {
  return !!ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID && telegramId === ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function initBot(token: string): Telegraf {
  bot = new Telegraf(token);

  bot.start(async (ctx) => {
    const telegramId = String(ctx.from.id);
    const firstName = ctx.from.first_name ?? "Гражданин";

    const [citizen] = await db
      .select()
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.telegramId, telegramId))
      .limit(1);

    if (citizen) {
      await ctx.reply(
        `🏛 Добро пожаловать в TimeNation, ${citizen.name}!\n\n` +
        `💰 Ваш баланс: *${citizen.balanceMinutes} минут*\n` +
        `📜 Ранг: *${citizen.rank}*\n\n` +
        `Откройте приложение TimeNation, чтобы начать TiMETR-сессию.`,
        { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
      );
    } else {
      await ctx.reply(
        `🏛 Добро пожаловать в TimeNation, ${firstName}!\n\n` +
        `Вы ещё не привязали Telegram к своему аккаунту.\n\n` +
        `Чтобы привязать аккаунт, отправьте команду:\n` +
        `*/link <ваш_номер_телефона>*\n\n` +
        `Например: \`/link +79991234567\``,
        { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
      );
    }
  });

  bot.command("link", async (ctx) => {
    const telegramId = String(ctx.from.id);
    const args = ctx.message.text.split(" ").slice(1);
    const phone = args[0]?.trim();

    if (!phone) {
      await ctx.reply(
        "Укажите номер телефона: */link +79991234567*",
        { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
      );
      return;
    }

    const [citizen] = await db
      .select()
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.phone, phone))
      .limit(1);

    if (!citizen) {
      await ctx.reply("❌ Гражданин с таким номером не найден. Сначала зарегистрируйтесь в приложении TimeNation.");
      return;
    }

    await db
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ telegramId })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.phone, phone));

    await ctx.reply(
      `✅ Аккаунт привязан!\n\n` +
      `👤 Гражданин: *${citizen.name}*\n` +
      `💰 Баланс: *${citizen.balanceMinutes} минут*\n` +
      `📜 Ранг: *${citizen.rank}*\n\n` +
      `Теперь вы будете получать уведомления о каждой транзакции в Казначействе TimeNation.`,
      { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
    );
  });

  bot.command("balance", async (ctx) => {
    const telegramId = String(ctx.from.id);

    const [citizen] = await db
      .select()
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.telegramId, telegramId))
      .limit(1);

    if (!citizen) {
      await ctx.reply("❌ Аккаунт не привязан. Используйте /link <телефон>");
      return;
    }

    await ctx.reply(
      `💰 *${citizen.name}*\n\n` +
      `Баланс: *${citizen.balanceMinutes} мин*\n` +
      `📜 Ранг: *${citizen.rank}* (Merit: ${citizen.meritScore.toFixed(2)})\n` +
      `Передано: ${citizen.totalGiven} мин\n` +
      `Получено: ${citizen.totalReceived} мин`,
      { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
    );
  });

  // ─── /migratedata — только для администратора ───────────────────────────
  bot.command("migratedata", async (ctx) => {
    const senderId = String(ctx.from.id);

    // Полностью игнорировать для всех не-администраторов
    if (!isAdmin(senderId)) return;

    if (LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS.length === 0) {
      await ctx.reply(
        "⚠️ Массив LEGACY\\_TRANSACTIONS пуст\\.\n\nДобавь исторические записи в telegramService\\.ts перед запуском импорта\\.",
        { parse_mode: "MarkdownV2" }
      );
      return;
    }

    await ctx.reply(
      `⏳ Начинаю импорт *${LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS.length}* исторических транзакций\\.\\.\\.`,
      { parse_mode: "MarkdownV2" }
    );

    let imported = 0;
    let skipped = 0;
    let notFound = 0;
    let errors = 0;

    for (const tx of LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS) {
      try {
        // Декодируем amount: minutes * 100 + seconds
        const minutesTransferred = Math.floor(tx.amount / 100);
        const durationSeconds    = tx.amount % 100;

        // Ищем граждан по имени (без учёта регистра)
        const [fromCitizen] = await db
          .select({ id: citizensTable.id, name: citizensTable.name })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(ilike(citizensTable.name, tx.fromName))
          .limit(1);

        const [toCitizen] = await db
          .select({ id: citizensTable.id, name: citizensTable.name })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(ilike(citizensTable.name, tx.toName))
          .limit(1);

        if (!fromCitizen || !toCitizen) {
          notFound++;
          logger.warn(
            { fromName: tx.fromName, toName: tx.toName, fromFound: !!fromCitizen, toFound: !!toCitizen },
            "migratedata: citizen not found by name, skipping"
          );
          continue;
        }

        // Дедупликация по (sessionId=0, fromCitizenId, toCitizenId, minutesTransferred)
        const [existing] = await db
          .select({ id: transactionsTable.id })
          .from(transactionsTable)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(transactionsTable.sessionId, 0),
              eq(transactionsTable.fromCitizenId, fromCitizen.id),
              eq(transactionsTable.toCitizenId, toCitizen.id),
              eq(transactionsTable.minutesTransferred, minutesTransferred),
            )
          )
          .limit(1);

        if (existing) {
          skipped++;
          continue;
        }

        await db.insert(transactionsTable).values({
          sessionId: 0,
          fromCitizenId: fromCitizen.id,
          toCitizenId: toCitizen.id,
          fromCitizenName: fromCitizen.name,
          toCitizenName: toCitizen.name,
          minutesTransferred,
          durationSeconds,
          createdAt: new Date(tx.createdAt),
        });

        imported++;
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error({ err, tx }, "migratedata: failed to insert legacy transaction");
        errors++;
      }
    }

    const report =
      `✅ *Импорт завершён*\n\n` +
      `📥 Успешно импортировано: *${imported}* транзакций\n` +
      `⏭ Пропущено (дубли): *${skipped}*\n` +
      `👤 Гражданин не найден: *${notFound}*\n` +
      `❌ Ошибок при записи: *${errors}*`;

    logger.info({ imported, skipped, notFound, errors }, "migratedata: import completed");
    await ctx.reply(report, { parse_mode: "Markdown" });
  });
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  bot.launch().catch((err) => {
    logger.error({ err }, "Telegram bot launch failed");
  });

  process.once("SIGINT", () => bot?.stop("SIGINT"));
  process.once("SIGTERM", () => bot?.stop("SIGTERM"));

  logger.info("Telegram bot started");
  return bot;
}

export async function sendTransactionNotification(
  citizenId: number,
  message: string
): Promise<void> {
  if (!bot) return;

  try {
    const [citizen] = await db
      .select({ telegramId: citizensTable.telegramId })
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
      .limit(1);

    if (!citizen?.telegramId) return;

    await bot.telegram.sendMessage(citizen.telegramId, message, {
      parse_mode: "Markdown",
    });
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const code = (err as { code?: number })?.code;
    if (code === 403) {
      logger.warn({ citizenId }, "Citizen blocked the Telegram bot");
    } else {
      logger.error({ err, citizenId }, "Failed to send Telegram notification");
    }
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/timeBankService.ts ---
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { timeBankLoansTable, timeBankLedgerTable, citizensTable, transactionsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, sum, count, and, asc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

export const MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES = 480; // 8 часов — абсолютный потолок

/**
 * Лестница доверия:
 * 0 услуг → 60 мин
 * 1+ услуга, <1 ч total → 120 мин
 * 2 ч total → 180 мин, 3 ч → 240 мин … 7 ч → 480 мин (cap)
 */
export function calculateMaxCreditLimit(
  completedServicesCount: number,
  totalMinutesWorked: number
): number {
  if (completedServicesCount === 0) return 60;
  const hoursWorked = Math.floor(totalMinutesWorked / 60);
  const step = Math.max(2, hoursWorked + 1); // минимум шаг 2 (120 мин) при 1+ услуге
  return Math.min(step * 60, MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES);
}

/** Уровень, необходимый для следующего шага */
export function nextLevelRequirement(
  completedServicesCount: number,
  totalMinutesWorked: number
): { label: string; currentMinutes: number; targetMinutes: number } | null {
  const currentLimit = calculateMaxCreditLimit(completedServicesCount, totalMinutesWorked);
  if (currentLimit >= MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES) return null; // уже потолок
  if (completedServicesCount === 0) {
    return { label: "Выполните первую услугу", currentMinutes: 0, targetMinutes: 0 };
  }
  const hoursWorked = Math.floor(totalMinutesWorked / 60);
  const nextHours = Math.max(2, hoursWorked + 1);
  return {
    label: `Отработайте ${nextHours} ч для лимита ${(nextHours + 1) * 60} мин`,
    currentMinutes: totalMinutesWorked,
    targetMinutes: nextHours * 60,
  };
}

/** Суммарный активный долг гражданина (Д) */
export async function getActiveDebt(citizenId: number): Promise<number> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ total: sum(timeBankLoansTable.remainingDebt) })
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId),
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")
      )
    );
  return Number(row?.total ?? 0);
}

/** Статус Банка Времени для гражданина */
export async function getTimeBankStatus(citizenId: number) {
  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const [svcRow] = await db
    .select({ cnt: count() })
    .from(transactionsTable)
    .where(eq(transactionsTable.toCitizenId, citizenId));

  const completedServicesCount = Number(svcRow?.cnt ?? 0);
  const totalMinutesWorked = citizen.totalReceived;

  const creditLimit = calculateMaxCreditLimit(completedServicesCount, totalMinutesWorked);
  const activeDebt = await getActiveDebt(citizenId);
  const availableCredit = Math.max(0, creditLimit - activeDebt);

  const nextLevel = nextLevelRequirement(completedServicesCount, totalMinutesWorked);
  const atCap = creditLimit >= MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES;

  const loans = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId));

  const ledger = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLedgerTable)
    .where(eq(timeBankLedgerTable.citizenId, citizenId))
    .orderBy(asc(timeBankLedgerTable.createdAt));

  return {
    citizenId,
    creditLimit,
    activeDebt,
    availableCredit,
    maxDebtCap: MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES,
    atCap,
    completedServicesCount,
    totalMinutesWorked,
    nextLevel,
    statusLabel: atCap
      ? "Достигнут максимальный предел доверия Банка Времени (8 часов)"
      : `Лимит доверия: ${creditLimit} мин`,
    loans: loans.map((l) => ({
      ...l,
      createdAt: l.createdAt.toISOString(),
      repaidAt: l.repaidAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
    })),
    ledger: ledger.map((e) => ({
      ...e,
      createdAt: e.createdAt.toISOString(),
    })),
  };
}

/** Взять кредит в Банке Времени */
export async function borrowFromBank(citizenId: number, amount: number): Promise<void> {
  if (amount <= 0) throw new Error("Сумма кредита должна быть положительной");

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const [svcRow] = await db
    .select({ cnt: count() })
    .from(transactionsTable)
    .where(eq(transactionsTable.toCitizenId, citizenId));

  const completedServicesCount = Number(svcRow?.cnt ?? 0);
  const creditLimit = calculateMaxCreditLimit(completedServicesCount, citizen.totalReceived);
  const activeDebt = await getActiveDebt(citizenId);

  if (activeDebt + amount > creditLimit) {
    const available = Math.max(0, creditLimit - activeDebt);
    if (available === 0 && creditLimit >= MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES) {
      throw new Error(
        "Достигнут максимальный предел доверия Банка Времени (8 часов). Погасите долг для дальнейшего кредитования."
      );
    }
    throw new Error(
      `Превышен кредитный лимит. Доступно: ${available} мин (лимит ${creditLimit} мин). ` +
      `Выполните больше услуг для повышения лимита.`
    );
  }

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const [loan] = await tx.insert(timeBankLoansTable).values({
      citizenId,
      amount,
      remainingDebt: amount,
      status: "active",
    }).returning();

    await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
      citizenId,
      loanId: loan!.id,
      type: "loan_issued",
      amount,
      note: `Выдан кредит ${amount} Д`,
    });

    await tx
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes + amount })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  });

  logger.info({ citizenId, amount, creditLimit }, "Банк Времени: выдан кредит");
}

/**
 * Ручное погашение долга (FIFO по активным займам).
 * Списывает с баланса, создаёт ledger-записи, освобождает залог при обнулении долга.
 */
export async function repayFromBalance(citizenId: number, amount: number): Promise<void> {
  if (amount <= 0) throw new Error("Сумма погашения должна быть положительной");

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const transferable = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1; // родовая минута неприкосновенна
  if (transferable < amount) {
    throw new Error(`Недостаточно минут на балансе. Доступно для погашения: ${transferable} Д`);
  }

  const activeLoans = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(and(eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId), eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")))
    .orderBy(asc(timeBankLoansTable.createdAt));

  if (activeLoans.length === 0) throw new Error("Нет активных займов для погашения");

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    let remaining = amount;

    for (const loan of activeLoans) {
      if (remaining <= 0) break;
      const toDeduct = Math.min(remaining, loan.remainingDebt);
      const newRemaining = loan.remainingDebt - toDeduct;
      const fullyRepaid = newRemaining === 0;

      await tx
        .update(timeBankLoansTable)
        .set({
          remainingDebt: newRemaining,
          status: fullyRepaid ? "repaid" : "active",
          repaidAt: fullyRepaid ? new Date() : undefined,
        })
        .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loan.id));

      await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
        citizenId,
        loanId: loan.id,
        type: "manual_repayment",
        amount: toDeduct,
        note: `Ручное погашение ${toDeduct} Д`,
      });

      if (fullyRepaid) {
        await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
          citizenId,
          loanId: loan.id,
          type: "collateral_released",
          amount: loan.amount,
          note: `Залог освобождён: ${loan.amount} Д`,
        });
      }

      remaining -= toDeduct;
    }

    // Списываем только фактически погашенное (amount - remaining),
    // а не весь запрошенный amount — защита от переплаты если долг меньше суммы.
    const actualRepaid = amount - remaining;
    await tx
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - actualRepaid })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  });

  logger.info({ citizenId, amount }, "Банк Времени: ручное погашение");
}

/**
 * Автоматическое погашение долга из заработанных минут (вызывается при завершении TiMETR-сессии).
 * НЕ обновляет citizens.balance_minutes — это делает вызывающий код.
 * Возвращает сумму, направленную на погашение долга (вычитается из зачисляемых минут).
 */
export async function autoRepayFromService(
  citizenId: number,
  earnedMinutes: number
): Promise<number> {
  if (earnedMinutes <= 0) return 0;

  const activeLoans = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(and(eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId), eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")))
    .orderBy(asc(timeBankLoansTable.createdAt));

  if (activeLoans.length === 0) return 0;

  let totalAutoRepaid = 0;
  let remaining = earnedMinutes;

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    for (const loan of activeLoans) {
      if (remaining <= 0) break;
      const toDeduct = Math.min(remaining, loan.remainingDebt);
      const newRemaining = loan.remainingDebt - toDeduct;
      const fullyRepaid = newRemaining === 0;

      await tx
        .update(timeBankLoansTable)
        .set({
          remainingDebt: newRemaining,
          status: fullyRepaid ? "repaid" : "active",
          repaidAt: fullyRepaid ? new Date() : undefined,
        })
        .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loan.id));

      await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
        citizenId,
        loanId: loan.id,
        type: "auto_repayment",
        amount: toDeduct,
        note: `Автопогашение из TiMETR: ${toDeduct} Д`,
      });

      if (fullyRepaid) {
        await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
          citizenId,
          loanId: loan.id,
          type: "collateral_released",
          amount: loan.amount,
          note: `Залог освобождён: ${loan.amount} Д`,
        });
      }

      totalAutoRepaid += toDeduct;
      remaining -= toDeduct;
    }
  });

  if (totalAutoRepaid > 0) {
    logger.info({ citizenId, earnedMinutes, totalAutoRepaid }, "Банк Времени: автопогашение из TiMETR");
  }

  return totalAutoRepaid;
}

/** @deprecated Используй repayFromBalance (FIFO, без loanId) */
export async function repayLoan(citizenId: number, loanId: number, amount: number): Promise<void> {
  const [loan] = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loanId),
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId),
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")
      )
    )
    .limit(1);

  if (!loan) throw new Error("Кредит не найден или уже погашен");

  const repayAmount = Math.min(amount, loan.remainingDebt);

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const transferable = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1;
  if (transferable < repayAmount) {
    throw new Error(`Недостаточно Дарминов для погашения. Доступно: ${transferable} Д`);
  }

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const newRemaining = loan.remainingDebt - repayAmount;
    const fullyRepaid = newRemaining === 0;

    await tx
      .update(timeBankLoansTable)
      .set({
        remainingDebt: newRemaining,
        status: fullyRepaid ? "repaid" : "active",
        repaidAt: fullyRepaid ? new Date() : undefined,
      })
      .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loanId));

    await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
      citizenId,
      loanId,
      type: "manual_repayment",
      amount: repayAmount,
      note: `Ручное погашение ${repayAmount} Д`,
    });

    if (fullyRepaid) {
      await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
        citizenId,
        loanId,
        type: "collateral_released",
        amount: loan.amount,
        note: `Залог освобождён: ${loan.amount} Д`,
      });
    }

    await tx
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - repayAmount })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  });

  logger.info({ citizenId, loanId, repayAmount }, "Банк Времени: погашение долга");
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/tvService.ts ---
import crypto from "crypto";
import type { Request } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tvSlotsTable, tvCapsulesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "./telegramService";

// ── Slot type by minute position ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// apex    : minuteIndex % 60 === 0           (:00)  → 24/day
// half    : minuteIndex % 30 === 0, ≠ :00    (:30)  → 24/day
// quarter : minuteIndex % 15 === 0, ≠ :00/:30 (:15/:45) → 48/day
// citizen : all others                                 → 1344/day
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export type SlotType = "citizen" | "quarter" | "half" | "apex";

export function getSlotType(minuteIndex: number): SlotType {
  const m = minuteIndex % 60;
  if (m === 0) return "apex";
  if (m === 30) return "half";
  if (m % 15 === 0) return "quarter";
  return "citizen";
}

// ── Burn Matrix ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const BURN_MATRIX: Record<
  SlotType,
  { eur: number; priceEurCents: number; timeltr: number; label: string }
> = {
  citizen: { eur: 4.99,  priceEurCents: 499,  timeltr: 1,  label: "Citizen Minute" },
  quarter: { eur: 9.99,  priceEurCents: 999,  timeltr: 10, label: "Quarter Minute (:15/:45)" },
  half:    { eur: 24.99, priceEurCents: 2499, timeltr: 25, label: "Half Minute (:30)" },
  apex:    { eur: 49.99, priceEurCents: 4999, timeltr: 50, label: "Apex Minute (:00)" },
};

// ── Fingerprint for anti-consecutive guard ────────────────────────────────────
export function getFingerprint(req: Request): string {
  const raw = `${req.ip ?? "unknown"}:${req.headers["user-agent"] ?? ""}`;
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(raw).digest("hex");
}

// ── Sovereign URL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function generateSovereignUrl(date: string, minuteIndex: number): string {
  const hour = String(Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const min = String(minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  return `timenation.tv/moment/${date}-${hour}${min}`;
}

// ── Retro detection (slot_time < NOW) ─────────────────────────────────────────
export function isRetroSlot(date: string, minuteIndex: number): boolean {
  const hour = Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60);
  const min = minuteIndex % 60;
  const slotTime = new Date(
    `${date}T${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(min).padStart(2, "0")}:00Z`
  );
  return slotTime < new Date();
}

// ── Date validation helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function isValidDate(date: string): boolean {
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) && !isNaN(Date.parse(date));
}

// ── Generate all 1440 slots for a given date (idempotent) ────────────────────
export async function ensureSlotsForDate(date: string): Promise<void> {
  const [existing] = await db
    .select({ id: tvSlotsTable.id })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.date, date))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing) return; // Already generated

  const rows = Array.from({ length: 1440 }, (_, i) => {
    const slotType = getSlotType(i);
    const pricing = BURN_MATRIX[slotType];
    return {
      date,
      minuteIndex: i,
      slotType,
      status: "free" as const,
      priceEur: pricing.eur,
      timeltrBurnCost: pricing.timeltr,
    };
  });

  // Insert in chunks of 200 to avoid large parameter arrays
  for (let offset = 0; offset < rows.length; offset += 200) {
    await db
      .insert(tvSlotsTable)
      .values(rows.slice(offset, offset + 200))
      .onConflictDoNothing();
  }

  logger.info({ date, count: 1440 }, "TV slots generated for date");
}

// ── Air Due Slots (runs every minute) ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Finds booked slots whose date+minuteIndex matches the current UTC minute,
// marks them "broadcast", marks their capsule "broadcast", notifies owner.
export async function airDueSlots(): Promise<number> {
  const now = new Date();
  const todayUTC = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const minuteIndex = now.getUTCHours() * 60 + now.getUTCMinutes();

  const dueSlots = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(tvSlotsTable.date, todayUTC),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex, minuteIndex),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.status, "booked"),
      )
    );

  if (dueSlots.length === 0) return 0;

  for (const slot of dueSlots) {
    await db
      .update(tvSlotsTable)
      .set({ status: "broadcast" })
      .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slot.id));

    await db
      .update(tvCapsulesTable)
      .set({ status: "broadcast" })
      .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slot.id))
      .catch(() => {});

    if (slot.ownerId) {
      const hh = String(Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
      const mm = String(minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");
      sendTransactionNotification(
        slot.ownerId,
        `📡 *Ваш слот в эфире!*\n\n🗓 ${todayUTC} · ${hh}:${mm} UTC\nТип: ${slot.slotType}\n\nВаша капсула вышла в эфир TimeNation-TV.`,
      ).catch(() => {});
    }
  }

  logger.info({ todayUTC, minuteIndex, count: dueSlots.length }, "Slots aired");
  return dueSlots.length;
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/videoStorage.ts ---
/**
 * Трёхконтурная система хранения видео TimeNation-TV
 *
 * Контур «Горячий»  — CDN ссылка (AWS S3 / DigitalOcean Spaces + Cloudflare)
 * Контур «Холодный» — архивная ссылка (Google Cloud Storage / Backblaze B2)
 * Контур «Метаданные» — PostgreSQL (только ссылки, не бинарные данные)
 *
 * В текущей реализации Replit хранит файлы локально (/media/slot_N.mp4).
 * При подключении реального CDN: заменить `buildHotUrl` на реальный S3/DO presigned URL,
 * а `buildColdUrl` — на GCS/B2 ссылку. БД продолжает хранить только URL.
 */

import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

export interface VideoStorageRecord {
  slotId: number;
  hotUrl: string | null;       // CDN / streaming URL (HLS / MP4)
  coldUrl: string | null;      // архивная ссылка (долгосрочное хранение)
  localPath: string | null;    // путь на диске Replit (dev/fallback)
  storageBackend: "local" | "s3" | "do_spaces" | "gcs" | "b2";
  sizeBytes: number | null;
  uploadedAt: Date | null;
}

// ── Env-based config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const S3_BUCKET    = process.env["S3_BUCKET"];
const S3_CDN_URL   = process.env["S3_CDN_URL"];     // e.g. https://cdn.timenation.tv
const GCS_BUCKET   = process.env["GCS_BUCKET"];
const GCS_BASE_URL = process.env["GCS_BASE_URL"];   // e.g. https://storage.googleapis.com/bucket

// ── Backend detection ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function getStorageBackend(): "s3" | "do_spaces" | "gcs" | "b2" | "local" {
  if (S3_BUCKET && S3_CDN_URL) return "s3";
  if (GCS_BUCKET && GCS_BASE_URL) return "gcs";
  return "local";
}

// ── Hot URL builder (streaming / CDN) ────────────────────────────────────────
export function buildHotUrl(slotId: number): string | null {
  const backend = getStorageBackend();

  if (backend === "s3" || backend === "do_spaces") {
    // AWS S3 or DigitalOcean Spaces behind Cloudflare CDN
    return `${S3_CDN_URL}/tv/slots/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  }

  if (backend === "gcs") {
    return `${GCS_BASE_URL}/tv/slots/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  }

  // Local fallback (Replit dev): serve through Express route
  return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
}

// ── Cold URL builder (archive) ────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function buildColdUrl(slotId: number): string | null {
  const backend = getStorageBackend();

  if (backend === "gcs") {
    return `${GCS_BASE_URL}/tv/archive/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  }

  if (backend === "s3" || backend === "do_spaces") {
    // Archive bucket (cheaper storage class / B2 compatible)
    return `${S3_CDN_URL}/tv/archive/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  }

  // No separate cold tier in local mode
  return null;
}

// ── Build full storage record for a rendered slot ─────────────────────────────
export function buildStorageRecord(
  slotId: number,
  localPath: string,
  sizeBytes?: number
): VideoStorageRecord {
  const backend = getStorageBackend();

  const record: VideoStorageRecord = {
    slotId,
    hotUrl: buildHotUrl(slotId),
    coldUrl: buildColdUrl(slotId),
    localPath,
    storageBackend: backend,
    sizeBytes: sizeBytes ?? null,
    uploadedAt: new Date(),
  };

  logger.info(
    { slotId, backend, hotUrl: record.hotUrl, coldUrl: record.coldUrl },
    "VideoStorage record built"
  );

  return record;
}

// ── Treasury tax calculator ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Казначейский налог: 2% на все Дармин-транзакции. Точная дробь, без округления.
// Применяется к: TiMETR-сессиям, TV-бронированию (timeltr), аукционам.
// Fiat/crypto платежи: налог включён в EUR-цену (BURN_MATRIX).
export const TREASURY_TAX_RATE = 0.02;

export function calcTreasuryTax(timeltrBurnCost: number): number {
  return timeltrBurnCost * TREASURY_TAX_RATE;
}

// ID казначейского аккаунта (гражданин с id=1 — системный кошелёк)
export const TREASURY_CITIZEN_ID = 1;

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/app/(tabs)/embassies.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useMemo, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, FlatList,
  ActivityIndicator, Alert, TextInput, Platform, Modal, ScrollView, Image,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import * as Location from "expo-location";
import { useCitizen } from "@/context/CitizenContext";
import { useColors } from "@/hooks/useColors";
import { LogoBrand } from "@/components/LogoBrand";

const BASE_URL = `https://${process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN}`;

// ── Inline OLC (Open Location Code) engine ───────────────────────────────────

const OLC_ALPHA = '23456789CFGHJMPQRVWX';
const OLC_STEPS: number[] = [20.0, 1.0, 0.05, 0.0025, 0.000125];

function olcIsValid(code: string): boolean {
  if (!code || code.length < 4) return false;
  const c = code.toUpperCase();
  const si = c.indexOf('+');
  if (si < 2 || si > 8 || si % 2 !== 0) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i++) {
    if (c[i] !== '+' && OLC_ALPHA.indexOf(c[i]) === -1) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function olcIsFull(code: string): boolean { return code.toUpperCase().indexOf('+') === 8; }
function olcDecode(code: string): { lat: number; lng: number } {
  const clean = code.toUpperCase().replace('+', '').replace(/0+$/, '');
  let lat = -90.0, lng = -180.0, lastStep = OLC_STEPS[0];
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(clean.length, 10); i += 2) {
    const step = OLC_STEPS[i / 2]; lastStep = step;
    lat += OLC_ALPHA.indexOf(clean[i]) * step;
    lng += OLC_ALPHA.indexOf(clean[i + 1]) * step;
  }
  return { lat: lat + lastStep / 2, lng: lng + lastStep / 2 };
}
function olcEncodePrefix(latN: number, lngN: number, numPairs: number): string {
  let la = latN, ln = lngN, prefix = '';
  for (let p = 0; p < numPairs; p++) {
    const step = OLC_STEPS[p];
    const li = Math.floor(la / step); const ni = Math.floor(ln / step);
    prefix += OLC_ALPHA[li] + OLC_ALPHA[ni]; la -= li * step; ln -= ni * step;
  }
  return prefix;
}
function olcRecover(shortCode: string, refLat: number, refLng: number): string | null {
  const sc = shortCode.toUpperCase(); const si = sc.indexOf('+');
  if (si < 2 || si > 7 || si % 2 !== 0) return null;
  const prefix = olcEncodePrefix(refLat + 90, ((refLng + 180) % 360), (8 - si) / 2);
  const fullDigits = prefix + sc.replace('+', '');
  return fullDigits.slice(0, 8) + '+' + fullDigits.slice(8);
}
type LatLng = [number, number];
const CITY_REFS: Record<string, LatLng> = {
  фамагуста:[35.1211,33.9363], famagusta:[35.1211,33.9363],
  никосия:[35.1856,33.3823], nicosia:[35.1856,33.3823],
  лимасол:[34.6786,33.0413], limassol:[34.6786,33.0413],
  москва:[55.7558,37.6176], moscow:[55.7558,37.6176],
  лондон:[51.5074,-0.1278], london:[51.5074,-0.1278],
  дубай:[25.2048,55.2708], dubai:[25.2048,55.2708],
};
const DEFAULT_REF: LatLng = [35.1211, 33.9363];
interface Coords { lat: number; lng: number }
function resolveLocation(input: string): Coords | null {
  const str = input.trim(); if (!str) return null;
  if (str.includes('+')) {
    const parts = str.split(/\s+/); const codeRaw = parts[0].toUpperCase();
    if (!olcIsValid(codeRaw)) return null;
    if (olcIsFull(codeRaw)) return olcDecode(codeRaw);
    const cityStr = parts.slice(1).join(' ').toLowerCase();
    let ref: LatLng = DEFAULT_REF;
    for (const [city, coords] of Object.entries(CITY_REFS)) { if (cityStr.includes(city)) { ref = coords; break; } }
    const fullCode = olcRecover(codeRaw, ref[0], ref[1]); if (!fullCode) return null;
    return olcDecode(fullCode);
  }
  const match = str.match(/^(-?\d{1,3}(?:\.\d+)?)[,\s]+(-?\d{1,3}(?:\.\d+)?)$/);
  if (match) {
    const lat = parseFloat(match[1]), lng = parseFloat(match[2]);
    if (!isNaN(lat) && !isNaN(lng) && lat >= -90 && lat <= 90 && lng >= -180 && lng <= 180) return { lat, lng };
  }
  return null;
}

// ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface Embassy {
  id: number; citizenId: number; citizenName: string | null;
  gpsLat: number; gpsLng: number; address: string | null;
  nodeActive: boolean; uptimeScore: number; status: string;
  nodeStatus: string; lastPingMs: number | null; isHardwareVerified: boolean;
  nftMetadata: string | null; createdAt: string;
}
interface NodeToken { token: string; qrPayload: string; expiresAt: string; embassyId: number }

// ── Node status badge ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function nodeStatusColor(s: string) {
  if (s === 'online') return '#22c55e';
  if (s === 'pending_verification') return '#f59e0b';
  return '#6b7280';
}
function nodeStatusLabel(s: string) {
  if (s === 'online')                return 'ONLINE';
  if (s === 'pending_verification')  return 'ПРОВЕРКА';
  return 'OFFLINE';
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function EmbassiesScreen() {
  const colors  = useColors();
  const insets  = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { citizen } = useCitizen();
  const qc      = useQueryClient();
  const [showForm,   setShowForm]   = useState(false);
  const [showVerify, setShowVerify] = useState<Embassy | null>(null);
  const [location,   setLocation]   = useState('');
  const [address,    setAddress]    = useState('');
  const [verifying,  setVerifying]  = useState(false);
  const topPad  = Platform.OS === 'web' ? 67 : insets.top;
  const styles  = getStyles(colors);

  const coords   = useMemo(() => resolveLocation(location), [location]);
  const hasInput = location.trim().length > 0;

  const { data: embassies = [], isLoading } = useQuery<Embassy[]>({
    queryKey: ['embassies'],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/embassies`);
      return res.json() as Promise<Embassy[]>;
    },
    refetchInterval: 30_000,
  });

  // My embassy (for verification panel)
  const myEmbassy = citizen ? embassies.find(e => e.citizenId === citizen.id) : null;

  // Node token query — only fetches when verify modal is open
  const { data: nodeToken, isLoading: tokenLoading, refetch: refetchToken } = useQuery<NodeToken>({
    queryKey: ['node-token', showVerify?.id],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/embassies/${showVerify!.id}/node-token`);
      const d = await res.json() as NodeToken & { error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(d.error ?? 'Ошибка');
      return d;
    },
    enabled: !!showVerify,
    staleTime: 0,
    gcTime: 0,
  });

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      if (!coords) throw new Error('Не удалось распознать координаты');
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/embassies`, {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ citizenId: citizen!.id, gpsLat: coords.lat, gpsLng: coords.lng, address: address.trim() || undefined }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((data as { error: string }).error);
      return data as Embassy;
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['embassies'] });
      setShowForm(false); setLocation(''); setAddress('');
      Alert.alert('🏛 Посольство зарегистрировано', 'Статус «Посол» активирован. NFT-сертификат выпущен.');
    },
    onError: (err: Error) => Alert.alert('Ошибка', err.message),
  });

  // ── Proof-of-Location verify ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  const handleVerifyLocation = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!nodeToken || !showVerify) return;
    setVerifying(true);
    try {
      const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
      if (status !== 'granted') {
        Alert.alert('Нет доступа к GPS', 'Разреши приложению использовать геолокацию в настройках');
        return;
      }
      const pos = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.BestForNavigation });
      const { latitude: phoneLat, longitude: phoneLng } = pos.coords;

      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/embassies/verify-location`, {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ token: nodeToken.token, phoneLat, phoneLng }),
      });
      const data = await res.json() as { success?: boolean; message?: string; error?: string; distanceM?: number };

      if (!res.ok) {
        Alert.alert('Верификация не пройдена', data.error ?? 'Ошибка');
        return;
      }

      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['embassies'] });
      setShowVerify(null);
      Alert.alert('✅ Нода активирована!', data.message ?? 'Аптайм-майнинг запущен.');
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert('Ошибка', 'Не удалось получить GPS. Проверь интернет и разрешения.');
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  }, [nodeToken, showVerify, qc]);

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: topPad }]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View style={styles.headerBrand}>
          <LogoBrand size={28} />
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>ПОСОЛЬСТВА</Text>
        </View>
        {citizen && (
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.addBtn} onPress={() => setShowForm(true)} activeOpacity={0.8}>
            <Feather name="plus" size={18} color={colors.primaryForeground} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.infoCard}>
        <Feather name="map-pin" size={16} color={colors.primary} />
        <Text style={styles.infoText}>
          Предоставь 1 м² под ноду — получай Дармины за аптайм. Верификация: Посол + QR Proof-of-Location.
        </Text>
      </View>

      {/* My node panel */}
      {myEmbassy && (
        <View style={[styles.myNodePanel, { borderColor: nodeStatusColor(myEmbassy.nodeStatus) + '66' }]}>
          <View style={styles.myNodeRow}>
            <View style={[styles.nodeStatusDot, { backgroundColor: nodeStatusColor(myEmbassy.nodeStatus) }]} />
            <Text style={[styles.myNodeStatus, { color: nodeStatusColor(myEmbassy.nodeStatus) }]}>
              {nodeStatusLabel(myEmbassy.nodeStatus)}
            </Text>
            {myEmbassy.isHardwareVerified && (
              <View style={styles.verifiedBadge}>
                <Feather name="shield" size={11} color="#22c55e" />
                <Text style={styles.verifiedText}>ВЕРИФИЦИРОВАНО</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
          {!myEmbassy.isHardwareVerified && (
            <View style={{ gap: 8 }}>
              {/* Native QR-scanner — full camera experience */}
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.verifyBtn}
                onPress={() => router.push('/embassy/verify')}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
              >
                <Feather name="camera" size={14} color={colors.primaryForeground} />
                <Text style={styles.verifyBtnText}>Сканировать QR ноды (камера)</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              {/* Fallback: GPS-only (no QR camera, token from modal) */}
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.verifyBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                onPress={() => setShowVerify(myEmbassy)}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
              >
                <Feather name="navigation" size={14} color={colors.primary} />
                <Text style={[styles.verifyBtnText, { color: colors.primary }]}>Верифицировать через GPS (без камеры)</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      )}

      {isLoading && <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 40 }} />}

      <FlatList
        data={embassies}
        keyExtractor={(e) => String(e.id)}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, paddingBottom: 120 }}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          !isLoading ? (
            <View style={styles.empty}>
              <Feather name="globe" size={40} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
              <Text style={styles.emptyText}>Нет зарегистрированных посольств</Text>
              <Text style={styles.emptySub}>Стань первым Послом TimeNation</Text>
            </View>
          ) : null
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
              <View style={styles.avatar}>
                <Text style={styles.avatarText}>{(item.citizenName ?? '?').charAt(0)}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.cardInfo}>
                <Text style={styles.cardName}>{item.citizenName ?? 'Неизвестный'}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.cardAddr}>
                  {item.address ?? `${item.gpsLat.toFixed(4)}, ${item.gpsLng.toFixed(4)}`}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.nodeBadge, { backgroundColor: nodeStatusColor(item.nodeStatus) + '22' }]}>
                <View style={[styles.nodeDot, { backgroundColor: nodeStatusColor(item.nodeStatus) }]} />
                <Text style={[styles.nodeText, { color: nodeStatusColor(item.nodeStatus) }]}>
                  {nodeStatusLabel(item.nodeStatus)}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.cardFooter}>
              <Feather name="award" size={12} color={colors.primary} />
              <Text style={styles.cardNft}>
                NFT Посол · Аптайм: {item.uptimeScore.toFixed(1)}%
                {item.lastPingMs ? `  · Пинг: ${item.lastPingMs}ms` : ''}
                {item.isHardwareVerified ? '  · ✓ PoL' : ''}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}
      />

      {/* ── Registration Modal ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <Modal visible={showForm} transparent animationType="slide">
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <ScrollView style={styles.modalSheet} contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 24 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
            <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>🏛 Зарегистрировать посольство</Text>
            <Text style={styles.modalSub}>Введи координаты или Google Plus Code площадки под ноду</Text>
            <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Местоположение / Plus Code</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.input, hasInput && !coords && { borderColor: '#ef4444' }, coords && { borderColor: '#22c55e' }]}
              placeholder="4WHF+GW5 Фамагуста  или  35.1211, 33.9363"
              placeholderTextColor={colors.mutedForeground}
              value={location} onChangeText={setLocation} autoCapitalize="characters" autoCorrect={false}
            />
            {coords ? (
              <View style={styles.coordPreview}>
                <Feather name="check-circle" size={14} color="#22c55e" />
                <Text style={styles.coordPreviewText}>{coords.lat.toFixed(6)},  {coords.lng.toFixed(6)}</Text>
              </View>
            ) : hasInput ? (
              <View style={styles.coordError}>
                <Feather name="alert-circle" size={14} color="#ef4444" />
                <Text style={styles.coordErrorText}>Не распознано. Попробуй полный код или «35.12, 33.93»</Text>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.coordHint}>
                <Feather name="info" size={13} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
                <Text style={styles.coordHintText}>Google Plus Code (короткий/полный) или «широта, долгота»</Text>
              </View>
            )}
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.input, { marginTop: 4 }]} placeholder="Адрес (необязательно)"
              placeholderTextColor={colors.mutedForeground} value={address} onChangeText={setAddress}
            />
            <View style={styles.nftNote}>
              <Feather name="award" size={14} color={colors.primary} />
              <Text style={styles.nftNoteText}>NFT-сертификат «Посол TimeNation» выпускается автоматически. После регистрации — пройди Proof-of-Location чтобы активировать аптайм.</Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.submitBtn, (createMutation.isPending || !coords) && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
              onPress={() => createMutation.mutate()} disabled={createMutation.isPending || !coords} activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              {createMutation.isPending ? <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primaryForeground} /> : <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>Зарегистрировать</Text>}
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cancelBtn} onPress={() => { setShowForm(false); setLocation(''); setAddress(''); }} activeOpacity={0.8}>
              <Text style={styles.cancelBtnText}>Отмена</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* ── Proof-of-Location Modal ─────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <Modal visible={!!showVerify} transparent animationType="slide">
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <ScrollView style={styles.modalSheet} contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 24 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
            <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>📡 Proof of Location</Text>
            <Text style={styles.modalSub}>
              Находясь в радиусе 1 метра от оборудования ноды, нажми кнопку — система считает твой GPS и верифицирует присутствие.
            </Text>

            {/* QR code display */}
            {tokenLoading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} style={{ marginVertical: 24 }} />
            ) : nodeToken ? (
              <View style={styles.qrSection}>
                <Image
                  source={{ uri: `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&margin=8&data=${encodeURIComponent(nodeToken.qrPayload)}` }}
                  style={styles.qrImage}
                  resizeMode="contain"
                />
                <Text style={styles.qrCaption}>QR-код для сканирования другим устройством</Text>
                <View style={styles.tokenBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.tokenExpiry}>
                    Действителен 10 минут · истекает в {new Date(nodeToken.expiresAt).toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.polSteps}>
              {[
                { icon: 'map-pin', text: 'Находишься рядом с оборудованием ноды' },
                { icon: 'wifi',    text: 'GPS-сигнал активен на смартфоне' },
                { icon: 'shield',  text: 'Разреши доступ к геолокации при запросе' },
              ].map((step, i) => (
                <View key={i} style={styles.polStep}>
                  <View style={styles.polStepIcon}><Feather name={step.icon as 'map-pin'} size={14} color={colors.primary} /></View>
                  <Text style={styles.polStepText}>{step.text}</Text>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.submitBtn, verifying && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
              onPress={() => void handleVerifyLocation()}
              disabled={verifying || !nodeToken}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              {verifying ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primaryForeground} />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Feather name="navigation" size={16} color={colors.primaryForeground} />
                  <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>  Верифицировать моё присутствие</Text>
                </>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cancelBtn} onPress={() => { setShowVerify(null); refetchToken(); }} activeOpacity={0.8}>
              <Text style={styles.cancelBtnText}>Закрыть</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

    modalTitle:       { fontSize: 20, fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold', color: colors.foreground, marginBottom: 6 },
    modalSub:         { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Inter_400Regular', color: colors.mutedForeground, marginBottom: 20, lineHeight: 20 },

    nftNoteText:      { flex: 1, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: 'Inter_400Regular', color: colors.primary, lineHeight: 18 },
    submitBtn:        { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: 12, padding: 16, marginBottom: 10 },

    cancelBtn:        { alignItems: 'center', padding: 12 },
