                <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
                  <View>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "700", color: colors.foreground, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold" }}>
                      {selectedSlot.hourLabel}:{selectedSlot.minLabel} · {bookDate}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 12, color: colors.mutedForeground }}>
                      {SLOT_TYPE_LABELS[selectedSlot.slotType]}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <Pressable onPress={() => setSelectedSlot(null)} style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                    <Feather name="x" size={18} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
                  </Pressable>
                </View>

                {/* Payment toggle */}
                <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: 8 }}>
                  <Pressable
                    onPress={() => setPaymentType("timeltr")}
                    style={({ pressed }) => ({
                      flex: 1, padding: 10, borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1.5,
                      borderColor: paymentType === "timeltr" ? colors.primary : colors.border,
                      backgroundColor: pressed ? colors.primary + "22" : paymentType === "timeltr" ? colors.primary + "18" : colors.background,
                      alignItems: "center", gap: 2,
                    })}
                  >
                    <Feather name="clock" size={14} color={paymentType === "timeltr" ? colors.primary : colors.mutedForeground} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: "700", color: paymentType === "timeltr" ? colors.primary : colors.mutedForeground }}>TiMETR</Text>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, color: paymentType === "timeltr" ? colors.primary : colors.mutedForeground }}>{totalCost} Д</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                  <Pressable
                    onPress={() => setPaymentType("fiat")}
                    style={({ pressed }) => ({
                      flex: 1, padding: 10, borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1.5,
                      borderColor: paymentType === "fiat" ? "#22c55e" : colors.border,
                      backgroundColor: pressed ? "#22c55e22" : paymentType === "fiat" ? "#22c55e18" : colors.background,
                      alignItems: "center", gap: 2,
                    })}
                  >
                    <Feather name="credit-card" size={14} color={paymentType === "fiat" ? "#22c55e" : colors.mutedForeground} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: "700", color: paymentType === "fiat" ? "#22c55e" : colors.mutedForeground }}>FIAT</Text>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, color: paymentType === "fiat" ? "#22c55e" : colors.mutedForeground }}>{FIAT_PRICES[selectedSlot.slotType] ?? "?"}</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>

                {/* Book button */}
                <Pressable
                  style={({ pressed }) => {
                    const active = paymentType === "fiat" || canAfford;
                    return {
                      backgroundColor: active ? (pressed ? colors.primary + "cc" : colors.primary) : colors.muted,
                      height: 48, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center",
                    };
                  }}
                  onPress={() => { setBookError(null); bookMutation.mutate(); }}
                  disabled={bookMutation.isPending || (paymentType === "timeltr" && !canAfford)}
                >
                  {bookMutation.isPending ? (
                    <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={{ color: "#fff", fontWeight: "700", fontSize: 15, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold" }}>
                      {paymentType === "fiat"
                        ? `Оплатить ${FIAT_PRICES[selectedSlot.slotType] ?? ""} через Stripe`
                        : canAfford
                        ? `Забронировать за ${totalCost} Д`
                        : "Недостаточно Дарминов"}
                    </Text>
                  )}
                </Pressable>

                {/* Inline error — visible on all platforms (replaces Alert.alert) */}
                {bookError && (
                  <View style={{ backgroundColor: "#7f1d1d22", borderWidth: 1, borderColor: "#ef444455", borderRadius: 8, padding: 10 }}>
                    <Text style={{ color: "#ef4444", fontSize: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>{bookError}</Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

// ── ChronosLivePulse ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface LiveSlotInfo {
  minuteIndex: number;
  videoUrl: string | null;
  hasContent: boolean;
  ownerName: string | null;
  slotType: string | null;
  slotId: number | null;
}

function ChronosLivePulse({ date, colors }: { date: string; colors: ReturnType<typeof useColors> }) {
  const [currentMinute, setCurrentMinute] = useState(() => {
    const n = new Date();
    return n.getUTCHours() * 60 + n.getUTCMinutes();
  });
  const [utcClock, setUtcClock] = useState("");
  const [slot, setSlot] = useState<LiveSlotInfo | null>(null);
  const prevMinute = useRef(-1);
  const prefetchRef = useRef<{ minute: number; data: LiveSlotInfo | null } | null>(null);

  const fetchSlot = useCallback(async (minute: number) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/tv/live-slot?date=${date}&minute=${minute}`);
      if (!res.ok) return null;
      return (await res.json()) as LiveSlotInfo;
    } catch { return null; }
  }, [date]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const n = new Date();
    const m = n.getUTCHours() * 60 + n.getUTCMinutes();
    setCurrentMinute(m);
    void fetchSlot(m).then((d) => { if (d) setSlot(d); });
  }, [fetchSlot]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const n = new Date();
      const h = n.getUTCHours(), m = n.getUTCMinutes(), s = n.getUTCSeconds();
      const minute = h * 60 + m;
      const p = (x: number) => String(x).padStart(2, "0");
      setUtcClock(`${p(h)}:${p(m)}:${p(s)} UTC`);
      if (s >= 55) {
        const next = (minute + 1) % 1440;
        if (!prefetchRef.current || prefetchRef.current.minute !== next) {
          void fetchSlot(next).then((d) => { prefetchRef.current = { minute: next, data: d }; });
        }
      }
      if (minute !== prevMinute.current) {
        prevMinute.current = minute;
        setCurrentMinute(minute);
        if (prefetchRef.current?.minute === minute) {
          const d = prefetchRef.current.data;
          prefetchRef.current = null;
          if (d) setSlot(d);
        } else {
          void fetchSlot(minute).then((d) => { if (d) setSlot(d); });
        }
      }
    }, 500);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [fetchSlot]);

  const hh = String(Math.floor(currentMinute / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const mm2 = String(currentMinute % 60).padStart(2, "0");

  const openLive = async () => {
    if (!slot?.videoUrl) return;
    const url = slot.videoUrl.startsWith("http") ? slot.videoUrl : `${BASE_URL}${slot.videoUrl}`;
    await WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync(url, { toolbarColor: "#0D1B2A" });
  };

  return (
    <View style={{
      flexDirection: "row",
      alignItems: "center",
      paddingHorizontal: 14,
      paddingVertical: 9,
      backgroundColor: colors.card,
      borderBottomWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
      borderBottomColor: colors.border,
      gap: 8,
    }}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 5 }}>
        <View style={{
          width: 7, height: 7, borderRadius: 4,
          backgroundColor: "#f97316",
          shadowColor: "#f97316",
          shadowRadius: 4,
          shadowOpacity: 0.8,
          shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 0 },
        }} />
        <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold", color: "#f97316", letterSpacing: 1.5 }}>В ЭФИРЕ</Text>
      </View>
      <Text style={{ fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold", color: colors.primary, fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
        {hh}:{mm2}
      </Text>
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        {slot?.hasContent ? (
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            {slot.slotType && (
              <View style={{ backgroundColor: colors.primary + "22", borderRadius: 5, paddingHorizontal: 5, paddingVertical: 1 }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 9, fontFamily: "Inter_600SemiBold", color: colors.primary }}>{SLOT_TYPE_LABELS[slot.slotType] ?? slot.slotType}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 12, color: colors.foreground, fontFamily: "Inter_500Medium" }} numberOfLines={1}>
              {slot.ownerName}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, color: colors.mutedForeground, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular" }}>
            Эфир ожидает · слот {hh}:{mm2}
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>
      <TouchableOpacity
        onPress={() => router.push("/broadcast")}
        style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 4, backgroundColor: "#f97316", paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 8 }}
        activeOpacity={0.8}
      >
        <Feather name="radio" size={11} color="#fff" />
        <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold", color: "#fff" }}>Эфир</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    </View>
  );
}

// ── Main screen ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function TvScreen() {
  const colors = useColors();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { citizen } = useCitizen();
  const [date, setDate] = useState(todayISO());
  const [showDatePicker, setShowDatePicker] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSlot, setSelectedSlot] = useState<TvSlot | null>(null);
  const [showAgeGate, setShowAgeGate] = useState(false);
  const [showBookModal, setShowBookModal] = useState(false);
  const [showCalBooking, setShowCalBooking] = useState(false);
  const [gridTappedSlot, setGridTappedSlot] = useState<TvSlot | null>(null);
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"list" | "grid">("list");
  const [citizenAgeVerified, setCitizenAgeVerified] = useState(
    () => !!(citizen?.ageVerified ?? 0)
  );
  const topPad = Platform.OS === "web" ? 67 : insets.top;
  const styles = getStyles(colors);
  const tvGuide = useContextualGuide("tv");

  const { data, isLoading, refetch, isRefetching } = useQuery<{ date: string; count: number; slots: TvSlot[] }>({
    queryKey: ["tvSlots", date],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/tv/slots?date=${date}`);
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Ошибка загрузки слотов");
      return res.json();
    },
    enabled: !!citizen,
  });

  const mySlots = (data?.slots ?? []).filter((s) => s.ownerId === citizen?.id);
  const freeCount = (data?.slots ?? []).filter((s) => s.status === "free").length;

  const handlePrevDay = useCallback(() => setDate((d) => stepDate(d, -1)), []);
  const handleNextDay = useCallback(() => setDate((d) => stepDate(d, 1)), []);

  // ── Dual clock (Local + UTC) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  const [localTime, setLocalTime] = useState("--:--:--");
  const [utcTime, setUtcTime] = useState("--:--:--");
  const [clockSynced, setClockSynced] = useState(false);
  const clockOffset = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = async () => {
      try {
        const before = Date.now();
        const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/system/time`);
        const after = Date.now();
        if (res.ok) {
          const d = (await res.json()) as { ts: number };
          clockOffset.current = d.ts - after + (after - before) / 2;
          setClockSynced(true);
        }
      } catch { setClockSynced(false); }
    };
    void sync();
    const syncTimer = setInterval(sync, 60_000);
    const tick = setInterval(() => {
      const nowLocal = new Date();
      const nowServer = new Date(Date.now() + clockOffset.current);
      const p = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
      setLocalTime(`${p(nowLocal.getHours())}:${p(nowLocal.getMinutes())}:${p(nowLocal.getSeconds())}`);
      setUtcTime(nowServer.toISOString().slice(11, 19));
    }, 1000);
    return () => { clearInterval(tick); clearInterval(syncTimer); };
  }, []);

  const renderSlotCard = ({ item }: { item: TvSlot }) => (
    <TouchableOpacity
      style={[styles.slotCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}
      onPress={() => setSelectedSlot(item)}
      activeOpacity={0.75}
    >
      <View style={styles.slotCardLeft}>
        <Text style={[styles.slotTime, { color: colors.foreground }]}>{slotLabel(item)}</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.slotType, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
          {SLOT_TYPE_LABELS[item.slotType] ?? item.slotType}
          {item.isRetro ? " · Ретро" : ""}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.slotCardRight}>
        <View style={[styles.slotBadge, {
          backgroundColor: item.status === "broadcast"
            ? colors.primary + "33"
            : colors.primary + "22",
        }]}>
          <Feather
            name={item.status === "broadcast" ? "film" : "clock"}
            size={12}
            color={colors.primary}
          />
          <Text style={[styles.slotBadgeText, { color: colors.primary }]}>
            {item.status === "broadcast" ? "Вышел в эфир" : "Мой слот"}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={colors.mutedForeground} style={{ marginLeft: 8 }} />
      </View>
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: topPad, backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
      <LogoBrand />

      {/* ── Dual clock ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <View style={[styles.clockBar, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
        {/* Local time — primary */}
        <View style={styles.clockBlock}>
          <Text style={[styles.clockLabel, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>LOCAL</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.clockValueLarge, { color: colors.foreground }]}>{localTime}</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Sync dot */}
        <View style={styles.clockSyncDot}>
          <View style={[styles.syncDot, { backgroundColor: clockSynced ? "#22c55e" : "#f59e0b" }]} />
          <Text style={[styles.syncLabel, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>{clockSynced ? "SYNC" : "..."}</Text>
        </View>

        {/* UTC time — secondary */}
        <View style={[styles.clockBlock, { alignItems: "flex-end" }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.clockLabel, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>UTC</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.clockValueSmall, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>{utcTime}</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* ── Chronos Live Pulse ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <ChronosLivePulse date={date} colors={colors} />

      {/* Date nav */}
      <View style={[styles.dateNav, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
        <TouchableOpacity onPress={handlePrevDay} style={styles.dateArrow}>
          <Feather name="chevron-left" size={20} color={colors.foreground} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => setShowDatePicker(true)}
          style={styles.dateLabelBtn}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <Feather name="calendar" size={14} color={colors.primary} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
          <Text style={[styles.dateLabel, { color: colors.foreground }]}>{date}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity onPress={handleNextDay} style={styles.dateArrow}>
          <Feather name="chevron-right" size={20} color={colors.foreground} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Summary row */}
      {data && (
        <View style={[styles.summaryRow, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
          {/* View toggle: LIST | GRID */}
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: 4 }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={{
                paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 7,
                backgroundColor: viewMode === "list" ? colors.primary + "22" : "transparent",
                borderWidth: 1, borderColor: viewMode === "list" ? colors.primary + "66" : colors.border,
              }}
              onPress={() => setViewMode("list")}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: "600", color: viewMode === "list" ? colors.primary : colors.mutedForeground }}>
                Список
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={{
                paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 7,
                backgroundColor: viewMode === "grid" ? colors.primary + "22" : "transparent",
                borderWidth: 1, borderColor: viewMode === "grid" ? colors.primary + "66" : colors.border,
              }}
              onPress={() => setViewMode("grid")}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: "600", color: viewMode === "grid" ? colors.primary : colors.mutedForeground }}>
                Сетка
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.summaryActions}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.bookSlotBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary + "22", borderColor: colors.primary + "55" }]}
              onPress={() => { setGridTappedSlot(null); tvGuide.trigger(() => { setShowCalBooking(true); }); }}
              activeOpacity={0.75}
            >
              <Feather name="calendar" size={12} color={colors.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.bookSlotBtnText, { color: colors.primary }]}>Забронировать</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => refetch()} disabled={isRefetching}>
              <Feather
                name="refresh-cw"
                size={15}
                color={isRefetching ? colors.mutedForeground : colors.primary}
              />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* No citizen */}
      {!citizen && (
        <View style={styles.emptyState}>
          <Feather name="user-x" size={40} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
          <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>Войдите в аккаунт для доступа к TV</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Loading */}
      {isLoading && (
        <View style={styles.emptyState}>
          <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} size="large" />
          <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>Загрузка слотов…</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* List mode: my booked slots */}
      {!isLoading && viewMode === "list" && citizen && mySlots.length === 0 && (
        <View style={styles.emptyState}>
          <Feather name="tv" size={44} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
          <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: colors.foreground }]}>Нет забронированных слотов</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => tvGuide.trigger(() => { setShowCalBooking(true); })}
            style={{ marginTop: 8, backgroundColor: colors.primary + "22", borderRadius: 10, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.primary + "55" }}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: colors.primary, fontWeight: "600", textAlign: "center" }}>
              Открыть календарь бронирования
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      )}

      {!isLoading && viewMode === "list" && mySlots.length > 0 && (
        <FlatList
          data={mySlots}
          keyExtractor={(s) => String(s.id)}
          renderItem={renderSlotCard}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: 8 }} />}
          ListHeaderComponent={
            <Text style={[styles.listHeader, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
              Нажмите на слот чтобы управлять капсулой
            </Text>
          }
        />
      )}

      {/* Grid mode: ChronosGrid — all 1440 slots */}
      {!isLoading && viewMode === "grid" && citizen && (
        <ChronosGrid
          slots={data?.slots ?? []}
          citizenId={citizen.id}
          date={date}
          colors={colors}
          selectedSlotId={gridTappedSlot?.id ?? null}
          onTapSlot={(slot) => {
            if (slot && slot.status === "free" && !slot.isRetro) {
              setGridTappedSlot(slot);
              tvGuide.trigger(() => { setShowCalBooking(true); });
            } else if (slot && slot.ownerId === citizen.id) {
              setSelectedSlot(slot);
            }
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Capsule modal */}
      {selectedSlot && (
        <CapsuleModal
          slot={selectedSlot}
          onClose={() => setSelectedSlot(null)}
          colors={colors}
          citizenAgeVerified={citizenAgeVerified}
          onNeedAgeVerify={() => setShowAgeGate(true)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Age gate modal */}
      {showAgeGate && citizen && (
        <AgeGateModal
          citizenId={citizen.id}
          onVerified={() => {
            setCitizenAgeVerified(true);
            setShowAgeGate(false);
          }}
          onCancel={() => setShowAgeGate(false)}
          colors={colors}
        />
      )}

      {/* Date picker */}
      {showDatePicker && (
        <DatePickerModal
          current={date}
          onSelect={setDate}
          onClose={() => setShowDatePicker(false)}
          colors={colors}
        />
      )}

      {/* Book slot modal (legacy, kept for direct access) */}
      {showBookModal && citizen && (
        <BookSlotModal
          visible={showBookModal}
          onClose={() => setShowBookModal(false)}
          slots={data?.slots ?? []}
          citizen={citizen}
          date={date}
          colors={colors}
          onBooked={() => { void refetch(); }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Calendar booking sheet */}
      {showCalBooking && citizen && (
        <CalendarBookingSheet
          visible={showCalBooking}
          onClose={() => { setShowCalBooking(false); setGridTappedSlot(null); }}
          citizen={citizen}
          initialDate={gridTappedSlot?.date ?? date}
          initialSlot={gridTappedSlot}
          colors={colors}
          onBooked={() => { setGridTappedSlot(null); void refetch(); }}
        />
      )}

      <ContextualGuideModal
        visible={tvGuide.visible}
        onClose={tvGuide.close}
        onConfirm={tvGuide.confirm}
        icon="📺"
        title="Забронировать TV-слот"
        explanation={"Бронирование эфира. Твой текст станет промтом, а бесплатный ИИ-движок отрендерит уникальное кино. Трансляция сильных смыслов ускоряет накопление Меритов в системе.\n\nДоступны слоты :00, :15, :30, :45 каждого часа."}
        ctaLabel="Написать сильный смысл и выбрать визуальный стиль"
      />
    </View>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, ScrollView, Pressable, StyleSheet,
  Platform, Animated, ActivityIndicator,
} from "react-native";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import * as WebBrowser from "expo-web-browser";
import { WebView } from "react-native-webview";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useColors } from "@/hooks/useColors";

const BASE_URL = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "";
const DOMAIN_BASE = `https://${process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN ?? ""}`;

function todayUTC(): string {
  const d = new Date();
  return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

const SLOT_TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  citizen: "Гражданин",
  quarter: "Квартал",
  half: "Полчаса",
  apex: "Апекс",
};

const SLOT_TYPE_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  citizen: "#f59e0b",
  quarter: "#3b82f6",
  half: "#8b5cf6",
  apex: "#ef4444",
};

// Double-buffer seamless player:
//   #va = active video (visible, playing)
//   #vb = standby video (hidden, preloading next slot)
// At second 55 we call preloadNext(url) → loads into standby.
// At minute :00 we call swapToNext() → shows standby, hides active, clears old.
// unmute() removes mute on the active player after user tap.
// setInitialSrc(url) bootstraps the active player on first load / fallback.
const VIDEO_HTML = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,maximum-scale=1"><style>*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box;}body{background:#000;overflow:hidden;}#va,#vb{position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;transition:opacity 0.25s;}#vb{opacity:0;pointer-events:none;}</style></head><body><video id="va" autoplay playsinline muted loop></video><video id="vb" playsinline muted preload="auto" loop></video><script>var cur='a';function ga(){return document.getElementById('v'+cur);}function gs(){return document.getElementById(cur==='a'?'vb':'va');}window.setInitialSrc=function(src){var v=ga();if(src){if(v.src!==src){v.src=src;v.load();}v.style.opacity='1';v.play().catch(function(){});}else{v.pause();v.removeAttribute('src');v.load();}};window.preloadNext=function(src){var s=gs();if(src&&s.src!==src){s.src=src;s.load();}};window.swapToNext=function(){var s=gs();var a=ga();s.style.opacity='1';s.style.pointerEvents='auto';s.play().catch(function(){});a.style.opacity='0';a.style.pointerEvents='none';setTimeout(function(){a.pause();a.removeAttribute('src');a.load();},400);cur=cur==='a'?'b':'a';};window.unmute=function(){ga().muted=false;gs().muted=false;};true;</script></body></html>`;

interface LiveSlotInfo {
  minuteIndex: number;
  videoUrl: string | null;
  hasContent: boolean;
  ownerName: string | null;
  slotType: string | null;
  slotId: number | null;
}

interface ScheduleEntry {
  slotId: number;
  minuteIndex: number;
  slotType: string;
  hourLabel: string;
  minLabel: string;
  ownerName: string | null;
  hasRenderedVideo: boolean;
  catcastUrl: string | null;
  mediaUrl: string | null;
  isRetro: boolean;
}

interface ScheduleResponse {
  date: string;
  totalSlots: number;
  bookedSlots: number;
  freeSlots: number;
  schedule: ScheduleEntry[];
}

function makeAbsoluteUrl(url: string): string {
  return url.startsWith("http") ? url : `${DOMAIN_BASE}${url}`;
}

export default function BroadcastScreen() {
  const colors = useColors();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const topPad = Platform.OS === "web" ? 67 : insets.top + 8;

  const [utcClock, setUtcClock] = useState("");
  const [currentMinute, setCurrentMinute] = useState(() => {
    const n = new Date();
    return n.getUTCHours() * 60 + n.getUTCMinutes();
  });
  const [currentSecond, setCurrentSecond] = useState(0);
  const [date, setDate] = useState(todayUTC);
  const prevMinuteRef = useRef(-1);
  const prefetchRef = useRef<{ minute: number; data: LiveSlotInfo | null } | null>(null);
  const [liveSlot,    setLiveSlot]    = useState<LiveSlotInfo | null>(null);
  const [scheduleTab, setScheduleTab] = useState<"upcoming" | "past">("upcoming");
  const [isMuted,     setIsMuted]     = useState(true);
  const hasPrefetchedRef = useRef(false);

  const videoWebViewRef     = useRef<WebView>(null);
  const currentVideoUrlRef  = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const pulseAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
  const progressAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    const pulse = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(pulseAnim, { toValue: 0.25, duration: 900, useNativeDriver: false }),
        Animated.timing(pulseAnim, { toValue: 1, duration: 900, useNativeDriver: false }),
      ])
    );
    pulse.start();
    return () => pulse.stop();
  }, [pulseAnim]);

  // Set initial video source (first load or fallback with no prefetch)
  const applyInitialVideo = useCallback((url: string | null) => {
    currentVideoUrlRef.current = url;
    hasPrefetchedRef.current = false;
    const js = `window.setInitialSrc(${url ? JSON.stringify(url) : "null"});true;`;
    videoWebViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(js);
  }, []);

  // At minute change: use swap if standby is preloaded, otherwise re-init
  const applyVideoUrl = useCallback((url: string | null) => {
    currentVideoUrlRef.current = url;
    if (hasPrefetchedRef.current) {
      videoWebViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(`window.swapToNext();true;`);
      hasPrefetchedRef.current = false;
    } else {
      const js = `window.setInitialSrc(${url ? JSON.stringify(url) : "null"});true;`;
      videoWebViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(js);
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchLiveSlot = useCallback(async (d: string, minute: number): Promise<LiveSlotInfo | null> => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/tv/live-slot?date=${d}&minute=${minute}`);
      if (!res.ok) return null;
      return (await res.json()) as LiveSlotInfo;
    } catch { return null; }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const d = todayUTC();
    const n = new Date();
    const m = n.getUTCHours() * 60 + n.getUTCMinutes();
    setDate(d);
    setCurrentMinute(m);
    setCurrentSecond(n.getUTCSeconds());
    void fetchLiveSlot(d, m).then((data) => {
      if (data) {
        setLiveSlot(data);
        const url = data.videoUrl ? makeAbsoluteUrl(data.videoUrl) : null;
        applyInitialVideo(url);
      }
    });
  }, [fetchLiveSlot, applyInitialVideo]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const n = new Date();
      const h = n.getUTCHours();
      const m = n.getUTCMinutes();
      const s = n.getUTCSeconds();
      const p = (x: number) => String(x).padStart(2, "0");
      setUtcClock(`${p(h)}:${p(m)}:${p(s)} UTC`);
      setCurrentSecond(s);
      Animated.timing(progressAnim, {
        toValue: s / 59,
        duration: 300,
        useNativeDriver: false,
      }).start();

      const minute = h * 60 + m;
      const d = todayUTC();

      if (s >= 55) {
        const next = (minute + 1) % 1440;
        if (!prefetchRef.current || prefetchRef.current.minute !== next) {
          void fetchLiveSlot(d, next).then((data) => {
            prefetchRef.current = { minute: next, data };
            // Preload video bytes into standby buffer so swap at :00 is instant
            if (data?.videoUrl) {
              const absUrl = makeAbsoluteUrl(data.videoUrl);
              videoWebViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(
                `window.preloadNext(${JSON.stringify(absUrl)});true;`
              );
              hasPrefetchedRef.current = true;
            }
          });
        }
      }

      if (minute !== prevMinuteRef.current) {
        prevMinuteRef.current = minute;
        setCurrentMinute(minute);
        setDate(d);
        progressAnim.setValue(0);

        if (prefetchRef.current?.minute === minute) {
          const data = prefetchRef.current.data;
          prefetchRef.current = null;
          if (data) {
            setLiveSlot(data);
            const url = data.videoUrl ? makeAbsoluteUrl(data.videoUrl) : null;
            applyVideoUrl(url);
          }
        } else {
          void fetchLiveSlot(d, minute).then((data) => {
            if (data) {
              setLiveSlot(data);
              const url = data.videoUrl ? makeAbsoluteUrl(data.videoUrl) : null;
              applyVideoUrl(url);
            }
          });
        }
      }
    }, 500);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [fetchLiveSlot, applyVideoUrl, progressAnim]);

  const { data: scheduleData, isLoading: scheduleLoading, refetch: refetchSchedule } =
    useQuery<ScheduleResponse>({
      queryKey: ["tvSchedule", date],
      queryFn: async () => {
        const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/tv/schedule?date=${date}`);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Ошибка загрузки расписания");
        return res.json() as Promise<ScheduleResponse>;
      },
      staleTime: 60_000,
      refetchInterval: 60_000,
    });

  const hourLabel = String(Math.floor(currentMinute / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const minLabel = String(currentMinute % 60).padStart(2, "0");

  const { upcoming, past } = useMemo(() => {
    const all = scheduleData?.schedule ?? [];
    const up = all
      .filter((s) => s.minuteIndex > currentMinute)
      .sort((a, b) => a.minuteIndex - b.minuteIndex);
    const pa = all
      .filter((s) => s.minuteIndex <= currentMinute)
      .sort((a, b) => b.minuteIndex - a.minuteIndex);
    return { upcoming: up, past: pa };
  }, [scheduleData, currentMinute]);

  const listData = scheduleTab === "upcoming" ? upcoming : past;

  const openVideo = async (entry: ScheduleEntry) => {
    const url = entry.catcastUrl ?? (entry.mediaUrl ? makeAbsoluteUrl(entry.mediaUrl) : null);
    if (!url) return;
    await WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync(url, { toolbarColor: "#0D1B2A" });
  };

  const openExternal = async () => {
    if (!liveSlot?.videoUrl) return;
    await WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync(makeAbsoluteUrl(liveSlot.videoUrl), { toolbarColor: "#0D1B2A" });
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: topPad }]}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backBtn} hitSlop={12}>
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={22} color={colors.foreground} />
        </Pressable>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 7 }}>
          <Animated.View style={[styles.liveDot, { opacity: pulseAnim }]} />
          <Text style={[styles.headerTitle, { color: "#f97316" }]}>ЭФИР</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.clockSmall, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>{utcClock}</Text>
      </View>

      <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>

        {/* ── Inline Video Player ── */}
        <View style={styles.videoContainer}>
          <WebView
            ref={videoWebViewRef}
            source={{ html: VIDEO_HTML, baseUrl: DOMAIN_BASE }}
            style={styles.webview}
            mediaPlaybackRequiresUserAction={false}
            allowsInlineMediaPlayback={true}
            allowsFullscreenVideo={false}
            scrollEnabled={false}
            onLoadEnd={() => {
              if (currentVideoUrlRef.current) {
                videoWebViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(
                  `window.setInitialSrc(${JSON.stringify(currentVideoUrlRef.current)});true;`
                );
              }
            }}
          />
          {/* Sound toggle overlay */}
          <Pressable
            style={styles.muteBtn}
            onPress={() => {
              videoWebViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(`window.unmute();true;`);
              setIsMuted(false);
            }}
            hitSlop={12}
          >
            <Feather name={isMuted ? "volume-x" : "volume-2"} size={16} color="#fff" />
          </Pressable>
          {!liveSlot?.hasContent && (
            <View style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject, styles.videoIdle]}>
              <Animated.View style={[styles.heroPulseDot, { opacity: pulseAnim, width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: 16 }]} />
              <Text style={styles.videoIdleTitle}>TN · TV</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.videoIdleSubtitle, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
                Эфир ожидает · {hourLabel}:{minLabel}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* NOW LIVE info card */}
        <View style={[styles.heroCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: "#f9731622" }]}>
          {/* Label row */}
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginBottom: 10 }}>
            <Animated.View style={[styles.heroPulseDot, { opacity: pulseAnim }]} />
            <Text style={styles.heroLiveLabel}>В ЭФИРЕ СЕЙЧАС</Text>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "#f97316aa", fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular" }}>UTC</Text>
          </View>

          {/* Big time */}
          <Text style={[styles.heroBigTime, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
            {hourLabel}:{minLabel}
          </Text>

          {/* Seconds progress bar */}
          <View style={[styles.progressTrack, { backgroundColor: colors.muted }]}>
            <Animated.View
              style={[styles.progressFill, {
                width: progressAnim.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: ["0%", "100%"] }),
              }]}
            />
          </View>
          <Text style={[styles.progressLabel, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
            {59 - currentSecond} с до смены слота
          </Text>

          {liveSlot?.hasContent ? (
            <View style={{ marginTop: 14, gap: 8 }}>
              <Text style={[styles.heroOwner, { color: colors.foreground }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {liveSlot.ownerName ?? "Гражданин"}
              </Text>
              {liveSlot.slotType && (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: "row" }}>
                  <View style={[styles.typeBadge, {
                    backgroundColor: (SLOT_TYPE_COLORS[liveSlot.slotType] ?? "#f59e0b") + "22",
                    borderColor: (SLOT_TYPE_COLORS[liveSlot.slotType] ?? "#f59e0b") + "55",
                  }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.typeBadgeText, {
                      color: SLOT_TYPE_COLORS[liveSlot.slotType] ?? "#f59e0b",
                    }]}>
                      {SLOT_TYPE_LABELS[liveSlot.slotType] ?? liveSlot.slotType}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              )}
              {liveSlot.videoUrl && (
                <Pressable
                  style={({ pressed }) => [styles.externalBtn, { borderColor: colors.border, opacity: pressed ? 0.6 : 1 }]}
                  onPress={openExternal}
                >
                  <Feather name="external-link" size={12} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
                  <Text style={[styles.externalBtnText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>Открыть во внешнем браузере</Text>
                </Pressable>
              )}
            </View>
          ) : (
            <View style={{ marginTop: 14, alignItems: "center", gap: 6, paddingVertical: 8 }}>
              <Feather name="radio" size={28} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 14, color: colors.mutedForeground, fontFamily: "Inter_500Medium" }}>
                Сигнал TimeNation
              </Text>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, color: colors.mutedForeground + "80", fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular" }}>
                Этот слот не занят — эфирная заставка
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* Stats row */}
        {scheduleData && (
          <View style={[styles.statsRow, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
            {[
              { label: "Занято", value: scheduleData.bookedSlots },
              { label: "Свободно", value: scheduleData.freeSlots },
              { label: "Всего", value: scheduleData.totalSlots },
            ].map(({ label, value }) => (
              <View key={label} style={styles.statItem}>
                <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: colors.foreground }]}>{value}</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.statLabel, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>{label}</Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Schedule section */}
        <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 16, marginTop: 20 }}>
          <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
            <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
              РАСПИСАНИЕ · {date}
            </Text>
            <Pressable onPress={() => void refetchSchedule()} style={{ padding: 4 }}>
              <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={14} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {/* Tab toggle */}
          <View style={[styles.tabToggle, { backgroundColor: colors.muted }]}>
            {(["upcoming", "past"] as const).map((tab) => (
              <Pressable
                key={tab}
                style={[styles.tabBtn, scheduleTab === tab && { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}
                onPress={() => setScheduleTab(tab)}
              >
                <Text style={{
                  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: "600",
                  color: scheduleTab === tab ? colors.foreground : colors.mutedForeground,
                  fontFamily: "Inter_600SemiBold",
                }}>
                  {tab === "upcoming"
                    ? `Предстоящие (${upcoming.length})`
                    : `Прошедшие (${past.length})`}
                </Text>
              </Pressable>
            ))}
          </View>

          {scheduleLoading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 32 }} />
          ) : listData.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyState}>
              <Feather name="calendar" size={36} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
              <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
                {scheduleTab === "upcoming"
                  ? "Нет предстоящих трансляций на сегодня"
                  : "Нет прошедших трансляций"}
              </Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            listData.map((item) => {
              const isLive = item.minuteIndex === currentMinute;
              const typeColor = SLOT_TYPE_COLORS[item.slotType] ?? "#f59e0b";
              const canWatch = item.hasRenderedVideo || !!item.catcastUrl;

              return (
                <Pressable
                  key={item.slotId}
                  style={({ pressed }) => [styles.scheduleItem, {
                    backgroundColor: isLive
                      ? "#f9731614"
                      : pressed && canWatch
                        ? colors.muted
                        : "transparent",
                    borderLeftColor: isLive ? "#f97316" : typeColor + "77",
                  }]}
                  onPress={() => canWatch ? void openVideo(item) : undefined}
                >
                  {/* Time */}
                  <View style={styles.scheduleLeft}>
                    {isLive && (
                      <Animated.View style={[styles.liveDotSmall, { opacity: pulseAnim }]} />
                    )}
                    <Text style={[styles.scheduleTime, {
                      color: isLive ? "#f97316" : colors.foreground,
                    }]}>
                      {item.hourLabel}:{item.minLabel}
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  {/* Info */}
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 3 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.scheduleOwner, { color: colors.foreground }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {item.ownerName ?? "Гражданин"}
                    </Text>
                    <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
                      <View style={[styles.typeBadge, {
                        backgroundColor: typeColor + "22",
                        borderColor: typeColor + "55",
                      }]}>
                        <Text style={[styles.typeBadgeText, { color: typeColor }]}>
                          {SLOT_TYPE_LABELS[item.slotType] ?? item.slotType}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                      {item.isRetro && !canWatch && (
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 9, color: colors.mutedForeground }}>
                          нет видео
                        </Text>
                      )}
                    </View>
                  </View>

                  {/* Watch button */}
                  {canWatch ? (
                    <View style={[styles.watchBadge, { borderColor: colors.primary + "55" }]}>
                      <Feather name="play" size={11} color={colors.primary} />
                      <Text style={[styles.watchBadgeText, { color: colors.primary }]}>
                        Смотреть
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  ) : (
                    <Feather name="lock" size={14} color={colors.mutedForeground + "55"} />
                  )}
                </Pressable>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={{ height: 48 }} />
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/app/shop.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  ScrollView,
  TextInput,
  Platform,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import * as Haptics from "expo-haptics";
import * as WebBrowser from "expo-web-browser";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { useCitizen } from "@/context/CitizenContext";
import { useColors } from "@/hooks/useColors";
import { LogoBrand } from "@/components/LogoBrand";

const BASE_URL = `https://${process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN}`;

// ── Discount engine (mirrors server + web) ─────────────────────────────────────

function calcDarminDiscount(amount: number): number {
  if (amount > 10000) return 10;
  if (amount >= 5000)  return 8;
  if (amount >= 1000)  return 7;
  if (amount >= 500)   return 6;
  if (amount >= 100)   return 5;
  return 0;
}

function calcPrice(amount: number) {
  const discountPct  = calcDarminDiscount(amount);
  const baseEur      = amount;
  const totalEur     = parseFloat((amount * (1 - discountPct / 100)).toFixed(2));
  const eurPerDarmin = parseFloat((totalEur / amount).toFixed(4));
  return { discountPct, baseEur, totalEur, eurPerDarmin };
}

const QUICK_AMOUNTS = [100, 500, 1000, 5000] as const;

const TIERS = [
  { label: "1–99 Д",          discount: 0,  from: 1     },
  { label: "100–499 Д",       discount: 5,  from: 100   },
  { label: "500–999 Д",       discount: 6,  from: 500   },
  { label: "1 000–4 999 Д",   discount: 7,  from: 1000  },
  { label: "5 000–10 000 Д",  discount: 8,  from: 5000  },
  { label: "свыше 10 000 Д",  discount: 10, from: 10001 },
] as const;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function ShopScreen() {
  const colors  = useColors();
  const insets  = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { citizen, refreshCitizen } = useCitizen();
  const router  = useRouter();

  const [amountStr, setAmountStr] = useState("100");
  const [loading,   setLoading]   = useState(false);
  const topPad = Platform.OS === "web" ? 67 : insets.top;

  const amount = useMemo(() => {
    const n = parseInt(amountStr, 10);
    return isNaN(n) || n < 1 ? 0 : n;
  }, [amountStr]);

  const price = useMemo(() => (amount > 0 ? calcPrice(amount) : null), [amount]);

  const activeTierIdx = TIERS.findLastIndex(t => amount >= t.from);

  const styles = getStyles(colors);

  async function handleBuy() {
    if (!citizen) {
      Alert.alert("Нужна авторизация", "Зарегистрируйся как гражданин");
      return;
    }
    if (amount < 1) {
      Alert.alert("Ошибка", "Введи количество Дарминов (минимум 1)");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    Haptics.impactAsync(Haptics.ImpactFeedbackStyle.Medium);

    try {
      const domain     = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN ?? "";
      const successUrl = `https://${domain}/api/payments/success`;
      const cancelUrl  = `https://${domain}/api/payments/cancel`;

      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/payments/checkout-custom`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ citizenId: citizen.id, amount, successUrl, cancelUrl }),
      });

      const data = await res.json() as { url?: string; error?: string };

      if (!res.ok || !data.url) {
        Alert.alert("Ошибка", data.error ?? "Не удалось создать сессию оплаты");
        return;
      }

      const result = await WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync(data.url, {
        dismissButtonStyle: "close",
        presentationStyle: WebBrowser.WebBrowserPresentationStyle.PAGE_SHEET,
        toolbarColor: "#0D1B2A",
      });

      if (result.type === "dismiss" || result.type === "cancel") {
        await refreshCitizen();
        Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Success);
      }
    } catch {
      Alert.alert("Ошибка соединения", "Проверь интернет и попробуй снова");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}
      behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
    >
      <ScrollView
        style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: topPad }]}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 40 }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
      >
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.backBtn} onPress={() => router.back()} activeOpacity={0.75}>
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={22} color={colors.foreground} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {/* Header */}
        <View style={styles.headerSection}>
          <LogoBrand size={64} />
          <Text style={styles.title}>Казначейство</Text>
          <Text style={styles.subtitle}>1 Дармин = 1 € · прогрессивные скидки</Text>
          {citizen && (
            <View style={styles.balanceBadge}>
              <Feather name="clock" size={14} color={colors.primary} />
              <Text style={styles.balanceBadgeText}>Баланс: {citizen.balanceMinutes} Д</Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {!citizen && (
          <View style={styles.authWarn}>
            <Feather name="alert-circle" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.authWarnText}>Зарегистрируйся, чтобы купить Дармины</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Calculator card */}
        <View style={styles.calcCard}>
          {/* Input label */}
          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Количество Дарминов</Text>

          {/* Text input */}
          <TextInput
            style={[styles.amountInput, { color: colors.foreground, borderColor: colors.border }]}
            value={amountStr}
            onChangeText={setAmountStr}
            keyboardType="number-pad"
            placeholder="Например: 250"
            placeholderTextColor={colors.mutedForeground}
            selectTextOnFocus
          />

          {/* Quick-pick buttons */}
          <View style={styles.quickRow}>
            {QUICK_AMOUNTS.map(n => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={n}
                style={[
                  styles.quickBtn,
                  amount === n && { borderColor: colors.primary, backgroundColor: colors.goldGlow },
                ]}
                onPress={() => {
                  setAmountStr(String(n));
                  Haptics.selectionAsync();
                }}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.quickBtnText, amount === n && { color: colors.primary }]}>
                  {n >= 1000 ? `${n / 1000}k` : n}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>

          {/* Price breakdown */}
          {price && amount > 0 ? (
            <>
              <View style={styles.priceRow}>
                <View style={[styles.priceBox, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                  <Text style={styles.priceBoxLabel}>Базовая цена</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.priceBoxValue, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
                    {price.baseEur.toFixed(2)} €
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[
                  styles.priceBox,
                  price.discountPct > 0
                    ? { borderColor: "#22c55e55", backgroundColor: "#052e1680" }
                    : { borderColor: colors.border },
                ]}>
                  <Text style={styles.priceBoxLabel}>Скидка</Text>
                  <Text style={[
                    styles.priceBoxValue,
                    { color: price.discountPct > 0 ? "#4ade80" : colors.mutedForeground },
                  ]}>
                    {price.discountPct > 0 ? `−${price.discountPct}%` : "нет"}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.totalBox, { borderColor: colors.primary + "55", backgroundColor: colors.goldGlow }]}>
                <View>
                  <Text style={styles.totalLabel}>Итого к оплате</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.totalValue, { color: colors.primary }]}>
                    {price.totalEur.toFixed(2)} €
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ alignItems: "flex-end" }}>
                  <Text style={styles.totalLabel}>Цена за 1 Д</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.perDarminValue, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
                    {price.eurPerDarmin.toFixed(2)} €/Д
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.buyBtn, (!citizen || loading) && styles.buyBtnDisabled]}
                onPress={() => void handleBuy()}
                disabled={loading || !citizen}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primaryForeground} />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Feather name="credit-card" size={18} color={colors.primaryForeground} />
                    <Text style={[styles.buyBtnText, { color: colors.primaryForeground }]}>
                      Купить {amount} Д за {price.totalEur.toFixed(2)} €
                    </Text>
                  </>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </>
          ) : (
            <View style={[styles.emptyHint, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.emptyHintText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
                Введите количество Дарминов (минимум 1)
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* Discount tiers */}
        <View style={styles.tiersSection}>
          <View style={styles.tiersHeader}>
            <Feather name="zap" size={13} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
            <Text style={[styles.tiersTitle, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
              Прогрессивные скидки
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.tiersGrid}>
            {TIERS.map((tier, i) => {
              const active = i === activeTierIdx;
              return (
                <View
                  key={tier.from}
                  style={[
                    styles.tierBox,
                    { borderColor: active ? colors.primary : colors.border },
                    active && { backgroundColor: colors.goldGlow },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.tierLabel, active && { color: colors.primary }]}>
                    {tier.label}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[
                    styles.tierDiscount,
                    { color: tier.discount > 0 ? (active ? colors.primary : "#4ade80") : colors.mutedForeground },
                  ]}>
                    {tier.discount > 0 ? `скидка ${tier.discount}%` : "без скидки"}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Legal */}
        <View style={styles.legalSection}>
          <Feather name="shield" size={13} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
          <Text style={[styles.legalText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
            Оплата через Stripe (EUR). Дармины — внутриигровые баллы, неисключительная лицензия на использование в платформе TimeNation. Не являются электронными деньгами или ценными бумагами.
          </Text>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.webhookNote}>
          <Feather name="info" size={13} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
          <Text style={[styles.legalText, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
            После оплаты баланс обновится автоматически через Stripe Webhook. Если Дармины не зачислились — закрой и снова открой приложение.
          </Text>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

function getStyles(colors: ReturnType<typeof useColors>) {
  return StyleSheet.create({
    container:        { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: 20 },
    backBtn:          { width: 40, height: 40, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", marginBottom: 4 },
    headerSection:    { alignItems: "center", paddingVertical: 16, gap: 8 },
    title:            { fontSize: 26, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold", color: colors.foreground, letterSpacing: 0.5 },
    subtitle:         { fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular", color: colors.mutedForeground, textAlign: "center" },
    balanceBadge:     { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, backgroundColor: colors.goldGlow, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.primary, paddingHorizontal: 14, paddingVertical: 7, borderRadius: 20, marginTop: 4 },
    balanceBadgeText: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Inter_600SemiBold", color: colors.primary },
    authWarn:         { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 12, padding: 14, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.primary, marginBottom: 12 },
    authWarnText:     { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Inter_500Medium", color: colors.foreground, flex: 1 },
    calcCard:         { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 16, padding: 18, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, gap: 14, marginBottom: 16 },
    inputLabel:       { fontSize: 12, fontFamily: "Inter_500Medium", color: colors.mutedForeground, letterSpacing: 0.5 },
    amountInput:      { fontSize: 24, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold", borderWidth: 1.5, borderRadius: 10, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 12, backgroundColor: colors.background },
    quickRow:         { flexDirection: "row", gap: 8 },
    quickBtn:         { flex: 1, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: 8, paddingVertical: 8, alignItems: "center" },
    quickBtnText:     { fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "Inter_600SemiBold", color: colors.mutedForeground },
    priceRow:         { flexDirection: "row", gap: 10 },
    priceBox:         { flex: 1, borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, gap: 4 },
    priceBoxLabel:    { fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular", color: colors.mutedForeground },
    priceBoxValue:    { fontSize: 20, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold" },
    totalBox:         { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 12, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 14 },
    totalLabel:       { fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular", color: colors.mutedForeground, marginBottom: 2 },
    totalValue:       { fontSize: 28, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold" },
    perDarminValue:   { fontSize: 16, fontFamily: "Inter_600SemiBold" },
    buyBtn:           { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: 10, backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: 12, paddingVertical: 16 },
    buyBtnDisabled:   { opacity: 0.45 },
    buyBtnText:       { fontSize: 16, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold" },
    emptyHint:        { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 8, padding: 12, alignItems: "center" },
    emptyHintText:    { fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular" },
    tiersSection:     { marginBottom: 16, gap: 10 },
    tiersHeader:      { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 },
    tiersTitle:       { fontSize: 12, fontFamily: "Inter_500Medium", letterSpacing: 0.5 },
    tiersGrid:        { flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8 },
    tierBox:          { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 8, padding: 10, minWidth: "45%", flex: 1 },
    tierLabel:        { fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "Inter_600SemiBold", color: colors.foreground, marginBottom: 2 },
    tierDiscount:     { fontSize: 12, fontFamily: "Inter_700Bold" },
    legalSection:     { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 8, backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, marginBottom: 8 },
    webhookNote:      { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 8, padding: 12 },
    legalText:        { flex: 1, fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular", lineHeight: 17 },
  });
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/app/embassy/verify.tsx ---
/**
 * Proof-of-Location QR Scanner Screen
 * Route: /embassy/verify
 *
 * Flow:
 *  1. Camera opens → scans QR code (payload: { embassyId, token, lat, lng })
 *  2. Requests GPS via expo-location (Highest accuracy)
 *  3. POSTs { token, phoneLat, phoneLng } to /api/embassies/verify-location
 *  4. Shows success (node ONLINE) or error (too far / expired)
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, ActivityIndicator,
  Alert, Platform, Pressable,
} from "react-native";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from "expo-camera";
import * as Location from "expo-location";
import { useColors } from "@/hooks/useColors";

const BASE_URL = `https://${process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN}`;

type Phase = "scanning" | "verifying" | "success" | "error";

interface QrPayload { embassyId: number; token: string; lat: number; lng: number }
interface VerifyResult { success?: boolean; message?: string; error?: string; distanceM?: number; nodeStatus?: string }

export default function EmbassyVerifyScreen() {
  const colors      = useColors();
  const insets      = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const [phase,      setPhase]     = useState<Phase>("scanning");
  const [result,     setResult]    = useState<VerifyResult | null>(null);
  const [qrPayload,  setQrPayload] = useState<QrPayload | null>(null);
  const [distM,      setDistM]     = useState<number | null>(null);
  const scannedRef   = useRef(false); // debounce multiple scan events

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!permission?.granted) requestPermission();
  }, [permission, requestPermission]);

  const handleBarcodeScanned = async ({ data }: { data: string }) => {
    if (scannedRef.current || phase !== "scanning") return;
    scannedRef.current = true;

    let payload: QrPayload;
    try {
      payload = JSON.parse(data) as QrPayload;
      if (!payload.token || !payload.embassyId) throw new Error("bad payload");
    } catch {
      Alert.alert(
        "Неверный QR-код",
        "Этот QR-код не является токеном верификации ноды TimeNation.",
        [{ text: "Повторить", onPress: () => { scannedRef.current = false; } }],
      );
      return;
    }

    setQrPayload(payload);
    setPhase("verifying");

    try {
      // ── Step 1: get GPS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
      if (status !== "granted") {
        setPhase("error");
        setResult({ error: "Доступ к GPS не разрешён. Открой настройки и разреши геолокацию для TimeNation." });
        return;
      }

      const pos = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({
        accuracy: Location.Accuracy.BestForNavigation,
      });

      const { latitude: phoneLat, longitude: phoneLng, accuracy } = pos.coords;

      // ── Step 2: POST to backend ──────────────────────────────────────────────
      const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/embassies/verify-location`, {
        method:  "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body:    JSON.stringify({ token: payload.token, phoneLat, phoneLng }),
      });

      const data2 = await res.json() as VerifyResult;

      if (res.ok && data2.success) {
        setResult(data2);
        setDistM(accuracy ? Math.round(accuracy) : null);
        setPhase("success");
      } else {
        setResult(data2);
        setDistM(data2.distanceM ?? null);
        setPhase("error");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setPhase("error");
      setResult({ error: "Ошибка сети. Проверь интернет-соединение и попробуй снова." });
    }
  };

  const reset = () => {
    scannedRef.current = false;
    setPhase("scanning");
    setResult(null);
    setQrPayload(null);
    setDistM(null);
  };

  const topPad = Platform.OS === "web" ? 67 : insets.top + 8;

  // ── Permission not granted ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (!permission?.granted) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: topPad }]}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backBtn}>
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={22} color={colors.foreground} />
        </Pressable>
        <View style={styles.centerBox}>
          <Feather name="camera-off" size={48} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
          <Text style={[styles.permTitle, { color: colors.foreground }]}>Нужен доступ к камере</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.permSub, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
            Для сканирования QR-кода ноды TimeNation требуется разрешение на использование камеры.
          </Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]} onPress={requestPermission} activeOpacity={0.8}>
            <Text style={[styles.btnText, { color: colors.primaryForeground }]}>Разрешить камеру</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  // ── Scanning phase ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (phase === "scanning") {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: "#000", paddingTop: topPad }]}>
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backBtnDark} hitSlop={12}>
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={22} color="#fff" />
          </Pressable>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitleDark}>📡 Сканирование ноды</Text>
          <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
        </View>

        <CameraView
          style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
          facing="back"
          onBarcodeScanned={handleBarcodeScanned}
          barcodeScannerSettings={{ barcodeTypes: ["qr"] }}
        />

        {/* Overlay frame */}
        <View style={styles.scanOverlay} pointerEvents="none">
          <View style={styles.scanFrame}>
            {/* Corner markers */}
            <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTL]} />
            <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTR]} />
            <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBL]} />
            <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBR]} />
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.scanHint}>
            Наведи камеру на QR-код на экране ноды
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.scanSubhint}>Находись в радиусе 15 метров от оборудования</Text>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  // ── Verifying phase ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (phase === "verifying") {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: topPad }]}>
        <View style={styles.centerBox}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
          <Text style={[styles.permTitle, { color: colors.foreground }]}>Верификация…</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.permSub, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
            Получаем GPS-координаты и отправляем данные на сервер
          </Text>
          {qrPayload && (
            <Text style={[styles.embassyIdHint, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
              Посольство #{qrPayload.embassyId}
            </Text>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  // ── Success phase ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (phase === "success") {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: topPad }]}>
        <View style={styles.centerBox}>
          <View style={[styles.statusIcon, { backgroundColor: "rgba(34,197,94,0.12)", borderColor: "#22c55e" }]}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={48} color="#22c55e" />
          </View>
          <Text style={[styles.permTitle, { color: "#22c55e" }]}>Нода активирована!</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.permSub, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
            {result?.message ?? "Аптайм-майнинг Дарминов запущен"}
          </Text>

          <View style={[styles.detailCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
            <View style={styles.detailRow}>
              <Feather name="wifi" size={14} color="#22c55e" />
              <Text style={[styles.detailText, { color: colors.foreground }]}>Статус ноды: ONLINE</Text>
            </View>
            {qrPayload && (
              <View style={styles.detailRow}>
                <Feather name="map-pin" size={14} color={colors.primary} />
                <Text style={[styles.detailText, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
                  Посольство #{qrPayload.embassyId}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, marginTop: 8 }]}
            onPress={() => router.replace("/(tabs)/embassies")}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.btnText, { color: colors.primaryForeground }]}>К посольствам</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  // ── Error phase ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: topPad }]}>
      <View style={styles.centerBox}>
        <View style={[styles.statusIcon, { backgroundColor: "rgba(239,68,68,0.12)", borderColor: "#ef4444" }]}>
          <Feather name="alert-triangle" size={48} color="#ef4444" />
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.permTitle, { color: "#ef4444" }]}>Верификация не пройдена</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.permSub, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
          {result?.error ?? "Неизвестная ошибка"}
        </Text>

        {distM !== null && (
          <View style={[styles.detailCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: "#ef444466" }]}>
            <View style={styles.detailRow}>
              <Feather name="navigation" size={14} color="#ef4444" />
              <Text style={[styles.detailText, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
                Расстояние до ноды: ~{distM} м (допустимо ≤15 м)
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, marginTop: 8 }]}
          onPress={reset}
          activeOpacity={0.8}
        >
          <Feather name="camera" size={16} color={colors.primaryForeground} />
          <Text style={[styles.btnText, { color: colors.primaryForeground }]}>  Сканировать снова</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cancelLink} onPress={() => router.back()} activeOpacity={0.7}>
          <Text style={{ color: colors.mutedForeground, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Inter_400Regular" }}>Назад</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
