// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/AnimatedHourglass.tsx ---
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { Animated, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { useColors } from "@/hooks/useColors";

interface Props {
  active: boolean;
  size?: number;
}

export function AnimatedHourglass({ active, size = 32 }: Props) {
  const colors = useColors();
  const spin = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const glow = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (active) {
      const spinLoop = Animated.loop(
        Animated.sequence([
          Animated.timing(spin, { toValue: 1, duration: 1600, useNativeDriver: true }),
          Animated.timing(spin, { toValue: 1, duration: 300, useNativeDriver: true }),
          Animated.timing(spin, { toValue: 2, duration: 1600, useNativeDriver: true }),
          Animated.timing(spin, { toValue: 2, duration: 300, useNativeDriver: true }),
        ])
      );
      const glowLoop = Animated.loop(
        Animated.sequence([
          Animated.timing(glow, { toValue: 1, duration: 900, useNativeDriver: true }),
          Animated.timing(glow, { toValue: 0, duration: 900, useNativeDriver: true }),
        ])
      );
      spinLoop.start();
      glowLoop.start();
      return () => {
        spinLoop.stop();
        glowLoop.stop();
        spin.setValue(0);
        glow.setValue(0);
      };
    }
  }, [active, spin, glow]);

  const rotate = spin.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1, 1, 2],
    outputRange: ["0deg", "180deg", "180deg", "360deg"],
  });

  const glowOpacity = glow.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: [0.4, 1.0],
  });

  if (!active) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.wrapper, { width: size + 16, height: size + 16 }]}>
        <Feather name="clock" size={size} color={colors.mutedForeground} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        styles.wrapper,
        styles.activeRing,
        {
          width: size + 16,
          height: size + 16,
          borderRadius: (size + 16) / 2,
          borderColor: colors.primary,
          opacity: glowOpacity,
        },
      ]}
    >
      <Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ rotate }] }}>
        <Feather name="clock" size={size} color={colors.primary} />
      </Animated.View>
    </Animated.View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrapper: {
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },
  activeRing: {
    borderWidth: 2,
    backgroundColor: "rgba(201,168,76,0.12)",
  },
});

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/ChronopediaMap.native.tsx ---
import React, {
  forwardRef,
  useEffect,
  useImperativeHandle,
  useRef,
  useState,
  useCallback,
} from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "react-native";
import WebView, { type WebViewMessageEvent } from "react-native-webview";
import { LEAFLET_HTML } from "./chronopedia-map-html";

// ── Shared types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SlotMarker {
  id: number;
  geoLat: number;
  geoLng: number;
  locationName: string;
  timeMarker: string;
  eventCount: number;
  hasMyEvent: boolean;
}

export interface ChronopediaMapHandle {
  centerOn: (lat: number, lng: number, zoom?: number) => void;
}

export interface ChronopediaMapProps {
  slots: SlotMarker[];
  myId?: number;
  selectedSlotId: number | null;
  pendingLat?: number | null;
  pendingLng?: number | null;
  onSlotClick: (slotId: number) => void;
  onMapClick: (lat: number, lng: number) => void;
  onReady: () => void;
  style?: object;
}

// ── Component ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const ChronopediaMap = forwardRef<ChronopediaMapHandle, ChronopediaMapProps>(
  (props, ref) => {
    const { slots, myId, selectedSlotId, pendingLat, pendingLng, onSlotClick, onMapClick, onReady, style } = props;

    const webViewRef = useRef<WebView>(null);
    const [mapReady, setMapReady] = useState(false);

    useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
      centerOn: (lat, lng, zoom = 12) => {
        webViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(`centerOn(${lat},${lng},${zoom}); true;`);
      },
    }));

    // Push marker data + selection whenever they change
    useEffect(() => {
      if (!mapReady) return;
      const selId = selectedSlotId === null ? "null" : String(selectedSlotId);
      const js = `updateMarkers(${JSON.stringify(slots)}, ${myId ?? 0}, ${selId}); true;`;
      webViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(js);
    }, [mapReady, slots, myId, selectedSlotId]);

    // Show/hide pending drop marker
    useEffect(() => {
      if (!mapReady) return;
      const lat = pendingLat ?? "null";
      const lng = pendingLng ?? "null";
      webViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript(`setPending(${lat},${lng}); true;`);
    }, [mapReady, pendingLat, pendingLng]);

    const handleMessage = useCallback(
      (e: WebViewMessageEvent) => {
        try {
          const msg = JSON.parse(e.nativeEvent.data) as {
            type: string;
            slotId?: number;
            lat?: number;
            lng?: number;
          };
          if (msg.type === "ready") {
            setMapReady(true);
            onReady();
          } else if (msg.type === "slotClick") {
            onSlotClick(msg.slotId!);
          } else if (msg.type === "mapClick") {
            onMapClick(msg.lat!, msg.lng!);
          }
        } catch { /* ignore */ }
      },
      [onReady, onSlotClick, onMapClick]
    );

    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, style as object]}>
        <WebView
          ref={webViewRef}
          source={{ html: LEAFLET_HTML, baseUrl: "https://unpkg.com" }}
          style={styles.webView}
          javaScriptEnabled
          originWhitelist={["*"]}
          domStorageEnabled
          allowUniversalAccessFromFileURLs
          onMessage={handleMessage}
          onLoadEnd={() => {
            setTimeout(() => setMapReady((v) => { if (!v) { onReady(); return true; } return v; }), 1500);
          }}
          onError={() => { setMapReady(true); onReady(); }}
          scrollEnabled={false}
          bounces={false}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        />
        {!mapReady && (
          <View style={styles.loading}>
            <ActivityIndicator color="#00ff87" />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Загрузка карты…</Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

ChronopediaMap.displayName = "ChronopediaMap";

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, position: "relative" },
  webView: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#0f172a" },
  loading: {
    ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject,
    backgroundColor: "#0f172a",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    gap: 10,
  },
  loadingText: { fontSize: 13, color: "#64748b" },
});

export default ChronopediaMap;

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/ChronopediaMap.tsx ---
// Platform-specific bridge for TypeScript resolution.
// At runtime Metro uses ChronopediaMap.native.tsx (iOS/Android) or ChronopediaMap.web.tsx (browser).
// This file is only used by `tsc --noEmit` for type checking.
export { default } from "./ChronopediaMap.native";
export type { ChronopediaMapHandle, ChronopediaMapProps, SlotMarker } from "./ChronopediaMap.native";

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/ChronopediaMap.web.tsx ---
/**
 * Web platform version of ChronopediaMap.
 * Uses an iframe (srcdoc) with the same LEAFLET_HTML used in the native WebView.
 * This avoids all Leaflet CSS import issues: the iframe has its own document
 * where CSS is loaded synchronously before the map initialises.
 *
 * Communication:
 *   map → host : window.parent.postMessage   (already in LEAFLET_HTML)
 *   host → map : iframe.contentWindow.postMessage({ fn, args })
 *                received by the message listener inside LEAFLET_HTML
 */

import React, {
  forwardRef,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useImperativeHandle,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "react-native";
import { LEAFLET_HTML } from "./chronopedia-map-html";
import type {
  ChronopediaMapHandle,
  ChronopediaMapProps,
} from "./ChronopediaMap.native";

// ── Component ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const ChronopediaMap = forwardRef<ChronopediaMapHandle, ChronopediaMapProps>(
  (props, ref) => {
    const { slots, myId, selectedSlotId, pendingLat, pendingLng, style } = props;

    const containerRef = useRef<View>(null);
    const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement | null>(null);
    const [mapReady, setMapReady] = useState(false);

    // Stable refs so effects don't re-fire on every render
    const onSlotClickRef = useRef(props.onSlotClick);
    const onMapClickRef = useRef(props.onMapClick);
    const onReadyRef = useRef(props.onReady);
    useEffect(() => { onSlotClickRef.current = props.onSlotClick; }, [props.onSlotClick]);
    useEffect(() => { onMapClickRef.current = props.onMapClick; }, [props.onMapClick]);
    useEffect(() => { onReadyRef.current = props.onReady; }, [props.onReady]);

    // Send a command to the iframe map via postMessage
    const sendCmd = useCallback((fn: string, ...args: unknown[]) => {
      iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
        JSON.stringify({ fn, args }),
        "*"
      );
    }, []);

    useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
      centerOn: (lat, lng, zoom = 12) => sendCmd("centerOn", lat, lng, zoom),
    }));

    // Mount iframe once; set up postMessage listener
    useEffect(() => {
      const container = containerRef.current as unknown as HTMLElement | null;
      if (!container) return;

      const iframe = document.createElement("iframe");
      iframe.style.cssText =
        "position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;border:none;background:#0f172a;";
      iframe.srcdoc = LEAFLET_HTML;
      // No sandbox — trusted content that needs to load external scripts/tiles.
      iframeRef.current = iframe;
      container.appendChild(iframe);

      const handler = (e: MessageEvent) => {
        if (e.source !== iframeRef.current?.contentWindow) return;
        try {
          const msg = JSON.parse(e.data as string) as {
            type: string;
            slotId?: number;
            lat?: number;
            lng?: number;
          };
          if (msg.type === "ready") {
            setMapReady(true);
            onReadyRef.current();
          } else if (msg.type === "slotClick" && msg.slotId != null) {
            onSlotClickRef.current(msg.slotId);
          } else if (msg.type === "mapClick" && msg.lat != null && msg.lng != null) {
            onMapClickRef.current(msg.lat, msg.lng);
          }
        } catch { /* ignore malformed messages */ }
      };

      window.addEventListener("message", handler);

      // Fallback: mark ready after 4 s if the message never arrives
      const fallback = setTimeout(() => {
        setMapReady((prev) => {
          if (!prev) onReadyRef.current();
          return true;
        });
      }, 4000);

      return () => {
        clearTimeout(fallback);
        window.removeEventListener("message", handler);
        if (iframe.parentNode) iframe.parentNode.removeChild(iframe);
        iframeRef.current = null;
      };
    }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

    // Sync slot markers whenever data or selection changes
    useEffect(() => {
      if (!mapReady) return;
      sendCmd("updateMarkers", slots, myId ?? 0, selectedSlotId ?? null);
    }, [mapReady, slots, myId, selectedSlotId, sendCmd]);

    // Show / hide pending drop-pin
    useEffect(() => {
      if (!mapReady) return;
      sendCmd("setPending", pendingLat ?? null, pendingLng ?? null);
    }, [mapReady, pendingLat, pendingLng, sendCmd]);

    return (
      <View
        ref={containerRef}
        style={[styles.container, style as object]}
      >
        {!mapReady && (
          <View style={styles.loading}>
            <ActivityIndicator color="#00ff87" />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Загрузка карты…</Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

ChronopediaMap.displayName = "ChronopediaMap";

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    position: "relative" as const,
    minHeight: 200,
  },
  loading: {
    ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject,
    backgroundColor: "#0f172a",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    gap: 10,
    zIndex: 10,
  },
  loadingText: { fontSize: 13, color: "#64748b" },
});

export default ChronopediaMap;

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx ---
import React, { Component, ComponentType, PropsWithChildren } from "react";

import { ErrorFallback, ErrorFallbackProps } from "@/components/ErrorFallback";

export type ErrorBoundaryProps = PropsWithChildren<{
  FallbackComponent?: ComponentType<ErrorFallbackProps>;
  onError?: (error: Error, stackTrace: string) => void;
}>;

type ErrorBoundaryState = { error: Error | null };

/**
 * This is a special case for for using the class components. Error boundaries must be class components because React only provides error boundary functionality through lifecycle methods (componentDidCatch and getDerivedStateFromError) which are not available in functional components.
 * https://react.dev/reference/react/Component#catching-rendering-errors-with-an-error-boundary
 */
export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<
  ErrorBoundaryProps,
  ErrorBoundaryState
> {
  state: ErrorBoundaryState = { error: null };

  static defaultProps: {
    FallbackComponent: ComponentType<ErrorFallbackProps>;
  } = {
    FallbackComponent: ErrorFallback,
  };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): ErrorBoundaryState {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: { componentStack: string }): void {
    if (typeof this.props.onError === "function") {
      this.props.onError(error, info.componentStack);
    }
  }

  resetError = (): void => {
    this.setState({ error: null });
  };

  render() {
    const { FallbackComponent } = this.props;

    return this.state.error && FallbackComponent ? (
      <FallbackComponent
        error={this.state.error}
        resetError={this.resetError}
      />
    ) : (
      this.props.children
    );
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/ErrorFallback.tsx ---
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { reloadAppAsync } from "expo";
import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Modal,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { useColors } from "@/hooks/useColors";

export type ErrorFallbackProps = {
  error: Error;
  resetError: () => void;
};

export function ErrorFallback({ error, resetError }: ErrorFallbackProps) {
  const colors = useColors();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const [isModalVisible, setIsModalVisible] = useState(false);

  const handleRestart = async () => {
    try {
      await reloadAppAsync();
    } catch (restartError) {
      console.error("Failed to restart app:", restartError);
      resetError();
    }
  };

  const formatErrorDetails = (): string => {
    let details = `Error: ${error.message}\n\n`;
    if (error.stack) {
      details += `Stack Trace:\n${error.stack}`;
    }
    return details;
  };

  const monoFont = Platform.select({
    ios: "Menlo",
    android: "monospace",
    default: "monospace",
  });

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
      {__DEV__ ? (
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => setIsModalVisible(true)}
          accessibilityLabel="View error details"
          accessibilityRole="button"
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            styles.topButton,
            {
              top: insets.top + 16,
              backgroundColor: colors.card,
              opacity: pressed ? 0.8 : 1,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Feather name="alert-circle" size={20} color={colors.foreground} />
        </Pressable>
      ) : null}

      <View style={styles.content}>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
          Something went wrong
        </Text>

        <Text style={[styles.message, { color: colors.mutedForeground }]}>
          Please reload the app to continue.
        </Text>

        <Pressable
          onPress={handleRestart}
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            styles.button,
            {
              backgroundColor: colors.primary,
              opacity: pressed ? 0.9 : 1,
              transform: [{ scale: pressed ? 0.98 : 1 }],
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.buttonText,
              { color: colors.primaryForeground },
            ]}
          >
            Try Again
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {__DEV__ ? (
        <Modal
          visible={isModalVisible}
          animationType="slide"
          transparent={true}
          onRequestClose={() => setIsModalVisible(false)}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.modalContainer,
                { backgroundColor: colors.background },
              ]}
            >
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.modalHeader,
                  { borderBottomColor: colors.border },
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: colors.foreground }]}>
                  Error Details
                </Text>
                <Pressable
                  onPress={() => setIsModalVisible(false)}
                  accessibilityLabel="Close error details"
                  accessibilityRole="button"
                  style={({ pressed }) => [
                    styles.closeButton,
                    { opacity: pressed ? 0.6 : 1 },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Feather name="x" size={24} color={colors.foreground} />
                </Pressable>
              </View>

              <ScrollView
                style={styles.modalScrollView}
                contentContainerStyle={[
                  styles.modalScrollContent,
                  { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 16 },
                ]}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator
              >
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.errorContainer,
                    { backgroundColor: colors.card },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.errorText,
                      {
                        color: colors.foreground,
                        fontFamily: monoFont,
                      },
                    ]}
                    selectable
                  >
                    {formatErrorDetails()}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </ScrollView>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/KeyboardAwareScrollViewCompat.tsx ---
import {
  KeyboardAwareScrollView,
  KeyboardAwareScrollViewProps,
} from "react-native-keyboard-controller";
import { Platform, ScrollView, ScrollViewProps } from "react-native";

type Props = KeyboardAwareScrollViewProps & ScrollViewProps;

export function KeyboardAwareScrollViewCompat({
  children,
  keyboardShouldPersistTaps = "handled",
  ...props
}: Props) {
  if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    return (
      <ScrollView keyboardShouldPersistTaps={keyboardShouldPersistTaps} {...props}>
        {children}
      </ScrollView>
    );
  }
  return (
    <KeyboardAwareScrollView
      keyboardShouldPersistTaps={keyboardShouldPersistTaps}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </KeyboardAwareScrollView>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/mobile/components/chronopedia-map-html.ts ---
// Leaflet map HTML — shared between native WebView and web iframe renderers.
// Native: injected via source={{ html: LEAFLET_HTML }} into react-native-webview.
// Web: mounted as a srcdoc iframe via ChronopediaMap.web.tsx.
//
// Communication protocol:
//   OUT (map → host):  window.parent.postMessage / ReactNativeWebView.postMessage
//                      { type: 'ready' | 'slotClick' | 'mapClick', ... }
//   IN  (host → map):  injectJavaScript (native) OR iframe.contentWindow.postMessage
//                      { fn: string, args: unknown[] }  →  window[fn].apply(null, args)

export const LEAFLET_HTML = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0,maximum-scale=1.0,user-scalable=no">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.css"/>
<script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
<style>
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box;}
html,body,#map{width:100%;height:100%;background:#0f172a;}
.leaflet-control-attribution,.leaflet-control-zoom{display:none;}
.tn-tip{background:#1e293b!important;color:#f1f5f9!important;border:1px solid #334155!important;font-size:12px!important;padding:6px 10px!important;border-radius:8px!important;white-space:nowrap!important;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)!important;}
.tn-tip::before{display:none!important;}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="map"></div>
<script>
var map=L.map('map',{center:[20,10],zoom:2,zoomControl:false,attributionControl:false,worldCopyJump:true});
L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/dark_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{maxZoom:19}).addTo(map);
var markers={};
var selectedSlotId=null;
var pendingMarker=null;
function send(d){var m=JSON.stringify(d);if(window.ReactNativeWebView){window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(m);}else{try{window.parent.postMessage(m,'*');}catch(e){}}}
function makePendingIcon(){
  return L.divIcon({className:'',html:'<div style="width:44px;height:44px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;"><div style="width:22px;height:22px;background:#f59e0b;border:3px solid #fff;border-radius:50%;box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(245,158,11,0.35),0 3px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);"></div></div>',iconSize:[44,44],iconAnchor:[22,22]});
}
function makeIcon(count,isMine,isSelected){
  var bg=isSelected?'#f59e0b':isMine?'#00ff87':'#60a5fa';
  var label=count>1?String(count):'\u25cf';
  return L.divIcon({className:'',html:'<div style="width:36px;height:36px;background:'+bg+';border:2.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.9);border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#111;box-shadow:0 3px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);cursor:pointer;">'+label+'</div>',iconSize:[36,36],iconAnchor:[18,18]});
}
function escHtml(s){return String(s||'').replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'})[c]||c;});}
function updateMarkers(slots,myId,selId){
  selectedSlotId=(selId!==undefined)?selId:selectedSlotId;
  Object.values(markers).forEach(function(m){m.remove();});
  markers={};
  (slots||[]).forEach(function(s){
    var isMine=!!s.hasMyEvent;
    var isSel=s.id===selectedSlotId;
    var m=L.marker([s.geoLat,s.geoLng],{icon:makeIcon(s.eventCount||0,isMine,isSel)}).addTo(map);
    m.bindTooltip('<b>'+escHtml(s.locationName)+'</b><br><small>'+escHtml(s.timeMarker)+'</small><br>'+(s.eventCount||0)+' \u043c\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438'+(s.eventCount===1?'\u0435':'\u0439'),{className:'tn-tip',direction:'top'});
    m.on('click',function(){send({type:'slotClick',slotId:s.id});});
    markers[s.id]=m;
    if(isSel){map.setView([s.geoLat,s.geoLng],13,{animate:true});setTimeout(function(){m.openTooltip();},300);}
  });
}
function normLng(v){return(((v%360)+540)%360)-180;}
function setPending(lat,lng){
  if(pendingMarker){pendingMarker.remove();pendingMarker=null;}
  if(lat==null||lng==null){return;}
  pendingMarker=L.marker([lat,lng],{icon:makePendingIcon(),zIndexOffset:1000}).addTo(map);
  var z=map.getZoom();
  map.setView([lat,lng],z<8?8:z,{animate:true});
}
function centerOn(lat,lng,z){map.setView([lat,lng],z||12,{animate:true});}
function clearSelection(){selectedSlotId=null;}
map.on('click',function(e){send({type:'mapClick',lat:parseFloat(e.latlng.lat.toFixed(6)),lng:parseFloat(normLng(e.latlng.lng).toFixed(6))});});
window.updateMarkers=updateMarkers;
window.setPending=setPending;
window.centerOn=centerOn;
window.clearSelection=clearSelection;
// Receive commands from parent iframe (web environment: host → iframe via postMessage)
window.addEventListener('message',function(e){
  try{
    var cmd=JSON.parse(typeof e.data==='string'?e.data:JSON.stringify(e.data));
    if(cmd&&cmd.fn&&typeof window[cmd.fn]==='function'){window[cmd.fn].apply(null,cmd.args||[]);}
  }catch(x){}
});
setTimeout(function(){send({type:'ready'});},400);
</script>
</body>
</html>`;

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/components/Layout.tsx ---
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { Activity, Tv, Users, History, ShoppingCart, UserPlus, LogOut, RefreshCw, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { SystemClock } from "./SystemClock";
import { useCitizen } from "@/context/CitizenContext";

function CitizenWidget() {
  const { citizen, setCitizen, refreshCitizen } = useCitizen();
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleLogin(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const val = input.trim();
    if (!val) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const byId = /^\d+$/.test(val);
      const url = byId
        ? `/api/citizens/${val}`
        : `/api/citizens/by-phone/${encodeURIComponent(val)}`;
      const res = await fetch(url);
      if (!res.ok) { setError("Гражданин не найден"); setLoading(false); return; }
      const citizenData = await res.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (!citizenData || !citizenData["id"]) { setError("Гражданин не найден"); setLoading(false); return; }
      setCitizen(citizenData as unknown as Parameters<typeof setCitizen>[0]);
      setInput("");
    } catch {
      setError("Ошибка соединения");
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  if (citizen) {
    return (
      <div className="px-3 py-3 border-t border-border space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground truncate">{citizen.name}</p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-primary font-mono">{(citizen.balanceMinutes as number).toFixed(2)} Д · #{citizen.id}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-1 shrink-0">
            <button
              onClick={() => void refreshCitizen()}
              className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground p-1 rounded-sm transition-colors"
              title="Обновить"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-3 h-3" />
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setCitizen(null)}
              className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive p-1 rounded-sm transition-colors"
              title="Выйти"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3 h-3" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-[9px] text-muted-foreground font-mono truncate">{citizen.rank}</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="px-3 py-3 border-t border-border">
      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground font-mono mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wider">Войти</p>
      <form onSubmit={(e) => void handleLogin(e)} className="space-y-1.5">
        <input
          value={input}
          onChange={e => { setInput(e.target.value); setError(null); }}
          placeholder="ID или телефон"
          className="w-full bg-background border border-border rounded-sm px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/60"
        />
        {error && <p className="text-[10px] text-destructive font-mono">{error}</p>}
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading || !input.trim()}
          className="w-full bg-primary/90 hover:bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-sm py-1 text-xs font-mono transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? "…" : "Войти"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

  return (

          }}
        />
        <div className="p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto min-h-full">
          {children}
        </div>

  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/components/SignaturePad.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import SignaturePadLib from "signature_pad";

interface SignaturePadProps {
  onChange: (dataUrl: string | null) => void;
}

export function SignaturePad({ onChange }: SignaturePadProps) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const padRef = useRef<SignaturePadLib | null>(null);
  const onChangeRef = useRef(onChange);
  onChangeRef.current = onChange;
  const [hasSignature, setHasSignature] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const ratio = Math.max(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1);
    canvas.width = canvas.offsetWidth * ratio;
    canvas.height = canvas.offsetHeight * ratio;
    canvas.getContext("2d")?.scale(ratio, ratio);

    const pad = new SignaturePadLib(canvas, {
      penColor: "rgb(10, 30, 80)",
      minWidth: 1,
      maxWidth: 3,
      backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0)",
    });
    padRef.current = pad;

    const onEnd = () => {
      const empty = pad.isEmpty();
      setHasSignature(!empty);
      onChangeRef.current(empty ? null : pad.toDataURL("image/png"));
    };
    pad.addEventListener("endStroke", onEnd);

    return () => {
      pad.removeEventListener("endStroke", onEnd);
      pad.off();
    };
  }, []);

  function handleClear() {
    padRef.current?.clear();
    setHasSignature(false);
    onChangeRef.current(null);
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative border-2 border-border rounded-sm overflow-hidden bg-zinc-950" style={{ height: 140 }}>
      <canvas
        ref={canvasRef}
        style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%", cursor: "crosshair", touchAction: "none", display: "block" }}
      />
      {!hasSignature && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground/50 font-mono text-sm italic select-none">Распишитесь здесь…</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {hasSignature && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleClear}
          className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-secondary/80 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded border border-border transition-colors"
        >
          Очистить
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/components/SystemClock.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function SystemClock() {
  const [localTime, setLocalTime] = useState("--:--:--");
  const [utcTime, setUtcTime] = useState("--:--:--");
  const [synced, setSynced] = useState(false);
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0); // server clock offset in ms

  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = async () => {
      try {
        const before = Date.now();
        const res = await fetch("/api/system/time");
        const after = Date.now();
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = (await res.json()) as { ts: number };
          const rtt = (after - before) / 2;
          setOffset(data.ts - after + rtt);
          setSynced(true);
        }
      } catch {
        setSynced(false);
      }
    };

    void sync();
    const syncTimer = setInterval(sync, 60_000);

    const tick = setInterval(() => {
      const nowLocal = new Date();
      const nowServer = new Date(Date.now() + offset);
      setLocalTime(nowLocal.toLocaleTimeString("ru-RU", { hour12: false }));
      setUtcTime(nowServer.toISOString().slice(11, 19));
    }, 1000);

    return () => {
      clearInterval(tick);
      clearInterval(syncTimer);
    };
  }, [offset]);

  return (
    <div className="px-4 pb-4 pt-2 border-t border-border mt-2 space-y-2">
      {/* Local time — primary */}
      <div>
        <div className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest font-mono mb-0.5">
          Local Time
        </div>
        <div className="text-xl font-mono font-bold text-foreground tabular-nums tracking-tight">
          {localTime}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* UTC time — secondary */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest font-mono mb-0.5">
            UTC Time
          </div>
          <div className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">
            {utcTime}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Sync indicator */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span
            className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full inline-block"
            style={{
              backgroundColor: synced ? "#22c55e" : "#f59e0b",
              boxShadow: synced ? "0 0 6px #22c55e88" : "0 0 6px #f59e0b88",
            }}
          />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase">
            {synced ? "SYNC" : "..."}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/context/CitizenContext.tsx ---
import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "timenation_citizen";

export interface CitizenProfile {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  balanceMinutes: number;
  totalGiven: number;
  totalReceived: number;
  status: string;
  meritScore: number;
  rank: string;
  lastActivity: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  walletAddress: string | null;
  walletVerified: number;
  ageVerified: number;
}

interface CitizenContextType {
  citizen: CitizenProfile | null;
  setCitizen: (c: CitizenProfile | null) => void;
  refreshCitizen: () => Promise<void>;
  isLoading: boolean;
}

const CitizenContext = createContext<CitizenContextType>({
  citizen: null,
  setCitizen: () => {},
  refreshCitizen: async () => {},
  isLoading: true,
});

export function CitizenProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [citizen, setCitizenState] = useState<CitizenProfile | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const stored = JSON.parse(raw) as CitizenProfile;
        setCitizenState(stored);
      }
    } catch {}
    setIsLoading(false);
  }, []);

  async function refreshCitizen() {
    if (!citizen) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/citizens/${citizen.id}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const updated = (await res.json()) as CitizenProfile;
        setCitizenState(updated);
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  function setCitizen(c: CitizenProfile | null) {
    setCitizenState(c);
    if (c) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(c));
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    }
  }

  return (
    <CitizenContext.Provider value={{ citizen, setCitizen, refreshCitizen, isLoading }}>
      {children}
    </CitizenContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useCitizen() {
  return useContext(CitizenContext);
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/hooks/use-mobile.tsx ---
import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange)
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/Citizens.tsx ---
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { useListCitizens, getListCitizensQueryKey } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";

const RANK_STYLES: Record<string, string> = {
  "Странник": "bg-zinc-500/20 text-zinc-300 border-zinc-500/30",
  "Созидатель": "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30",
  "Посол": "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30",
  "Хранитель Времени": "bg-violet-500/20 text-violet-300 border-violet-500/30",
  "Вершитель Эпох": "bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30",
  "Архитектор Таймлайна": "bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/30",
};

export function Citizens() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Реестр граждан | TimeNation";
  }, []);
  const { data: citizens, isLoading } = useListCitizens({
    query: { queryKey: getListCitizensQueryKey() },
  });

  const filtered = (citizens ?? []).filter(
    (c) =>
      !query ||
      c.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()) ||
      c.phone.includes(query)
  );

  const sorted = [...filtered].sort((a, b) => (b.meritScore ?? 0) - (a.meritScore ?? 0));

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Реестр граждан</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">
            Рейтинг по Merit Score · {citizens?.length ?? "..."} граждан
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="relative max-w-sm">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Поиск по имени или номеру..."
            className="pl-9 bg-secondary border-border font-mono text-sm"
          />
        </div>

        <Card className="bg-card border-border">
          <CardHeader className="pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">
              <span className="w-8">#</span>
              <span className="flex-1">Гражданин</span>
              <span className="w-24 text-right">Ранг</span>
              <span className="w-20 text-right">Merit</span>
              <span className="w-24 text-right">Баланс</span>
              <span className="w-20 text-right">Отдал</span>
              <span className="w-20 text-right">Принял</span>
            </div>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="p-0">
            {isLoading ? (
              <div className="p-4 space-y-2">
                {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <Skeleton key={i} className="h-10 bg-secondary" />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : sorted.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm py-8 text-center font-mono">
                Граждан не найдено
              </p>
            ) : (
              <div className="divide-y divide-border/50">
                {sorted.map((citizen, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={citizen.id}
                    className="flex items-center px-6 py-3 hover:bg-secondary/30 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="w-8 text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground">{idx + 1}</span>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{citizen.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground font-mono">{citizen.phone}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-24 text-right">
                      <Badge
                        variant="outline"
                        className={`text-[10px] font-mono truncate max-w-full ${RANK_STYLES[citizen.rank] ?? ""}`}
                      >
                        {citizen.rank}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-20 text-right font-mono text-sm text-primary">
                      {citizen.meritScore ?? 0}
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-24 text-right font-mono text-sm">
                      {(citizen.balanceMinutes as number).toFixed(2)} Д
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-20 text-right font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {(citizen.totalGiven as number).toFixed(2)} Д
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-20 text-right font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {(citizen.totalReceived as number).toFixed(2)} Д
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { useGetStats } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Activity, Clock, Users, Zap } from "lucide-react";

export function Home() {
  const { data: stats, isLoading } = useGetStats();

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Хронос-Консоль | TimeNation";
  }, []);

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Хронос-Консоль</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-2">Пульс экономики в реальном времени</p>
        </div>

        {isLoading || !stats ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {[1,2,3,4].map(i => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-32 bg-secondary" />)}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <StatCard title="Всего граждан" value={stats.totalCitizens} icon={Users} />

            <StatCard title="Транзакций" value={stats.totalTransactions} icon={Zap} />
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mt-8">

            </CardHeader>

                        <div className="text-sm font-medium">{tx.fromCitizenName} → {tx.toCitizenName}</div>

                      </div>
                      <div className="text-primary font-mono font-bold">+{(tx.minutesTransferred as number).toFixed(2)} Д</div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {stats.recentTransactions.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm py-4 text-center">Транзакций пока нет</div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          
          <div className="space-y-6">
             <Card className="bg-card border-border">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="text-lg font-medium">Топ — Отдают</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                {isLoading || !stats ? <Skeleton className="h-32 bg-secondary" /> : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {stats.topGivers.slice(0, 3).map((citizen, i) => (
                      <div key={citizen.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs w-4">{i + 1}.</span>
                          <span className="font-medium">{citizen.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground">{(citizen.totalGiven as number).toFixed(2)} Д</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>

            <Card className="bg-card border-border">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="text-lg font-medium">Топ — Принимают</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                {isLoading || !stats ? <Skeleton className="h-32 bg-secondary" /> : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {stats.topReceivers.slice(0, 3).map((citizen, i) => (
                      <div key={citizen.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs w-4">{i + 1}.</span>
                          <span className="font-medium">{citizen.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground">{(citizen.totalReceived as number).toFixed(2)} Д</span>
                      </div>

                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Layout>

function StatCard({ title, value, icon: Icon }: { title: string, value: string | number, icon: any }) {
  return (
    <Card className="bg-card border-border">
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">{title}</CardTitle>
        <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <div className="text-2xl font-bold font-mono text-primary">{value}</div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/LaborDashboard.tsx ---
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import {
  Shield, Clock, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle, ChevronDown, ChevronUp,
  Brain, Users, Settings, Loader2, Lock,
} from "lucide-react";

const API_BASE = "/api";

function govFetch(path: string, password: string, opts: RequestInit = {}) {
  return fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    ...opts,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Gov-Password": password,
      ...(opts.headers as Record<string, string> | undefined),
    },
  });
}

// ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface AuditLog {
  id: number;
  citizenId: number | null;
  taskDescription: string;
  estimatedMinutes: number;
  actualMinutes: number | null;
  deviationPercent: number | null;
  complexityLevel: string;
  justification: string;
  requiresCalibration: number;
  moderatedByName: string | null;
  moderatedAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
}

interface GovCitizen {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  citizenNumber: string | null;
  govRole: string;
  meritScore: number;
  rank: string;
}

// ── Sub-components ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function ComplexityBadge({ level }: { level: string }) {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    low: "bg-green-900/40 text-green-300 border-green-700",
    medium: "bg-yellow-900/40 text-yellow-300 border-yellow-700",
    high: "bg-red-900/40 text-red-300 border-red-700",
  };
  const labels: Record<string, string> = { low: "Низкая", medium: "Средняя", high: "Высокая" };
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs border font-medium ${map[level] ?? "bg-secondary text-muted-foreground"}`}>
      {labels[level] ?? level}
    </span>
  );
}

function RoleBadge({ role }: { role: string }) {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    citizen: "bg-secondary text-muted-foreground border-border",
    labor_officer: "bg-blue-900/40 text-blue-300 border-blue-700",
    gov_admin: "bg-purple-900/40 text-purple-300 border-purple-700",
  };
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    citizen: "Гражданин",
    labor_officer: "Сотрудник Минтруда",
    gov_admin: "Администратор",
  };
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs border font-medium ${map[role] ?? "bg-secondary"}`}>
      {labels[role] ?? role}
    </span>
  );
}

// ── Password gate ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function PasswordGate({ onUnlock }: { onUnlock: (pw: string) => void }) {
  const [pw, setPw] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const { toast } = useToast();

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/government/auth/verify`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ password: pw }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast({ title: "Доступ разрешён", description: "Добро пожаловать в кабинет Министерства Труда" });
        onUnlock(pw);
      } else {
        setError("Неверный пароль правительственного контура");
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Ошибка соединения с сервером");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[70vh]">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md border-yellow-800/40 bg-card">
        <CardHeader className="text-center space-y-3">
          <div className="flex justify-center">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-full bg-yellow-900/30 border border-yellow-700/40">
              <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-yellow-400" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <CardTitle className="text-xl">Правительственный контур</CardTitle>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Министерство Труда TimeNation · Только для уполномоченных сотрудников
          </p>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <form onSubmit={(e) => void handleSubmit(e)} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Правительственный пароль</label>
              <Input
                type="password"
                placeholder="Введите пароль доступа"
                value={pw}
                onChange={e => setPw(e.target.value)}
                className="bg-secondary border-border"
              />
            </div>
            {error && (
              <p className="text-sm text-red-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" /> {error}
              </p>
            )}
            <Button type="submit" className="w-full bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-500 text-black font-semibold" disabled={loading || !pw}>
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Shield className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
              Войти
            </Button>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Task Auditor tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function AuditorTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [taskDesc, setTaskDesc] = useState("");
  const [citizenId, setCitizenId] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<AuditLog | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  async function handleAudit() {
    if (!taskDesc.trim()) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setResult(null);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch("/government/audit/task", password, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          taskDescription: taskDesc,
          citizenId: citizenId ? parseInt(citizenId) : undefined,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json() as { log?: AuditLog; error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast({ title: "Ошибка", description: data.error ?? "Неизвестная ошибка", variant: "destructive" });
        return;
      }
      setResult(data.log ?? null);
      toast({ title: "Аудит выполнен", description: `Оценка: ${data.log?.estimatedMinutes ?? "?"} Д` });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка сети", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Card className="border-border">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base">
            <Brain className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
            Новый аудит задачи
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Описание задачи</label>
            <Textarea
              placeholder="Опишите задачу подробно: технология, объём работы, уровень сложности..."
              value={taskDesc}
              onChange={e => setTaskDesc(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-secondary border-border min-h-[120px] resize-none"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">ID гражданина (необязательно)</label>
            <Input
              type="number"
              placeholder="Например: 42"
              value={citizenId}
              onChange={e => setCitizenId(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-secondary border-border max-w-xs"
            />
          </div>
          <Button
            onClick={() => void handleAudit()}
            disabled={loading || !taskDesc.trim()}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-600"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Clock className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
            Оценить задачу ИИ-аудитором
          </Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {result && (
        <Card className="border-green-800/40 bg-green-950/20">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base text-green-300">
              <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              Результат аудита #{result.id}
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Оценка времени</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-300">{result.estimatedMinutes} Д</p>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Сложность</p>
                <div className="mt-1"><ComplexityBadge level={result.complexityLevel} /></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Обоснование ИИ</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-foreground/90 leading-relaxed">{result.justification}</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Audit Logs tab ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function LogsTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditLog[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [calibOnly, setCalibOnly] = useState(false);
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [actualInputs, setActualInputs] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const loadLogs = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch(
        `/government/audit/logs${calibOnly ? "?calibrationOnly=true" : ""}`,
        password
      );
      const data = await res.json() as { logs?: AuditLog[] };
      setLogs(data.logs ?? []);
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка загрузки логов", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [password, calibOnly, toast]);

  useEffect(() => { void loadLogs(); }, [loadLogs]);

  async function submitActual(log: AuditLog) {
    const val = parseFloat(actualInputs[log.id] ?? "");
    if (!val || val <= 0) { toast({ title: "Введите корректное время", variant: "destructive" }); return; }
    setSubmitting(log.id);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch(`/government/audit/logs/${log.id}`, password, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ actualMinutes: val }),
      });
      const data = await res.json() as { log?: AuditLog; error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) { toast({ title: "Ошибка", description: data.error, variant: "destructive" }); return; }
      toast({
        title: `Отклонение: ${data.log?.deviationPercent?.toFixed(1) ?? "?"}%`,
        description: data.log?.requiresCalibration ? "⚠️ Требует калибровки" : "✓ В норме",
      });
      void loadLogs();
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка сети", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={calibOnly}
              onChange={e => setCalibOnly(e.target.checked)}
              className="rounded"
            />
            Только требующие калибровки
          </label>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => void loadLogs()} disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : "Обновить"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
          <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
        </div>
      ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="border-dashed">
          <CardContent className="py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            Записей не найдено
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {logs.map(log => (
            <Card key={log.id} className={`border transition-colors ${log.requiresCalibration ? "border-orange-700/40 bg-orange-950/10" : "border-border"}`}>
              <div
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 cursor-pointer"
                onClick={() => setExpandedId(expandedId === log.id ? null : log.id)}
              >
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    {log.requiresCalibration === 1 && (
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    <span className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{log.taskDescription}</span>
                    <ComplexityBadge level={log.complexityLevel} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>#{log.id}</span>
                    <span>Оценка: <span className="text-foreground font-mono">{log.estimatedMinutes} Д</span></span>
                    {log.actualMinutes != null && (
                      <span>Факт: <span className="font-mono">{log.actualMinutes} Д</span></span>
                    )}
                    {log.deviationPercent != null && (
                      <span className={log.requiresCalibration ? "text-orange-400" : "text-green-400"}>
                        Δ {log.deviationPercent.toFixed(1)}%
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span>{new Date(log.createdAt).toLocaleString("ru")}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                {expandedId === log.id ? <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
              </div>

              {expandedId === log.id && (
                <div className="px-4 pb-4 border-t border-border pt-4 space-y-4">
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">{log.justification}</p>

                  {log.actualMinutes == null && (
                    <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
                      <div className="space-y-1 flex-1 max-w-xs">
                        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Фактическое время (мин / Д)</label>
                        <Input
                          type="number"
                          placeholder="0"
                          value={actualInputs[log.id] ?? ""}
                          onChange={e => setActualInputs(prev => ({ ...prev, [log.id]: e.target.value }))}
                          className="bg-secondary border-border h-8 text-sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => void submitActual(log)}
                        disabled={submitting === log.id}
                        className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-600 h-8"
                      >
                        {submitting === log.id ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : "Записать"}
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {log.moderatedAt && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      Промодерировано: {new Date(log.moderatedAt).toLocaleString("ru")}
                      {log.moderatedByName ? ` · ${log.moderatedByName}` : ""}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ── System Prompt tab ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function PromptTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [promptText, setPromptText] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await govFetch("/government/system-prompt", password);
        const data = await res.json() as { promptText?: string };
        setPromptText(data.promptText ?? "");
      } catch { /* ignore */ } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [password]);

  async function handleSave() {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch("/government/system-prompt", password, {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify({ promptText }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast({ title: "Промт обновлён", description: "Новые настройки ИИ-аудитора применены" });
      } else {
        const d = await res.json() as { error?: string };
        toast({ title: "Ошибка", description: d.error, variant: "destructive" });
      }
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка сети", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Card className="border-border">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base">
            <Settings className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400" />
            Системный промт ИИ-аудитора
          </CardTitle>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Инструкции, которые получает Gemini при каждом запросе на аудит. Изменения вступают в силу немедленно.
          </p>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> Загрузка…
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Textarea
                value={promptText}
                onChange={e => setPromptText(e.target.value)}
                className="bg-secondary border-border min-h-[400px] resize-none font-mono text-sm"
              />
              <Button
                onClick={() => void handleSave()}
                disabled={saving || promptText.length < 20}
                className="bg-purple-700 hover:bg-purple-600"
              >
                {saving ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Settings className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
                Сохранить промт
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Citizens tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function CitizensTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [citizens, setCitizens] = useState<GovCitizen[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await govFetch("/government/citizens", password);
        const data = await res.json() as { citizens?: GovCitizen[] };
        setCitizens(data.citizens ?? []);
      } catch { /* ignore */ } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [password]);

  async function setRole(citizenId: number, govRole: string) {
    setUpdating(citizenId);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch(`/government/citizens/${citizenId}/gov-role`, password, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ govRole }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setCitizens(prev => prev.map(c => c.id === citizenId ? { ...c, govRole } : c));
        toast({ title: "Роль обновлена" });
      }
    } catch { /* ignore */ } finally {
      setUpdating(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center py-12">
          <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-1">
          {citizens.map(c => (
            <div key={c.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-secondary/50 hover:bg-secondary transition-colors">
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium">{c.name}</span>
                  {c.citizenNumber && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">#{c.citizenNumber}</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                  <RoleBadge role={c.govRole} />
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.rank} · {c.meritScore.toFixed(0)} Merit</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <select
                value={c.govRole}
                disabled={updating === c.id}
                onChange={e => void setRole(c.id, e.target.value)}
                className="text-xs bg-background border border-border rounded px-2 py-1 text-foreground"
              >
                <option value="citizen">Гражданин</option>
                <option value="labor_officer">Сотрудник Минтруда</option>
                <option value="gov_admin">Администратор</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
